"""F-I curves and microcircuit spectra; biological reference acceptance is separate."""

import numpy as np
from nova.neuron import AdExPopulation
from nova.brain import Microcircuit, spectral_metrics
from nova.perception import PredictiveHierarchy
from experiments.common import write_report


def main():
    rows = []
    for seed in range(5):
        for current in (0.0, 200.0, 400.0, 600.0):
            model = AdExPopulation(20, seed=seed, backend="rust")
            counts = np.zeros(20)
            for _ in range(2000):
                counts += model.step((current, 0.0), dt=0.1)[0]
            rows.append(
                dict(
                    seed=seed,
                    condition="fi",
                    current_pa=current,
                    rate_hz=float(counts.mean() * 5),
                )
            )
        circuit = Microcircuit(100, seed=seed, backend="rust")
        spikes = []
        for _ in range(5000):
            spikes.append(circuit.step()[0])
        metrics = spectral_metrics(spikes, 0.1)
        hierarchy = PredictiveHierarchy(seed=seed)
        _, energy = hierarchy.infer(np.ones(8))
        rows.append(
            dict(
                seed=seed,
                condition="microcircuit",
                dale=circuit.check_dale(),
                prediction_energy_before=energy[0],
                prediction_energy_after=energy[-1],
                **metrics,
            )
        )
    write_report(
        7,
        rows,
        "AdEx Python/Rust com dois compartimentos; curvas F–I (200 ms por corrente); microcircuito E/PV/SST/VIP esparso (500 ms); relaxamento preditivo. Cinco sementes.",
        "Modelos N0/N1/N2, áudio log-mel em fluxo, visão foveal, texto, slots e fusão têm testes. Não há registros Allen/Blue Brain fornecidos nem ajuste de parâmetros contra eles. Banda gama é medida, não garantida; estes números não constituem validação biológica. Comparador de referências e importador de conectoma CSV implementados. Escala cluster e hardware neuromórfico não validados.",
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: experiments/08_cognition.py
========================================================================================
from time import perf_counter
import numpy as np
from scipy.special import softmax
from nova.reasoning import (
    grid_search,
    TemperatureCalibrator,
    expected_calibration_error,
)
from nova.runtime import CognitiveRuntime, RuntimeConfig
from experiments.common import write_report


def main():
    rows = []
    for seed in range(5):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        grids = []
        for _ in range(20):
            grid = rng.uniform(size=(12, 12)) < 0.2
            grid[0, :] = False
            grid[:, -1] = False
            grids.append(grid)
        for budget in (1, 32, 128, 256):
            start = perf_counter()
            solved = sum(
                grid_search(g, (0, 0), (11, 11), budget).verified for g in grids
            )
            rows.append(
                dict(
                    seed=seed,
                    condition="search",
                    budget=budget,
                    success=solved / len(grids),
                    seconds=perf_counter() - start,
                )
            )
        true_logits = rng.normal(size=(4000, 3))
        prob = softmax(true_logits, axis=1)
        labels = np.array([rng.choice(3, p=p) for p in prob])
        logits = true_logits * 3
        cal = TemperatureCalibrator().fit(logits[:2000], labels[:2000])
        before = expected_calibration_error(
            softmax(logits[2000:], axis=1), labels[2000:]
        )
        after = expected_calibration_error(cal.predict(logits[2000:]), labels[2000:])
        runtime = CognitiveRuntime(RuntimeConfig(seed=seed))
        for _ in range(100):
            runtime.tick()
        latencies = [m["latency_ms"] for m in runtime.metrics]
        rows.append(
            dict(
                seed=seed,
                condition="calibration_idle",
                ece_before=before,
                ece_after=after,
                temperature=cal.temperature,
                idle_latent_norm=float(np.linalg.norm(runtime.z)),
                tick_p95_ms=float(np.quantile(latencies, 0.95)),
            )
        )
    write_report(
        8,
        rows,
        "Busca BFS com verificador exato em 20 grids/seed; quatro orçamentos. Calibração por temperatura em partição separada e avaliação held-out. Ciclo ocioso e checkpoint determinístico testados.",
        "Mais expansões melhoram sucesso neste domínio finito, não provam raciocínio geral. O runtime integrado é um demonstrador com encoder inicial aleatório, a substituir por pesos treinados. Busca/neuromodulação opt-in. Latência é medida, não uma garantia de tempo real. Curiosidade ignora erro estacionário; ablações comportamentais amplas permanecem abertas.",
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: experiments/09_identity_fidelity.py
========================================================================================
"""Synthetic consent fixtures only. No real person's data or consent is invented."""

import json
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from nova.identity import IdentityStore
from nova.values import SIMULATION_LABEL
from experiments.common import write_report


def main():
    rows = []
    for seed in range(5):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            subject = f"fictional-{seed}"
            consent = root / "consent.json"
            consent.write_text(
                json.dumps(
                    dict(
                        subject_id=subject,
                        granted=True,
                        authority="synthetic benchmark fixture",
                        granted_at="2020-01-01T00:00:00Z",
                        purposes=["memory", "style"],
                        authorized_principals=["test"],
                    )
                )
            )
            store = IdentityStore(
                root / "vault", consent, subject, "test", bytes([seed + 1]) * 32
            )
            for i in range(10):
                store.ingest(
                    dict(
                        subject_id=subject,
                        id=f"doc-{i}",
                        kind="diary",
                        rights="synthetic",
                        timestamp=f"2020-{i + 1:02d}-01T00:00:00Z",
                        content=f"Fictional episode number {i}.",
                        facts=[{"predicate": f"fact-{i}", "value": f"value-{i}"}],
                        events=[{"content": f"episode-{i}"}],
                    )
                )
            store.review()
            answers = [store.answer(f"fact-{i}") for i in range(10)]
            accuracy = np.mean(
                [f"value-{i}" in answer for i, answer in enumerate(answers)]
            )
            citations = np.mean(
                [f"fonte: doc-{i}" in answer for i, answer in enumerate(answers)]
            )
            unknown = [store.answer(f"unknown-{i}") for i in range(10)]
            fabricated = np.mean(["Não sei" not in answer for answer in unknown])
            label_rate = np.mean([SIMULATION_LABEL in a for a in answers + unknown])
            receipt = store.delete_source("doc-0")
            store.review()
            deletion = "value-0" not in store.answer("fact-0") and receipt["verified"]
            erased = store.erase()["verified"]
            rows.append(
                dict(
                    seed=seed,
                    factual_accuracy=accuracy,
                    source_accuracy=citations,
                    fabrication_rate=fabricated,
                    label_rate=label_rate,
                    source_deleted=deletion,
                    profile_erased=erased,
                )
            )
    write_report(
        9,
        rows,
        "Apenas perfis fictícios com consentimento de fixture: dez fatos e dez perguntas sem resposta por semente; proveniência, rótulo, apagamento por fonte e perfil completo.",
        "É uma avaliação de armazenamento/recuperação estruturada, não teste held-out de compreensão natural ou fidelidade de uma pessoa. Estilo é um perfil estatístico; naturalidade/voz/LoRA não validadas. OCR/ASR/LLM são adaptadores opcionais que requerem dependências e pesos locais. Exclusão cobre o cofre ativo e estado derivado; cópias externas/backups não estão sob controle desta biblioteca. Minimização remove participantes declarados e contatos reconhecidos, não garante descoberta de todos os terceiros em texto livre.",
    )
    assert all(
        r["source_accuracy"] >= 0.95
        and r["fabrication_rate"] == 0
        and r["source_deleted"]
        and r["profile_erased"]
        for r in rows
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: experiments/10_adaptive_capacity.py
========================================================================================
"""Predeclared saturation/revisit experiment. Not executed during this revision."""

import argparse
import numpy as np
from nova.continual import ProtectedLinear, AdaptiveSubspaceLinear, AdaptiveConfig
from experiments.common import write_report


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--tasks", type=int, default=12)
    parser.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=200)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.tasks < 1 or args.steps < 1:
        parser.error("tasks and steps must be positive")
    rows = []
    for seed in range(5):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        tasks = []
        for _ in range(args.tasks):
            q = np.linalg.qr(rng.normal(size=(16, 16)))[0]
            x = rng.normal(size=(64, 8)) @ q[:, :8].T
            tasks.append((x, x @ rng.normal(size=(16, 4))))
        for policy in ("baseline", "strict", "relax", "expand", "hybrid"):
            model = (
                ProtectedLinear(16, 4, seed=seed)
                if policy == "baseline"
                else AdaptiveSubspaceLinear(
                    16,
                    4,
                    seed=seed,
                    config=AdaptiveConfig(
                        policy=policy, max_features=64, growth_step=8
                    ),
                )
            )
            historical = []
            for task, (x, y) in enumerate(tasks):
                version = 0 if policy == "baseline" else model.feature_version
                encode = lambda values, v: (
                    values if policy == "baseline" else model.encode(values, v)
                )
                features = encode(x, version)
                for _ in range(args.steps):
                    model.step(features, y, 0.05)
                reference = model(features).copy()
                historical.append((x, y, version, reference))
                if policy == "baseline":
                    model.consolidate(features)
                else:
                    # First task is an explicit anchor; later capacity must respect it.
                    model.consolidate(features, anchor=task == 0)
                    model.age(20.0, consolidation=0.1)
                    model.manage_capacity()
                stored_errors = [
                    float(np.mean((model(encode(px, pv)) - py) ** 2))
                    for px, py, pv, _ in historical
                ]
                retention = [
                    model.retention_error(encode(px, pv), ref)
                    for px, _, pv, ref in historical
                ]
                # This separate evaluation deliberately does NOT promise old-version mapping.
                current_errors = [
                    float(
                        np.mean(
                            (
                                model(
                                    encode(
                                        px,
                                        model.feature_version
                                        if policy != "baseline"
                                        else 0,
                                    )
                                )
                                - py
                            )
                            ** 2
                        )
                    )
                    for px, py, _, _ in historical
                ]
                metrics = {} if policy == "baseline" else model.capacity_metrics()
                rows.append(
                    dict(
                        seed=seed,
                        policy=policy,
                        task=task,
                        new_task_mse=stored_errors[-1],
                        mean_revisit_mse=float(np.mean(stored_errors)),
                        reencoded_mse=float(np.mean(current_errors)),
                        anchor_retention=retention[0],
                        max_retention_change=max(retention),
                        free_dims=model.free_dims,
                        width=model.in_features,
                        feature_version=version,
                        released_total=metrics.get("released_total", 0),
                    )
                )
    write_report(
        10,
        rows,
        "Cinco sementes; tarefas lineares conflitantes, primeira tarefa ancorada; baseline exato e quatro políticas adaptativas.",
        "Versões armazenadas e re-encoding atual são avaliados separadamente. Liberação pode esquecer; expansão tem teto de largura/bytes. Não existe capacidade infinita. Este script gera dados somente quando executado explicitamente.",
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: experiments/11_equilibrium_world.py
========================================================================================
"""Held-out EqProp world prediction and implicit-gradient audit, unrun by default."""

import argparse
import numpy as np
from nova.jepa.equilibrium import EquilibriumWorldModel, EquilibriumNetwork
from nova.environments import point_transitions
from experiments.common import write_report


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=400)
    parser.add_argument("--train-encoder", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.steps < 1:
        parser.error("steps must be positive")
    rows = []
    for seed in range(5):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        x, a, y, _ = point_transitions(seed=seed)
        tx, ta, ty, _ = point_transitions(512, seed + 500)
        model = EquilibriumWorldModel(12, 8, 2, seed=seed)
        model.fit_normalizer(x)
        updates = 0
        for _ in range(args.steps):
            idx = rng.integers(len(x), size=32)
            result = model.learn_transition(
                x[idx], a[idx], y[idx], lr=0.01, train_encoder=args.train_encoder
            )
            updates += result["updated"]
        model.freeze_encoder()
        z = model.encode(tx)
        target = model.target.predict(model._normalize(ty))
        prediction = model.predict(z, ta)
        train_target = model.target.predict(model._normalize(y))
        std = float(z.std(0).mean())
        mse = float(np.mean((prediction - target) ** 2))
        baseline = float(np.mean((target - train_target.mean(0)) ** 2))
        # Analytic implicit reference, not autograd and not a finite-difference target.
        network = EquilibriumNetwork(3, 2, seed=seed)
        u = rng.normal(size=(8, 3))
        desired = rng.normal(0, 0.2, (8, 2))
        free = network.relax(u)
        reference = []
        for state, target_value in zip(free.state, desired):
            force = np.zeros(len(state))
            force[-2:] = state[-2:] - target_value
            hessian = np.diag(1 / (1 - state * state)) - network.W
            reference.append(np.linalg.solve(hessian, force) @ network.B.T)
        result = network.learn(u, desired, lr=0.0)
        reference = np.asarray(reference)
        estimate = result["input_gradient"]
        cosine = float(
            np.sum(reference * estimate)
            / max(np.linalg.norm(reference) * np.linalg.norm(estimate), 1e-12)
        )
        rows.append(
            dict(
                seed=seed,
                steps=args.steps,
                encoder_trained=args.train_encoder,
                updates=updates,
                latent_mse=mse,
                mean_baseline_mse=baseline,
                latent_std=std,
                eqprop_input_gradient_cosine=cosine,
                noncollapsed=std >= 0.05,
                prediction_better_than_mean=mse < baseline,
            )
        )
    write_report(
        11,
        rows,
        "EqProp não linear com alvo EMA, erro held-out sob distratores e referência implícita de gradiente; cinco sementes.",
        "Falhas em previsão, convergência ou anti-colapso devem permanecer no relatório. Evitar autograd não prova menor custo/energia nem valida o circuito AdEx. O modo --train-encoder é exclusivamente offline, antes de proteger coordenadas.",
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: experiments/12_energy_planning.py
========================================================================================
"""Latent dimension vs. action-space search: CEM and adjoint energy planning."""

from time import perf_counter
import numpy as np
from nova.planning import LatentPlanner
from nova.planning.energy import EnergyPlanner, EnergyPlannerConfig
from experiments.common import write_report


class LinearDynamics:
    def __init__(self, dimension, seed):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.dimension = dimension
        self.decay = np.linspace(0.85, 0.95, dimension)
        self.B = rng.normal(0, 0.2 / np.sqrt(dimension), (dimension, 2))

    def predict(self, z, a):
        return z * self.decay + a @ self.B.T

    def linearize(self, z, a):
        return (
            self.predict(z, a),
            np.broadcast_to(
                np.diag(self.decay), (len(z), self.dimension, self.dimension)
            ),
            np.broadcast_to(self.B, (len(z), self.dimension, 2)),
        )


def main():
    rows = []
    for seed in range(5):
        for dimension in (8, 32, 64, 128):
            model = LinearDynamics(dimension, seed)
            z = np.array([1.0, -1.0]) @ model.B.T
            for horizon in (6, 12):
                for solver in ("cem", "energy", "energy_sheaf"):
                    cost = lambda state: np.sum(state * state, axis=-1)
                    if solver == "cem":
                        planner = LatentPlanner(
                            model.predict, cost, 2, horizon, 128, 4, seed=seed
                        )
                    else:
                        config = EnergyPlannerConfig(
                            horizon=horizon,
                            iterations=20,
                            max_model_calls=horizon * 128 * 4,
                            action_penalty=0.0,
                            slew_penalty=0.0,
                            sheaf_weight=0.01 if solver == "energy_sheaf" else 0.0,
                        )
                        planner = EnergyPlanner(
                            model,
                            lambda state: (cost(state), 2 * state),
                            2,
                            dimension,
                            config=config,
                        )
                    start = perf_counter()
                    plan = planner.plan(z)
                    elapsed = (perf_counter() - start) * 1000
                    actions = np.vstack((plan.action, planner.warm_actions[:-1]))
                    state = z.copy()
                    environment_cost = 0.0
                    for action in actions:
                        state = model.predict(state, action)
                        environment_cost += float(cost(state))
                    rows.append(
                        dict(
                            seed=seed,
                            solver=solver,
                            latent_dim=dimension,
                            horizon=horizon,
                            search_variables=2 * horizon,
                            environment_cost=environment_cost,
                            solver_objective=plan.cost,
                            transition_evaluations=plan.diagnostics["model_calls"],
                            feasible=plan.diagnostics["feasible"],
                            latency_ms=elapsed,
                            iterations=plan.iterations,
                        )
                    )
    write_report(
        12,
        rows,
        "Cinco sementes; D=8/32/64/128, horizontes 6/12, ação bidimensional, CEM vs energia e ablação de feixe temporal.",
        "O custo de ambiente é recomputado sem o regularizador de feixe. Modelos lineares conhecidos isolam o escalonamento do solver; não demonstram generalização em cenas multimodais. Espaço de busca=H×action_dim em todos os métodos; contagens são avaliações de transição, não equivalência de FLOPs.",
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: experiments/common.py
========================================================================================
"""Machine-readable evidence, grouped conditions and reproducibility metadata."""

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from importlib.metadata import version, PackageNotFoundError
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import platform
import numpy as np
from scipy.stats import ttest_rel

GROUPS = {
    1: ("rank", "alpha"),
    3: ("dataset", "method"),
    5: ("batch", "input_dim", "output_dim", "dtype"),
    7: ("condition", "current_pa"),
    8: ("condition", "budget"),
    10: ("policy", "task"),
    11: ("encoder_trained",),
    12: ("latent_dim", "horizon", "solver"),
}


def source_digest():
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    files = sorted(
        [
            *Path("nova").rglob("*.py"),
            *Path("experiments").glob("*.py"),
            *Path("nova-core/src").glob("*.rs"),
        ]
    )
    for file in files:
        digest.update(str(file).encode())
        digest.update(file.read_bytes())
    return digest.hexdigest()


def aggregate(rows, keys):
    return {
        key: {
            "mean": float(np.mean(values)),
            "std": float(np.std(values, ddof=1)) if len(values) > 1 else 0.0,
        }
        for key in keys
        if key != "seed"
        if (values := [r[key] for r in rows if isinstance(r.get(key), (float, int))])
    }


def write_report(
    phase: int, rows: list[dict], summary: str, limitations: str = ""
) -> None:
    root = Path("reports")
    root.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    keys = sorted(set().union(*(r.keys() for r in rows)))
    with (root / f"phase{phase}.csv").open("w", newline="") as stream:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, keys)
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)
    group_keys = GROUPS.get(phase, ())
    conditions = {}
    for row in rows:
        label = (
            ", ".join(
                f"{key}={row.get(key, '')}"
                for key in group_keys
                if row.get(key, "") != ""
            )
            or "all seeds"
        )
        conditions.setdefault(label, []).append(row)
    grouped = {
        label: aggregate(group, [key for key in keys if key not in group_keys])
        for label, group in conditions.items()
    }
    packages = {}
    for package in ("numpy", "scipy", "torch", "pytest", "nova-native"):
        try:
            packages[package] = version(package)
        except PackageNotFoundError:
            packages[package] = "not installed"
    metadata = {
        "python": platform.python_version(),
        "platform": platform.platform(),
        "report_generated_at_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "packages": packages,
        "report_source_sha256": source_digest(),
        "observations": len(rows),
        "groups": grouped,
    }
    (root / f"phase{phase}.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(metadata, indent=2, allow_nan=False)
    )
    table = "\n".join(
        f"| {label} | {metric} | {stats['mean']:.6g} | {stats['std']:.6g} |"
        for label, metrics in grouped.items()
        for metric, stats in metrics.items()
    )
    (root / f"fase{phase}.md").write_text(
        f"# Fase {phase}\n\n{summary}\n\n| Condição | Métrica | Média | Desvio amostral |\n|---|---|---:|---:|\n{table}\n\n{limitations}\n\nDados: `phase{phase}.csv`; grupos não misturam métodos ou orçamentos.\n"
    )


def paired(a, b):
    a, b = np.asarray(a), np.asarray(b)
    if np.allclose(a, b):
        return 1.0
    return float(ttest_rel(a, b).pvalue)


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: experiments/plot_reports.py
========================================================================================
"""Generate publication/exportable figures from recorded results, never invented data."""

from pathlib import Path
import csv
import os

os.environ.setdefault("MPLCONFIGDIR", "/tmp/nova-matplotlib")
import matplotlib

matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np


def main():
    root = Path("reports")
    with (root / "phase1.csv").open() as f:
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(10, 4))
    for rank in (4, 8, 16, 32):
        data = [r for r in rows if int(r["rank"]) == rank]
        alpha = sorted({float(r["alpha"]) for r in data})
        means = [
            np.mean([float(r["mse_after"]) for r in data if float(r["alpha"]) == a])
            for a in alpha
        ]
        axes[0].plot(alpha, means, marker="o", label=f"rank={rank}")
    axes[0].set(xlabel="Overlap α", ylabel="New task MSE")
    axes[0].legend()
    axes[1].hist([float(r["eps_ret"]) for r in rows], bins=20)
    axes[1].set(xlabel="Retention error (float64)", ylabel="Conditions")
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(root / "phase1.png", dpi=160)
    plt.close(fig)
    if (root / "phase3.csv").exists():
        with (root / "phase3.csv").open() as f:
            rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
        datasets = list(dict.fromkeys(r["dataset"] for r in rows))
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            len(datasets), 2, figsize=(11, 4 * len(datasets)), squeeze=False
        )
        for row_axes, dataset in zip(axes, datasets):
            selected = [r for r in rows if r["dataset"] == dataset]
            methods = list(dict.fromkeys(r["method"] for r in selected))
            for ax, metric in zip(row_axes, ["acc", "fm"]):
                data = [
                    [float(r[metric]) for r in selected if r["method"] == m]
                    for m in methods
                ]
                ax.bar(
                    methods,
                    [np.mean(d) for d in data],
                    yerr=[np.std(d, ddof=1) for d in data],
                )
                ax.set_ylabel(metric)
                ax.set_title(dataset)
                ax.tick_params(axis="x", rotation=30)
        fig.tight_layout()
        fig.savefig(root / "phase3.png", dpi=160)
        plt.close(fig)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: experiments/refresh_reports.py
========================================================================================
"""Reformat existing observations without rerunning or inventing measurements."""

import csv
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from experiments.common import write_report


def parse(value):
    if value == "":
        return ""
    if value in ("True", "False"):
        return value == "True"
    try:
        return int(value)
    except ValueError:
        try:
            return float(value)
        except ValueError:
            return value


def main():
    for phase in range(1, 13):
        path = Path(f"reports/fase{phase}.md")
        if not path.exists():
            continue
        paragraphs = path.read_text().split("\n\n")
        summary = paragraphs[1]
        limitations = paragraphs[-2]
        with Path(f"reports/phase{phase}.csv").open() as stream:
            rows = [{k: parse(v) for k, v in r.items()} for r in csv.DictReader(stream)]
        if phase == 3:
            for row in rows:
                a = np.load(
                    f"reports/accuracy_{row['dataset']}_{row['method']}_{row['seed']}.npy",
                    allow_pickle=False,
                )
                row["fm"] = float(
                    np.mean([a[j:-1, j].max() - a[-1, j] for j in range(len(a) - 1)])
                )
        write_report(phase, rows, summary, limitations)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

//! Native CPU research kernels. Ternary coefficients use branches and add/subtract.
use numpy::{ndarray::Array2, PyArray2, PyReadonlyArray2};
use pyo3::{exceptions::PyValueError, prelude::*};
use rayon::prelude::*;

#[pyclass]
struct TernaryBlock {
    rows: usize,
    cols: usize,
    packed: Vec<u8>,
}

fn pack(weights: &[i8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, &'static str> {
    let mut packed = vec![0u8; weights.len().div_ceil(4)];
    for (i, &w) in weights.iter().enumerate() {
        let code = match w { 0 => 0, 1 => 1, -1 => 2, _ => return Err("weights must be -1, 0, 1") };
        packed[i / 4] |= code << (2 * (i % 4));
    }
    Ok(packed)
}

#[pymethods]
impl TernaryBlock {
    #[new]
    fn new(weights: Vec<i8>, rows: usize, cols: usize) -> PyResult<Self> {
        if rows == 0 || cols == 0 || rows.checked_mul(cols) != Some(weights.len()) {
            return Err(PyValueError::new_err("invalid dimensions"));
        }
        Ok(Self { rows, cols, packed: pack(&weights).map_err(PyValueError::new_err)? })
    }
    #[getter]
    fn nbytes(&self) -> usize { self.packed.len() }
    fn packed_bytes(&self) -> Vec<u8> { self.packed.clone() }
    fn matmul<'py>(&self, py: Python<'py>, x: PyReadonlyArray2<'py, f64>) -> PyResult<Bound<'py, PyArray2<f64>>> {
        let a = x.as_array();
        if a.ncols() != self.rows { return Err(PyValueError::new_err("input dimension mismatch")); }
        let batch = a.nrows();
        let input: Vec<f64> = a.iter().copied().collect();
        let mut output = vec![0.; batch * self.cols];
        py.allow_threads(|| {
            output.par_chunks_mut(self.cols).enumerate().for_each(|(n, out)| {
                for k in 0..self.rows {
                    let value = input[n*self.rows+k];
                    for (j, result) in out.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                        let i = k*self.cols+j;
                        match (self.packed[i/4] >> (2*(i%4))) & 3 {
                            1 => *result += value,
                            2 => *result -= value,
                            _ => (),
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
        });
        let result = Array2::from_shape_vec((batch,self.cols),output).map_err(|e|PyValueError::new_err(e.to_string()))?;
        Ok(PyArray2::from_owned_array(py,result))
    }
}

fn hadamard(values: &mut [f64]) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
    if !values.len().is_power_of_two() { return Err("length must be a nonzero power of two"); }
    let mut width=1;
    while width<values.len() {
        for base in (0..values.len()).step_by(2*width) {
            for j in 0..width {
                let a=values[base+j]; let b=values[base+j+width];
                values[base+j]=a+b; values[base+j+width]=a-b;
            }
        }
        width*=2;
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[pyfunction]
fn fast_hadamard_transform(mut values: Vec<f64>, normalize: bool) -> PyResult<Vec<f64>> {
    hadamard(&mut values).map_err(PyValueError::new_err)?;
    if normalize { let scale=(values.len() as f64).sqrt(); for x in &mut values { *x/=scale; } }
    Ok(values)
}

#[pyfunction]
fn kuramoto_step(py: Python<'_>, theta: Vec<f64>, omega: Vec<f64>, adjacency: Vec<f64>, lag: Vec<f64>, coupling: f64, dt: f64) -> PyResult<Vec<f64>> {
    let n=theta.len();
    if n==0 || omega.len()!=n || adjacency.len()!=n*n || lag.len()!=n*n || dt<=0. {
        return Err(PyValueError::new_err("invalid Kuramoto dimensions/dt"));
    }
    Ok(py.allow_threads(|| (0..n).into_par_iter().map(|i| {
        let sum: f64=(0..n).map(|j|adjacency[i*n+j]*(theta[j]-theta[i]-lag[i*n+j]).sin()).sum();
        (theta[i]+dt*(omega[i]+coupling*sum/n as f64)).rem_euclid(std::f64::consts::TAU)
    }).collect()))
}


#[pyfunction]
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
fn adex_step(py: Python<'_>, v: Vec<f64>, w: Vec<f64>, dendrite: Vec<f64>, refractory: Vec<f64>, basal: Vec<f64>, apical: Vec<f64>, adapt_a: Vec<f64>, adapt_b: Vec<f64>, tau: Vec<f64>, dt: f64, nmda: bool) -> PyResult<(Vec<f64>,Vec<f64>,Vec<f64>,Vec<f64>,Vec<bool>)> {
    let n=v.len();
    if n==0 || [w.len(),dendrite.len(),refractory.len(),basal.len(),apical.len(),adapt_a.len(),adapt_b.len(),tau.len()].iter().any(|&len|len!=n) || dt<=0. || dt>1. || tau.iter().any(|&t|t<=0.) {
        return Err(PyValueError::new_err("invalid AdEx arrays/dt (0 < dt <= 1 ms)"));
    }
    let results: Vec<(f64,f64,f64,f64,bool)>=py.allow_threads(|| (0..n).into_par_iter().map(|i| {
        let nmda_current=if nmda { 30./(1.+(-(dendrite[i]+40.)/5.).exp()) } else { 0. };
        let d=dendrite[i]+dt*(-5.*(dendrite[i]+70.)+2.*(v[i]-dendrite[i])+apical[i]+nmda_current)/100.;
        let mut new_w=w[i]+dt*(adapt_a[i]*(v[i]+70.)-w[i])/tau[i];
        let mut r=(refractory[i]-dt).max(0.);
        let mut new_v=if refractory[i]>0. { -58. } else {
            v[i]+dt*(-10.*(v[i]+70.)+20.*((v[i]+50.)/2.).min(10.).exp()-w[i]+2.*(dendrite[i]-v[i])+basal[i])/200.
        };
        let spike=new_v>=20.;
        if spike { new_v=-58.;new_w+=adapt_b[i];r=2.; }
        (new_v,new_w,d,r,spike)
    }).collect());
    Ok((results.iter().map(|r|r.0).collect(),results.iter().map(|r|r.1).collect(),results.iter().map(|r|r.2).collect(),results.iter().map(|r|r.3).collect(),results.iter().map(|r|r.4).collect()))
}

#[pyfunction]
fn cascade_rk4(py: Python<'_>, state: Vec<f64>, matrix: Vec<f64>, drive: Vec<f64>, reservoirs: usize, dt: f64) -> PyResult<Vec<f64>> {
    if reservoirs==0 || state.len()%reservoirs!=0 || drive.len()!=state.len()/reservoirs || matrix.len()!=reservoirs*reservoirs || dt<=0. {
        return Err(PyValueError::new_err("invalid cascade shapes/dt"));
    }
    Ok(py.allow_threads(|| {
        let mut result=state.clone();
        result.par_chunks_mut(reservoirs).enumerate().for_each(|(index,out)| {
            let s=&state[index*reservoirs..(index+1)*reservoirs];
            let f=|v:&[f64]| -> Vec<f64> {(0..reservoirs).map(|i| {
                let value:f64=(0..reservoirs).map(|j|matrix[i*reservoirs+j]*v[j]).sum();
                value+if i==0 {drive[index]} else {0.}
            }).collect()};
            let advance=|k:&[f64],factor:f64| -> Vec<f64> {s.iter().zip(k).map(|(v,d)|v+dt*factor*d).collect()};
            let k1=f(s);let k2=f(&advance(&k1,0.5));let k3=f(&advance(&k2,0.5));let k4=f(&advance(&k3,1.));
            for i in 0..reservoirs {out[i]=s[i]+dt*(k1[i]+2.*k2[i]+2.*k3[i]+k4[i])/6.;}
        });
        result
    }))
}

#[pymodule]
fn _native(m: &Bound<'_, PyModule>) -> PyResult<()> {
    m.add_class::<TernaryBlock>()?;
    m.add_function(wrap_pyfunction!(adex_step,m)?)?;
    m.add_function(wrap_pyfunction!(cascade_rk4,m)?)?;
    m.add_function(wrap_pyfunction!(fast_hadamard_transform,m)?)?;
    m.add_function(wrap_pyfunction!(kuramoto_step,m)?)?;
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn packing_and_invalid() { assert_eq!(pack(&[0,1,-1,0,1]).unwrap(),vec![36,1]); assert!(pack(&[2]).is_err()); }
    #[test]
    fn hadamard_involution() {
        let mut x=vec![1.,2.,3.,4.]; hadamard(&mut x).unwrap(); hadamard(&mut x).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(x,vec![4.,8.,12.,16.]); assert!(hadamard(&mut [1.,2.,3.]).is_err());
    }
}

"""NOVA: experimental continual learning and cognitive simulation components."""

__version__ = "0.3.0"

========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/bioma/brains.py

"""Developed mixed neural graph: rates, AdEx, HH and Boolean units actually step."""

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
from nova.neuron import AdExPopulation, HodgkinHuxleyCompartments
from nova.jepa.equilibrium import EquilibriumWorldModel
from nova.continual import AdaptiveSubspaceLinear, AdaptiveConfig
from .genetics import MorphogeneticCPPN
from .determinism import SplitMix64


class DevelopedBrain:
    def __init__(self, genome: MorphogeneticCPPN, sensors=12, actions=3):
        self.topology = genome.develop_brain()
        self.n = len(self.topology["types"])
        self.sensors, self.actions = sensors, actions
        rng = SplitMix64(genome.seed)
        self.sensory_projection = rng.uniform((sensors, self.n), -0.5, 0.5)
        self.motor_projection = rng.uniform((self.n, actions), -0.5, 0.5)
        self.latent_projection = rng.uniform((self.n, 8), -0.25, 0.25)
        self.activation = np.zeros(self.n)
        self.adex = AdExPopulation(self.n, seed=genome.seed)
        self.adex.types = self.topology["types"].copy()
        self.adex.tau = np.choose(self.adex.types, [200.0, 30.0, 250.0, 150.0])
        self.adex.a = np.choose(self.adex.types, [2.0, 0.0, 4.0, 2.0])
        self.adex.b = np.choose(self.adex.types, [40.0, 0.0, 80.0, 20.0])
        self.hh = {
            int(i): HodgkinHuxleyCompartments(1)
            for i in np.flatnonzero(self.topology["families"] == 2)
        }
        self.world = EquilibriumWorldModel(sensors, 8, actions, seed=genome.seed)
        self.world.freeze_encoder()
        self.memory = AdaptiveSubspaceLinear(
            8,
            8,
            seed=genome.seed,
            config=AdaptiveConfig(
                max_features=32, max_state_bytes=1024 * 1024, growth_step=8
            ),
        )
        self.previous_latent = None
        self.previous_action = np.zeros(actions)
        self.ticks = 0
        self.last_error = 0.0
        self.resource_readout = np.zeros(9)
        self.planning_weight = 0.0

    def step(self, sensory, modulator=0.0):
        sensory = np.asarray(sensory, dtype=float)
        if sensory.shape != (self.sensors,) or not np.isfinite(sensory).all():
            raise ValueError("invalid organism sensors")
        current = (
            self.activation @ self.topology["weights"]
            + sensory @ self.sensory_projection
        )
        rate = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(current)
        spikes, _ = self.adex.step((300 + 50 * np.tanh(current), 0.0), dt=0.1)
        output = rate.copy()
        families = self.topology["families"]
        output[families == 1] = spikes[families == 1]
        for index, neuron in self.hh.items():
            fired = False
            for _ in range(10):
                fired = (
                    bool(
                        neuron.step(
                            (10 + float(np.clip(current[index], -20, 20)), 0.0), dt=0.01
                        )[0][0]
                    )
                    or fired
                )
            output[index] = float(fired)
        for index in np.flatnonzero(families == 3):
            inputs = self.activation[self.topology["weights"][:, index] != 0] > 0.5
            gate = int(index) % 4
            output[index] = float(
                inputs.all()
                if gate == 0 and len(inputs)
                else inputs.any()
                if gate == 1
                else np.count_nonzero(inputs) % 2
                if gate == 2
                else not inputs.any()
            )
        self.activation = np.clip(0.9 * self.activation + 0.1 * output, 0, 1)
        latent = np.tanh(self.activation @ self.latent_projection)
        action = np.tanh((self.activation - 0.5) @ self.motor_projection)
        if self.previous_latent is not None:
            prediction = self.world.predict(
                self.previous_latent[None], self.previous_action[None]
            )[0]
            self.last_error = float(np.mean((prediction - latent) ** 2))
            self.world.learn_latent(
                self.previous_latent[None],
                self.previous_action[None],
                latent[None],
                lr=0.001,
            )
            self.memory.step(
                self.memory.encode(self.previous_latent[None]), latent[None], lr=0.001
            )
        resource_features = np.r_[latent, 1.0]
        residual = float(sensory[-1] - resource_features @ self.resource_readout)
        self.resource_readout += (
            0.01
            * np.clip(residual, -1, 1)
            * resource_features
            / (1 + resource_features @ resource_features)
        )
        # Learned equilibrium forecasts participate in the action path. This is
        # a bounded one-step heuristic, not a demonstrated optimal controller.
        if self.ticks >= 8:
            candidates = np.clip(
                np.vstack(
                    (
                        action,
                        np.zeros(self.actions),
                        action + 0.25 * np.eye(self.actions),
                        action - 0.25 * np.eye(self.actions),
                    )
                ),
                -1,
                1,
            )
            future = self.world.predict(
                np.repeat(latent[None], len(candidates), axis=0), candidates
            )
            value = (
                future @ self.resource_readout[:8]
                + self.resource_readout[8]
                - 0.01 * np.sum(candidates * candidates, axis=1)
            )
            self.planning_weight = 0.25 / (1 + 100 * self.last_error)
            action = (
                1 - self.planning_weight
            ) * action + self.planning_weight * candidates[int(np.argmax(value))]
        self.ticks += 1
        self.memory.age(1.0, consolidation=float(np.clip(modulator, 0, 1)) * 0.01)
        if self.ticks % 32 == 0:
            self.memory.consolidate(self.memory.encode(latent[None]))
            self.memory.manage_capacity()
        self.previous_latent = latent
        self.previous_action = action.copy()
        return action, latent

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "topology": self.topology,
            "activation": self.activation.copy(),
            "adex": self.adex.state_dict(),
            "hh": {
                str(i): {"v": n.v.copy(), "gates": n.gates.copy()}
                for i, n in self.hh.items()
            },
            "world": self.world.state_dict(),
            "memory": self.memory.state_dict(),
            "previous_latent": self.previous_latent,
            "previous_action": self.previous_action,
            "ticks": self.ticks,
            "last_error": self.last_error,
            "resource_readout": self.resource_readout.copy(),
            "planning_weight": self.planning_weight,
        }

    def restore(self, state):
        self.topology = {
            k: np.asarray(v) if k != "active_synapses" else v
            for k, v in state["topology"].items()
        }
        self.activation = np.asarray(state["activation"]).copy()
        self.adex.load_state_dict(state["adex"])
        for key, value in state["hh"].items():
            self.hh[int(key)].v = np.asarray(value["v"]).copy()
            self.hh[int(key)].gates = np.asarray(value["gates"]).copy()
        self.world = EquilibriumWorldModel.from_state_dict(state["world"])
        self.memory = AdaptiveSubspaceLinear.from_state_dict(state["memory"])
        self.previous_latent = state["previous_latent"]
        self.previous_action = np.asarray(state["previous_action"]).copy()
        self.ticks = state["ticks"]
        self.last_error = state["last_error"]
        self.resource_readout = np.asarray(state["resource_readout"]).copy()
        self.planning_weight = state["planning_weight"]

ARQUIVO: nova/bioma/cli.py

"""Explicit entry points for local Biocosm experiments and opt-in peer exchange."""

from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import asyncio
from pathlib import Path
from .determinism import canonical_json, strict_json
from .presets import PRESETS, create_ecosystem


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="nova cosm",
        description="Experimental fields, organisms, reproducible seeds and scoped discovery",
    )
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    for name in ("run", "serve", "render"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name)
        p.add_argument("--preset", choices=PRESETS, default="organisms")
        p.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
        p.add_argument("--size", type=int, default=32)
        p.add_argument("--dimensions", type=int, choices=(2, 3), default=3)
        p.add_argument("--population", type=int)
        p.add_argument(
            "--genome",
            help="local genome or evolution champion JSON for custom genesis",
        )
        p.add_argument(
            "--resume", help="local ecosystem checkpoint; not a remote executable"
        )
        if name == "run":
            p.add_argument("--ticks", type=int, default=100)
            p.add_argument("--output", default="runs/cosm")
            p.add_argument(
                "--tier",
                choices=("same_environment", "strict_integer"),
                default="same_environment",
            )
        if name == "serve":
            p.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8765)
        if name == "render":
            p.add_argument("--output", default="runs/cosm.html")
            p.add_argument(
                "--kind", choices=("html", "volume", "slice"), default="html"
            )
            p.add_argument("--resolution", type=int, default=384)
    p = sub.add_parser("reproduce")
    p.add_argument("packet")
    p.add_argument("--output")
    p.add_argument("--max-ticks", type=int, default=10000)
    p.add_argument("--require-signature", action="store_true")
    p = sub.add_parser("discover")
    p.add_argument("--dataset", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--output", default="runs/candidate.json")
    p.add_argument("--target", choices=("du_dt", "dv_dt"), default="dv_dt")
    p.add_argument("--degree", type=int, default=3)
    p.add_argument("--max-terms", type=int, default=8)
    p = sub.add_parser("verify")
    p.add_argument("--variables", nargs="+", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--lhs", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--rhs", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--backend", choices=("exact", "z3", "lean"), default="exact")
    p.add_argument("--lean-project")
    p = sub.add_parser("evolve")
    p.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    p.add_argument("--population", type=int, default=4)
    p.add_argument("--generations", type=int, default=4)
    p.add_argument("--evaluations", type=int, default=16)
    p.add_argument("--ticks", type=int, default=32)
    p.add_argument("--output", default="runs/evolution.json")
    for name in ("node", "sync", "peer-id"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name)
        p.add_argument("--directory", default="runs/peer")
        p.add_argument(
            "--peers", help="local JSON list of pinned {peer_id,endpoint} objects"
        )
        if name == "node":
            p.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
            p.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8766)
            p.add_argument("--transport", choices=("tcp", "websocket"), default="tcp")
            p.add_argument("--certfile")
            p.add_argument("--keyfile")
        if name == "sync":
            p.add_argument("--peer-id", required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.command in ("run", "serve", "render"):
        from .ecosystem import Ecosystem

        genomes = None
        if args.genome:
            if args.resume:
                parser.error("--genome sets genesis and cannot accompany --resume")
            content = strict_json(Path(args.genome).read_bytes())
            genomes = [content.get("champion", content)]
        eco = (
            Ecosystem.load(args.resume)
            if args.resume
            else create_ecosystem(
                args.preset,
                seed=args.seed,
                size=args.size,
                dimensions=args.dimensions,
                population=args.population,
                genesis_genomes=genomes,
            )
        )
        if args.command == "run":
            from .pipeline import run_and_export
            from nova.visuals import FieldRenderer

            result = run_and_export(eco, args.output, args.ticks, tier=args.tier)
            FieldRenderer.export_html(eco, Path(args.output) / "viewer.html")
            FieldRenderer.render(eco, Path(args.output) / "volume.png")
        elif args.command == "serve":
            from nova.visuals.server import serve

            serve(eco, port=args.port)
            return
        else:
            from nova.visuals import FieldRenderer

            result = (
                FieldRenderer.export_html(eco, args.output)
                if args.kind == "html"
                else FieldRenderer.render(
                    eco, args.output, kind=args.kind, resolution=args.resolution
                )
            )
    elif args.command == "reproduce":
        from nova.swarm.holoseed import HoloSeed

        eco, result = HoloSeed.reproduce(
            strict_json(Path(args.packet).read_bytes()),
            max_ticks=args.max_ticks,
            require_signature=args.require_signature,
        )
        if args.output:
            eco.save(args.output)
        if result["status"] != "matched":
            print(canonical_json(result).decode())
            raise SystemExit(2)
    elif args.command == "discover":
        from .pipeline import discover_dataset

        result = discover_dataset(
            args.dataset,
            args.output,
            target=args.target,
            degree=args.degree,
            max_terms=args.max_terms,
        )
    elif args.command == "verify":
        from nova.scientific.polynomial import Polynomial
        from nova.scientific.verification import PolynomialVerifier, Z3Verifier

        lhs = Polynomial.parse(args.lhs, args.variables)
        rhs = Polynomial.parse(args.rhs, args.variables)
        if args.backend == "lean":
            from nova.scientific.verifier_lean import LeanVerifier

            if not args.lean_project:
                parser.error("--lean-project is required for Lean")
            verifier = LeanVerifier(args.lean_project)
        else:
            verifier = PolynomialVerifier() if args.backend == "exact" else Z3Verifier()
        result = verifier.identity(lhs, rhs).to_dict()
    elif args.command == "evolve":
        from .evolution import ArchitectureSearch

        if not 1 <= args.ticks <= 512:
            parser.error("--ticks must be in [1,512]")

        def evaluate(genome, seed):
            eco = create_ecosystem(
                "organisms", seed=seed, size=16, genesis_genomes=[genome.state_dict()]
            )
            eco.step(args.ticks)
            energy = sum(o.energy for o in eco.organisms)
            return {
                "fitness": energy,
                "survivors": len(eco.organisms),
                "world_seed": seed,
                "ticks": args.ticks,
                "metric": "remaining synthetic resource energy",
            }

        result = ArchitectureSearch(args.seed, args.population, args.evaluations).run(
            evaluate, args.generations
        )
        out = Path(args.output)
        out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        out.write_bytes(canonical_json(result))
        result = {k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != "champion"} | {
            "champion_artifact": str(out),
            "validation_used_for_selection": False,
        }
    else:
        from nova.swarm.artifacts import SigningIdentity, ArtifactStore
        from nova.swarm.p2p_node import Peer, PeerNode

        directory = Path(args.directory)
        identity = SigningIdentity.load_or_create(directory / "private" / "signing.key")
        peers = (
            []
            if not args.peers
            else [Peer(**p) for p in strict_json(Path(args.peers).read_bytes())]
        )
        node = PeerNode(identity, ArtifactStore(directory / "artifacts"), peers)
        if args.command == "peer-id":
            result = {"peer_id": identity.peer_id, "directory": str(directory)}
        elif args.command == "sync":
            result = asyncio.run(node.synchronize(args.peer_id))
        else:
            print(
                canonical_json(
                    {
                        "peer_id": identity.peer_id,
                        "host": args.host,
                        "port": args.port,
                        "transport": args.transport,
                    }
                ).decode(),
                flush=True,
            )
            try:
                asyncio.run(
                    node.serve(
                        args.host,
                        args.port,
                        certfile=args.certfile,
                        keyfile=args.keyfile,
                        transport=args.transport,
                    )
                )
            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                pass
            return
    print(canonical_json(result).decode())


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/bioma/determinism.py
========================================================================================
"""Specified PRNG and strict JSON/array encodings for replayable artifacts."""

from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib
import json
import platform
import sys
import numpy as np

MASK = (1 << 64) - 1


class SplitMix64:
    """SplitMix64 with explicit unsigned 64-bit wrapping; no NumPy RNG dependency."""

    def __init__(self, seed: int):
        if not isinstance(seed, int) or not 0 <= seed <= MASK:
            raise ValueError("seed must be uint64")
        self.state = seed

    def next_u64(self) -> int:
        self.state = (self.state + 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15) & MASK
        z = self.state
        z = ((z ^ (z >> 30)) * 0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9) & MASK
        z = ((z ^ (z >> 27)) * 0x94D049BB133111EB) & MASK
        return (z ^ (z >> 31)) & MASK

    def uniform(self, shape, low=0.0, high=1.0):
        count = int(np.prod(shape))
        values = np.fromiter(
            ((self.next_u64() >> 11) * 2.0**-53 for _ in range(count)), float, count
        )
        return (low + (high - low) * values).reshape(shape)

    def integers(self, low: int, high: int, shape):
        if high <= low:
            raise ValueError("empty integer range")
        width = high - low
        limit = (1 << 64) - ((1 << 64) % width)
        result = []
        for _ in range(int(np.prod(shape))):
            value = self.next_u64()
            while value >= limit:
                value = self.next_u64()
            result.append(low + value % width)
        return np.asarray(result, dtype=np.int64).reshape(shape)


def canonical_json(value) -> bytes:
    return json.dumps(
        value,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
        ensure_ascii=False,
        allow_nan=False,
    ).encode("utf-8")


def strict_json(raw: bytes | str, max_bytes=262144):
    if len(raw) > max_bytes:
        raise ValueError("JSON exceeds byte budget")

    def pairs(items):
        result = {}
        for key, value in items:
            if key in result:
                raise ValueError("duplicate JSON key")
            result[key] = value
        return result

    def constant(value):
        raise ValueError("nonfinite JSON number")

    try:
        return json.loads(raw, object_pairs_hook=pairs, parse_constant=constant)
    except RecursionError as error:
        raise ValueError("JSON nesting exceeds parser budget") from error


def digest_arrays(arrays: dict[str, np.ndarray], metadata=None) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(metadata or {}))
    for name, value in sorted(arrays.items()):
        value = np.asarray(value)
        if value.dtype.hasobject:
            raise ValueError("object arrays not serializable")
        dtype = value.dtype.newbyteorder("<")
        data = np.asarray(value, dtype=dtype, order="C")
        digest.update(
            canonical_json(
                {"name": name, "shape": list(data.shape), "dtype": dtype.str}
            )
        )
        digest.update(data.tobytes(order="C"))
    return digest.hexdigest()


def environment_fingerprint():
    import scipy

    return {
        "python": platform.python_version(),
        "numpy": np.__version__,
        "scipy": scipy.__version__,
        "machine": platform.machine(),
        "byteorder": sys.byteorder,
        "float_format": "IEEE754-binary64",
        "note": "Fingerprint is diagnostic; float kernels still require replay comparison.",
    }


def digest_state(state):
    """Hash every nested scalar and array with unambiguous structural paths."""
    arrays = {}

    def visit(value, path):
        if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
            name = canonical_json(path).decode()
            arrays[name] = value
            return {"array": name}
        if isinstance(value, np.generic):
            return value.item()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {
                str(k): visit(v, path + [str(k)])
                for k, v in sorted(value.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]))
            }
        if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
            return [visit(v, path + [i]) for i, v in enumerate(value)]
        return value

    metadata = visit(state, [])
    return digest_arrays(arrays, metadata)
