ARQUIVO: nova/bioma/ecosystem.py
========================================================================================
"""Finite digital ecosystem with resource accounting and deterministic event order."""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import hashlib
import numpy as np
from nova.checkpoint import save_state, load_state
from .physics_engine import PhysicsConfig, PhysicsEngine
from .genetics import MorphogeneticCPPN, GeneRegulatoryNetwork
from .brains import DevelopedBrain
from .determinism import SplitMix64, canonical_json, digest_state


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EcosystemConfig:
    initial_population: int = 4
    max_population: int = 32
    initial_energy: float = 3.0
    reproduction_energy: float = 6.0
    metabolic_rate: float = 0.002
    motor_cost: float = 0.005
    feeding_rate: float = 0.05
    food_yield: float = 4.0
    max_history: int = 2048
    neural_control: bool = True
    mutation_rate: float = 0.05

    def __post_init__(self):
        if (
            not 0 <= self.initial_population <= self.max_population <= 256
            or not 1 <= self.max_history <= 8192
        ):
            raise ValueError("population/history budget exceeded")
        values = (
            self.initial_energy,
            self.reproduction_energy,
            self.metabolic_rate,
            self.motor_cost,
            self.feeding_rate,
            self.food_yield,
        )
        if (
            any(not np.isfinite(x) or x < 0 for x in values)
            or self.initial_energy <= 0
            or self.reproduction_energy <= 0
            or not 0 <= self.mutation_rate <= 1
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid resource parameters")


class Organism:
    def __init__(self, identifier, genome, position, energy, *, neural_control=True):
        self.id = identifier
        self.genome = genome
        self.position = np.asarray(position, dtype=float)
        self.velocity = np.zeros_like(self.position)
        self.energy = float(energy)
        self.age = 0
        self.body = genome.develop_body()
        self.grn = GeneRegulatoryNetwork(genome.grn)
        self.brain = (
            DevelopedBrain(genome, actions=len(self.position))
            if neural_control
            else None
        )
        self.last_latent = np.zeros(8)
        self.neural_complexity = genome.develop_brain()["active_synapses"]

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "genome": self.genome.state_dict(),
            "position": self.position.copy(),
            "velocity": self.velocity.copy(),
            "energy": self.energy,
            "age": self.age,
            "grn_expression": self.grn.expression.copy(),
            "body": self.body,
            "brain": None if self.brain is None else self.brain.state_dict(),
            "last_latent": self.last_latent.copy(),
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state):
        obj = cls(
            state["id"],
            MorphogeneticCPPN.from_state_dict(state["genome"]),
            state["position"],
            state["energy"],
            neural_control=state["brain"] is not None,
        )
        obj.velocity = np.asarray(state["velocity"]).copy()
        obj.age = state["age"]
        obj.grn.expression = np.asarray(state["grn_expression"]).copy()
        obj.body = state["body"]
        obj.last_latent = np.asarray(state["last_latent"]).copy()
        if obj.brain:
            obj.brain.restore(state["brain"])
        return obj


class Ecosystem:
    """Organisms consume a chemical resource and spend a nondimensional energy proxy.

    Body morphology changes mass/drag/metabolic costs. Bodies are point masses
    with developed voxel appearance, not resolved muscle/soft-body mechanics.
    Reproduction transfers parent energy; metabolism is an explicit sink. This
    is digital selection over a grammar, not evidence of biological life.
    """

    protocol = "NOVA-ECOSYSTEM-v1"

    def __init__(
        self,
        physics: PhysicsConfig | None = None,
        config: EcosystemConfig | None = None,
        *,
        genesis_genomes=None,
    ):
        self.world = PhysicsEngine(physics)
        self.config = config or EcosystemConfig()
        self.rng = SplitMix64(self.world.config.seed ^ 0xCAFE)
        self.genesis_genomes = None
        if genesis_genomes is not None:
            if len(genesis_genomes) != self.config.initial_population:
                raise ValueError("genesis population mismatch")
            self.genesis_genomes = [
                MorphogeneticCPPN.from_state_dict(g).state_dict()
                for g in genesis_genomes
            ]
        self.organisms = []
        self.events = []
        self.history = []
        self.births = 0
        self.deaths = 0
        self.spent_energy = 0.0
        self.food_energy = 0.0
        for index in range(self.config.initial_population):
            seed = self.rng.next_u64()
            genome = (
                MorphogeneticCPPN(seed)
                if self.genesis_genomes is None
                else MorphogeneticCPPN.from_state_dict(self.genesis_genomes[index])
            )
            self.organisms.append(
                Organism(
                    self._id(genome),
                    genome,
                    self.rng.uniform((self.world.ndim,)) * np.asarray(self.world.shape),
                    self.config.initial_energy,
                    neural_control=self.config.neural_control,
                )
            )

    def _id(self, genome):
        return hashlib.sha256(
            canonical_json(
                {
                    "genome": genome.hash(),
                    "serial": self.births,
                    "initial_slot": len(self.organisms),
                    "tick": self.world.tick,
                }
            )
        ).hexdigest()[:24]

    def apply_event(self, event):
        # Only data operations, never source code/callback deserialization.
        if len(self.events) >= 10000:
            raise ValueError("event log budget exceeded")
        if event.get("tick") != self.world.tick or event.get("kind") not in (
            "inject",
            "impulse",
        ):
            raise ValueError("event tick/kind mismatch")
        position = np.asarray(event["position"], dtype=float)
        if position.shape != (self.world.ndim,) or not np.isfinite(position).all():
            raise ValueError("invalid event position")
        amount = float(event.get("amount", 0))
        if not np.isfinite(amount) or abs(amount) > 1:
            raise ValueError("event amplitude out of range")
        self.world.inject(
            position,
            nutrient=amount if event["kind"] == "inject" else 0.0,
            signal=amount if event["kind"] == "impulse" else 0.0,
        )
        self.events.append(dict(event))

    def _sensory(self, o):
        resource = float(self.world.sample(self.world.v, o.position[None])[0])
        signal = float(self.world.sample(self.world.wave, o.position[None])[0])
        flow = np.array(
            [self.world.sample(v, o.position[None])[0] for v in self.world.velocity]
        )
        position = o.position / np.asarray(self.world.shape)
        values = np.r_[
            position,
            o.velocity,
            flow,
            resource,
            signal,
            o.energy / self.config.reproduction_energy,
        ]
        return np.resize(np.tanh(values), 12), resource, flow

    def step(self, steps=1):
        if not isinstance(steps, int) or not 1 <= steps <= 10000:
            raise ValueError("invalid tick budget")
        c = self.config
        dt = self.world.config.dt
        for _ in range(steps):
            self.world.step()
            newborn = []
            survivors = []
            for o in sorted(self.organisms, key=lambda x: x.id):
                sensory, _, flow = self._sensory(o)
                expression = o.grn.step(sensory, dt=min(dt, 1.0))
                if o.brain:
                    action, o.last_latent = o.brain.step(
                        sensory, modulator=expression[0]
                    )
                else:
                    action = np.sin(o.position + o.age * 0.1 + o.genome.seed % 31)
                mass = 1 + o.body["mass_proxy"] / max(
                    1, o.genome.config.body_resolution**3
                )
                force = action * (0.25 + 0.75 * expression[1])
                drag = 0.2 + o.body["surface_proxy"] / max(
                    1, o.genome.config.body_resolution**3
                )
                o.velocity += dt * (force / mass + drag * (flow - o.velocity))
                o.position = (o.position + dt * o.velocity) % np.asarray(
                    self.world.shape
                )
                taken = self.world.consume(
                    o.position, c.feeding_rate * dt * (0.5 + expression[2])
                )
                gain = c.food_yield * taken
                cost = dt * (
                    c.metabolic_rate * mass
                    + c.motor_cost * float(action @ action)
                    + 0.00001 * o.neural_complexity
                )
                o.energy += gain - cost
                self.spent_energy += cost
                self.food_energy += gain
                o.age += 1
                # Reaction to drag is deposited on the field; nearest-cell coupling
                # is approximate, so exact momentum conservation is not claimed.
                self.world.inject(
                    o.position,
                    heat=cost,
                    signal=0.005 * action.mean() * expression[3],
                    impulse=-dt * drag * (flow - o.velocity),
                )
                if o.energy <= 0:
                    self.deaths += 1
                    self.world.inject(o.position, nutrient=min(0.2, mass * 0.01))
                    continue
                if (
                    o.energy >= c.reproduction_energy
                    and len(self.organisms) + len(newborn) < c.max_population
                ):
                    child_genome = o.genome.mutate(self.rng.next_u64(), c.mutation_rate)
                    energy = o.energy * 0.5
                    o.energy -= energy
                    self.births += 1
                    newborn.append(
                        Organism(
                            self._id(child_genome),
                            child_genome,
                            (
                                o.position
                                + self.rng.uniform((self.world.ndim,), -0.1, 0.1)
                            )
                            % np.asarray(self.world.shape),
                            energy,
                            neural_control=c.neural_control,
                        )
                    )
                survivors.append(o)
            self.organisms = (survivors + newborn)[: c.max_population]
            self.history.append(self.metrics())
            self.history = self.history[-c.max_history :]
        return self.metrics()

    def metrics(self):
        return {
            **self.world.metrics(),
            "population": len(self.organisms),
            "births": self.births,
            "deaths": self.deaths,
            "organism_energy": float(sum(o.energy for o in self.organisms)),
            "metabolic_sink": self.spent_energy,
            "food_energy": self.food_energy,
            "genetic_diversity": len({o.genome.hash() for o in self.organisms}),
        }

    def observation_rows(self):
        rows = []
        for o in sorted(self.organisms, key=lambda x: x.id):
            rows.append(
                {
                    "tick": self.world.tick,
                    "organism": o.id,
                    "genome": o.genome.hash(),
                    "position": o.position.tolist(),
                    "velocity": o.velocity.tolist(),
                    "energy": o.energy,
                    "latent": o.last_latent.tolist(),
                    "neural_nodes": o.genome.config.neural_nodes,
                }
            )
        return rows

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "kind": self.protocol,
            "genesis_genomes": self.genesis_genomes,
            "world": self.world.state_dict(),
            "config": asdict(self.config),
            "rng": self.rng.state,
            "organisms": [o.state_dict() for o in self.organisms],
            "events": self.events,
            "history": self.history,
            "births": self.births,
            "deaths": self.deaths,
            "spent_energy": self.spent_energy,
            "food_energy": self.food_energy,
        }

    def save(self, path):
        save_state(path, self.state_dict())

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state):
        if state.get("kind") != cls.protocol:
            raise ValueError("unknown ecosystem format")
        wc = state["world"]["config"].copy()
        wc["shape"] = tuple(wc["shape"])
        wc["birth"] = tuple(wc["birth"])
        wc["survive"] = tuple(wc["survive"])
        cfg = EcosystemConfig(**state["config"])
        empty = asdict(cfg)
        empty["initial_population"] = 0
        obj = cls(PhysicsConfig(**wc), EcosystemConfig(**empty))
        obj.genesis_genomes = state.get("genesis_genomes")
        obj.config = cfg
        obj.world = PhysicsEngine.from_state_dict(state["world"])
        obj.rng.state = state["rng"]
        if len(state["organisms"]) > cfg.max_population:
            raise ValueError("population checkpoint exceeds budget")
        obj.organisms = [Organism.from_state_dict(s) for s in state["organisms"]]
        for key in (
            "events",
            "history",
            "births",
            "deaths",
            "spent_energy",
            "food_energy",
        ):
            setattr(obj, key, state[key])
        return obj

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path):
        return cls.from_state_dict(load_state(path))

    def state_hash(self):
        return digest_state(self.state_dict())


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/bioma/evolution.py
========================================================================================
"""Budgeted architecture/morphology search with separate seeds and Pareto archives."""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
import numpy as np
from .genetics import MorphogeneticCPPN
from .determinism import SplitMix64


@dataclass
class Evaluation:
    genome_hash: str
    seed: int
    fitness: float
    complexity: int
    metrics: dict


class ArchitectureSearch:
    """Mutation/selection over CPPN + graph grammar, not arbitrary universal NAS.

    A caller supplies the actual evaluation task. Archive uses measured fitness
    and complexity; validation seeds are not fed into parent selection.
    """

    def __init__(self, seed=0, population=8, max_evaluations=128):
        if not 2 <= population <= 128 or not population <= max_evaluations <= 10000:
            raise ValueError("search budget out of range")
        self.rng = SplitMix64(seed)
        self.population = [
            MorphogeneticCPPN(self.rng.next_u64()) for _ in range(population)
        ]
        self.max_evaluations = max_evaluations
        self.evaluations = []
        self.archive = []

    def run(self, evaluator, generations=4, validation_seeds=(101, 103, 107)):
        if generations < 1:
            raise ValueError("generations must be positive")
        evaluated = 0
        champion = None
        for _ in range(generations):
            scored = []
            evaluation_seed = self.rng.next_u64()
            for genome in self.population:
                if evaluated >= self.max_evaluations:
                    break
                seed = evaluation_seed
                metrics = evaluator(genome, seed)
                fitness = float(metrics["fitness"])
                if not np.isfinite(fitness):
                    raise ValueError("nonfinite task fitness")
                result = Evaluation(
                    genome.hash(),
                    seed,
                    fitness,
                    genome.config.neural_nodes
                    + genome.develop_brain()["active_synapses"],
                    dict(metrics),
                )
                self.evaluations.append(result)
                evaluated += 1
                scored.append((fitness, genome))
                self.archive.append(result)
            if not scored:
                break
            scored.sort(key=lambda x: (-x[0], x[1].hash()))
            champion = scored[0][1]
            parents = [g for _, g in scored[: max(1, len(scored) // 2)]]
            self.population = [champion] + [
                parents[i % len(parents)].mutate(self.rng.next_u64(), 0.1)
                for i in range(len(self.population) - 1)
            ]
        archive = self.archive
        self.archive = [
            a
            for a in archive
            if not any(
                b.fitness >= a.fitness
                and b.complexity <= a.complexity
                and (b.fitness > a.fitness or b.complexity < a.complexity)
                for b in archive
            )
        ]
        validation = (
            []
            if champion is None
            else [evaluator(champion, int(seed)) for seed in validation_seeds]
        )
        return {
            "champion": None if champion is None else champion.state_dict(),
            "training_evaluations": evaluated,
            "validation_evaluations": len(validation),
            "evaluation_budget_scope": "training; validation adds explicit held-out seeds",
            "validation": validation,
            "pareto": [vars(x) for x in self.archive],
        }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/bioma/genetics.py
========================================================================================
"""Seeded compositional patterns, graph grammar mutations and digital GRNs."""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict, replace
import hashlib
import numpy as np
from .determinism import SplitMix64, canonical_json


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GenomeConfig:
    width: int = 16
    depth: int = 3
    neural_nodes: int = 24
    body_resolution: int = 12
    connection_threshold: float = 0.6
    max_edges: int = 512
    max_n2: int = 2

    def __post_init__(self):
        if (
            not 4 <= self.width <= 64
            or not 1 <= self.depth <= 6
            or not 4 <= self.neural_nodes <= 128
            or not 4 <= self.body_resolution <= 24
        ):
            raise ValueError("genome development budget exceeded")
        if (
            not 0 <= self.connection_threshold <= 1
            or not 0 <= self.max_n2 <= 8
            or not 0 <= self.max_edges <= 4096
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid topology budget")


class MorphogeneticCPPN:
    """8-coordinate CPPN; pair queries use both endpoints, not just a midpoint.

    Body output is an occupancy/density score, NOT a signed distance function.
    Functions are sin, tanh, Gaussian, abs and linear, selected per hidden unit.
    Neural architecture varies within this finite grammar; not every conceivable
    neural architecture is representable or discovered by a random draw.
    """

    def __init__(self, seed=42, config: GenomeConfig | None = None):
        self.seed = seed
        self.config = config or GenomeConfig()
        c = self.config
        rng = SplitMix64(seed)
        self.w_in = rng.uniform((8, c.width), -1, 1)
        self.weights = [
            rng.uniform((c.width, c.width), -1 / np.sqrt(c.width), 1 / np.sqrt(c.width))
            for _ in range(c.depth)
        ]
        self.biases = [rng.uniform((c.width,), -0.5, 0.5) for _ in range(c.depth)]
        self.activations = rng.integers(0, 5, (c.depth, c.width))
        self.w_out = rng.uniform((c.width, 8), -0.5, 0.5)
        self.coordinates = rng.uniform((c.neural_nodes, 3), -1, 1)
        self.grn = rng.uniform((6, 6), -0.5, 0.5)
        self.grammar = []
        self.parents = []
        self.generation = 0

    def _evaluate(self, features):
        h = np.tanh(np.asarray(features) @ self.w_in)
        for w, b, kinds in zip(self.weights, self.biases, self.activations):
            x = np.clip(h @ w + b, -20, 20)
            options = np.stack(
                (np.sin(x), np.tanh(x), np.exp(-x * x), np.abs(x), x), axis=-1
            )
            h = np.take_along_axis(
                options, np.broadcast_to(kinds, x.shape)[..., None], axis=-1
            )[..., 0]
        return np.tanh(h @ self.w_out)

    def query_body(self, coordinates):
        p = np.asarray(coordinates, dtype=float)
        r = np.linalg.norm(p, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
        return self._evaluate(
            np.concatenate(
                (p, r, np.zeros((*p.shape[:-1], 3)), np.ones_like(r)), axis=-1
            )
        )

    def query_pair(self, left, right):
        left, right = np.broadcast_arrays(np.asarray(left), np.asarray(right))
        distance = np.linalg.norm(left - right, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
        return self._evaluate(
            np.concatenate((left, right, distance, np.ones_like(distance)), axis=-1)
        )

    def develop_body(self):
        axis = np.linspace(-1, 1, self.config.body_resolution)
        coords = np.stack(np.meshgrid(axis, axis, axis, indexing="ij"), -1)
        values = self.query_body(coords)
        density = np.maximum(values[..., 0] - 0.2 * np.linalg.norm(coords, axis=-1), 0)
        material = np.argmax(values[..., 1:5], axis=-1).astype(np.uint8)
        return {
            "density": density,
            "material": material,
            "occupied_voxels": int(np.count_nonzero(density > 0.1)),
            "mass_proxy": float(density.sum()),
            "surface_proxy": float(
                sum(np.abs(np.diff(density, axis=a)).sum() for a in range(3))
            ),
        }

    def develop_brain(self):
        n = len(self.coordinates)
        properties = self.query_body(self.coordinates)
        types = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int64)
        # Cell identity determines every outgoing sign (Dale); row=source.
        inhibitory = np.argsort(properties[:, 1], kind="stable")[: max(1, n // 5)]
        types[inhibitory] = 1
        if len(inhibitory) > 1:
            types[inhibitory[len(inhibitory) // 2 :]] = 2
        if len(inhibitory) > 3:
            types[inhibitory[-max(1, len(inhibitory) // 4) :]] = 3
        pair = self.query_pair(
            self.coordinates[:, None, :], self.coordinates[None, :, :]
        )
        strength = np.maximum(pair[..., 3], 0)
        mask = pair[..., 4] > self.config.connection_threshold
        np.fill_diagonal(mask, False)
        eligible = np.flatnonzero(mask)
        keep = (
            eligible[
                np.argsort(strength.ravel()[eligible], kind="stable")[
                    -self.config.max_edges :
                ]
            ]
            if self.config.max_edges
            else []
        )
        weights = np.zeros((n, n))
        weights.ravel()[keep] = strength.ravel()[keep] * 10
        weights *= np.where(types == 0, 1.0, -1.0)[:, None]
        families = np.argmax(
            properties[:, 4:8], axis=1
        )  # 0=rate, 1=AdEx, 2=HH, 3=logic
        n2 = np.flatnonzero(families == 2)
        if len(n2) > self.config.max_n2:
            families[n2[self.config.max_n2 :]] = 1
        for operation in self.grammar:
            if operation["op"] == "edge":
                i, j = operation["source"] % n, operation["target"] % n
                if i != j:
                    weights[i, j] = abs(operation["weight"]) * (
                        1 if types[i] == 0 else -1
                    )
            elif operation["op"] == "prune":
                weights[operation["source"] % n, operation["target"] % n] = 0
        # Apply edge budget after grammar too.
        active = np.flatnonzero(weights)
        if len(active) > self.config.max_edges:
            drop = (
                active[
                    np.argsort(abs(weights.ravel()[active]), kind="stable")[
                        : -self.config.max_edges
                    ]
                ]
                if self.config.max_edges
                else active
            )
            weights.ravel()[drop] = 0
        return {
            "coordinates": self.coordinates.copy(),
            "types": types,
            "families": families,
            "weights": weights,
            "active_synapses": int(np.count_nonzero(weights)),
        }

    def mutate(self, seed, rate=0.05):
        if not 0 <= rate <= 1:
            raise ValueError("mutation rate must be in [0,1]")
        child = MorphogeneticCPPN.from_state_dict(self.state_dict())
        rng = SplitMix64(seed)
        for value in [
            child.w_in,
            *child.weights,
            *child.biases,
            child.w_out,
            child.grn,
        ]:
            mask = rng.uniform(value.shape) < rate
            value += mask * rng.uniform(value.shape, -0.15, 0.15)
        if rng.uniform((1,))[0] < rate:
            child.activations = rng.integers(0, 5, child.activations.shape)
        if rng.uniform((1,))[0] < rate:
            n = len(child.coordinates)
            if n < 128 and (n <= 4 or rng.next_u64() % 2):
                child.coordinates = np.vstack(
                    (child.coordinates, rng.uniform((1, 3), -1, 1))
                )
            elif n > 4:
                child.coordinates = child.coordinates[:-1].copy()
            child.config = replace(child.config, neural_nodes=len(child.coordinates))
        n = len(child.coordinates)
        op = {
            "op": "edge" if rng.next_u64() % 2 else "prune",
            "source": int(rng.next_u64() % n),
            "target": int(rng.next_u64() % n),
            "weight": float(rng.uniform((1,), 0, 10)[0]),
        }
        child.grammar = (child.grammar + [op])[-64:]
        child.parents = [self.hash()]
        child.generation += 1
        child.seed = seed
        return child

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "config": asdict(self.config),
            "seed": self.seed,
            "w_in": self.w_in.tolist(),
            "weights": [w.tolist() for w in self.weights],
            "biases": [b.tolist() for b in self.biases],
            "activations": self.activations.tolist(),
            "w_out": self.w_out.tolist(),
            "coordinates": self.coordinates.tolist(),
            "grn": self.grn.tolist(),
            "grammar": self.grammar,
            "parents": self.parents,
            "generation": self.generation,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state):
        obj = cls(state["seed"], GenomeConfig(**state["config"]))
        for name in ("w_in", "w_out", "coordinates", "grn"):
            setattr(obj, name, np.asarray(state[name], dtype=float))
        obj.weights = [np.asarray(w, dtype=float) for w in state["weights"]]
        obj.biases = [np.asarray(b, dtype=float) for b in state["biases"]]
        obj.activations = np.asarray(state["activations"], dtype=int)
        obj.grammar = state["grammar"]
        obj.parents = state["parents"]
        obj.generation = state["generation"]
        expected = cls(obj.seed, obj.config)
        for name in ("w_in", "w_out", "coordinates", "grn", "activations"):
            value = getattr(obj, name)
            if (
                value.shape != getattr(expected, name).shape
                or not np.isfinite(value).all()
            ):
                raise ValueError("invalid genome array")
        if (
            len(obj.weights) != obj.config.depth
            or len(obj.biases) != obj.config.depth
            or len(obj.grammar) > 64
        ):
            raise ValueError("genome depth/grammar budget exceeded")
        for w, b in zip(obj.weights, obj.biases):
            if (
                w.shape != (obj.config.width, obj.config.width)
                or b.shape != (obj.config.width,)
                or not np.isfinite(w).all()
                or not np.isfinite(b).all()
            ):
                raise ValueError("invalid genome layer")
        if np.any(obj.activations < 0) or np.any(obj.activations > 4):
            raise ValueError("invalid activation")
        for op in obj.grammar:
            if op.get("op") not in ("edge", "prune") or any(
                type(op.get(k)) is not int or not 0 <= op[k] < 128
                for k in ("source", "target")
            ):
                raise ValueError("invalid graph operation")
            if not np.isfinite(op.get("weight", 0)) or abs(op.get("weight", 0)) > 50:
                raise ValueError("invalid graph weight")
        if (
            len(obj.parents) > 2
            or not isinstance(obj.generation, int)
            or obj.generation < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid lineage")
        return obj

    def hash(self):
        return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(self.state_dict())).hexdigest()


class GeneRegulatoryNetwork:
    """Digital expression dx/dt = sigmoid(Wx + sensory) - x; not real wet-lab DNA."""

    def __init__(self, weights):
        self.weights = np.asarray(weights, dtype=float)
        self.expression = np.zeros(len(self.weights))

    def step(self, sensory, dt=0.1):
        if not 0 < dt <= 1:
            raise ValueError("GRN dt must be in (0,1]")
        drive = np.clip(
            self.weights @ self.expression + np.resize(sensory, len(self.expression)),
            -30,
            30,
        )
        self.expression += dt * (1 / (1 + np.exp(-drive)) - self.expression)
        return self.expression.copy()


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/bioma/physics_engine.py
========================================================================================
"""Explicit numerical substrates: reaction/diffusion, life, fluid, wave, particles.

All quantities are nondimensional. Fluid is periodic incompressible flow using
semi-Lagrangian advection and spectral pressure projection, not a turbulence or
molecular simulator. Stability/clipping diagnostics are part of the state.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from fractions import Fraction
from itertools import product
import numpy as np
from scipy.ndimage import map_coordinates
from .determinism import SplitMix64, digest_arrays


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PhysicsConfig:
    mode: str = "gray_scott"
    shape: tuple[int, ...] = (32, 32, 32)
    seed: int = 0
    dt: float = 0.1
    dx: float = 1.0
    feed: float = 0.035
    kill: float = 0.065
    diffusion_u: float = 0.16
    diffusion_v: float = 0.08
    viscosity: float = 0.02
    thermal_diffusion: float = 0.01
    wave_speed: float = 1.0
    wave_damping: float = 0.02
    lenia_radius: float = 4.0
    lenia_mu: float = 0.15
    lenia_sigma: float = 0.035
    birth: tuple[int, ...] = ()
    survive: tuple[int, ...] = ()
    particles: int = 128
    max_cells: int = 262144
    max_particles: int = 4096
    max_substeps: int = 128

    def __post_init__(self):
        if (
            not 1 <= self.max_cells <= 262144
            or not 0 <= self.max_particles <= 4096
            or not 1 <= self.max_substeps <= 128
        ):
            raise ValueError("absolute allocation/iteration budget exceeded")
        if self.mode not in (
            "gray_scott",
            "fixed_gray_scott",
            "lenia",
            "conway",
            "fluid",
            "wave",
            "particles",
            "hybrid",
            "nca",
        ):
            raise ValueError("unknown field mode")
        if len(self.shape) not in (2, 3) or any(
            not isinstance(n, int) or not 4 <= n <= 128 for n in self.shape
        ):
            raise ValueError("shape requires 2/3 dimensions in [4,128]")
        if (
            int(np.prod(self.shape)) > self.max_cells
            or not 0 <= self.particles <= self.max_particles
        ):
            raise ValueError("field/particle budget exceeded")
        values = (
            self.dt,
            self.dx,
            self.feed,
            self.kill,
            self.diffusion_u,
            self.diffusion_v,
            self.viscosity,
            self.thermal_diffusion,
            self.wave_speed,
            self.wave_damping,
            self.lenia_radius,
            self.lenia_sigma,
        )
        if (
            any(not np.isfinite(v) or not 0 <= v <= 1000 for v in values)
            or min(self.dt, self.dx, self.lenia_radius, self.lenia_sigma) <= 0
            or self.max_substeps < 1
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid physical scales")
        if not 1e-3 <= self.dx <= 1e3 or not 1e-6 <= self.dt <= 10:
            raise ValueError("spatial/time scale budget exceeded")
        if self.mode == "fixed_gray_scott" and (
            self.dx != 1
            or self.dt < 1 / (1 << 16)
            or self.dt > 1
            or max(self.feed, self.kill, self.diffusion_u, self.diffusion_v) > 1
        ):
            raise ValueError("fixed-point coefficients must be in [0,1], dt<=1")
        SplitMix64(self.seed)


class PhysicsEngine:
    protocol = "NOVA-FIELD-v1"
    Q = 1 << 16

    def __init__(self, config: PhysicsConfig | None = None):
        self.config = config or PhysicsConfig()
        c = self.config
        self.shape = tuple(c.shape)
        self.ndim = len(self.shape)
        self.rng = SplitMix64(c.seed)
        self.tick = 0
        self.elapsed = 0.0
        self.diagnostics = {
            "clipped_values": 0,
            "external_nutrient": 0.0,
            "substeps": 0,
        }
        self.u = np.ones(self.shape)
        self.v = np.zeros(self.shape)
        center = tuple(
            slice(n // 2 - max(1, n // 8), n // 2 + max(1, n // 8)) for n in self.shape
        )
        self.v[center] = self.rng.uniform(self.v[center].shape, 0.2, 0.8)
        self.life = (self.rng.integers(0, 4, self.shape) == 0).astype(np.uint8)
        self.fixed_u = np.full(self.shape, self.Q, dtype=np.int64)
        self.fixed_v = np.zeros(self.shape, dtype=np.int64)
        self.fixed_v[center] = SplitMix64(c.seed ^ 0xF17ED).integers(
            self.Q // 5, 4 * self.Q // 5, self.v[center].shape
        )
        if c.mode == "fixed_gray_scott":
            self.v = self.fixed_v.astype(float) / self.Q
        self.velocity = np.zeros((self.ndim, *self.shape))
        self.temperature = np.zeros(self.shape)
        self.pressure = np.zeros(self.shape)
        self.wave = np.zeros(self.shape)
        self.wave_velocity = np.zeros(self.shape)
        self.channels = np.zeros((4, *self.shape))
        self.channels[0] = self.v
        self.particle_positions = self.rng.uniform(
            (c.particles, self.ndim)
        ) * np.asarray(self.shape)
        self.particle_velocity = np.zeros_like(self.particle_positions)
        self._coordinates = np.indices(self.shape, dtype=float)
        ks = []
        for axis, n in enumerate(self.shape):
            frequency = 2 * np.pi * np.fft.fftfreq(n, d=c.dx)
            if n % 2 == 0:
                frequency[n // 2] = 0  # real spectral derivative of Nyquist modes
            view = [1] * self.ndim
            view[axis] = n
            ks.append(frequency.reshape(view))
        self._k = ks
        self._k2 = sum(k * k for k in ks)
        radius = np.sqrt(
            sum(
                np.minimum(a, n - a) ** 2 for a, n in zip(self._coordinates, self.shape)
            )
        )
        kernel = np.exp(-0.5 * ((radius / c.lenia_radius - 0.5) / 0.15) ** 2) * (
            radius < c.lenia_radius
        )
        self._lenia_kernel = np.fft.fftn(kernel / max(kernel.sum(), 1e-12))
        self._nca_weights = self.rng.uniform((8, 4), -0.08, 0.08)

    def laplacian(self, x):
        return (
            sum(np.roll(x, 1, a) + np.roll(x, -1, a) - 2 * x for a in range(self.ndim))
            / self.config.dx**2
        )

    def _clip(self, x, low=0.0, high=1.0):
        self.diagnostics["clipped_values"] += int(
            np.count_nonzero((x < low) | (x > high))
        )
        return np.clip(x, low, high)

    def sample(self, field, positions):
        positions = np.asarray(positions, dtype=float)
        return map_coordinates(
            field, positions.T, order=1, mode="grid-wrap", prefilter=False
        )

    def inject(self, position, *, nutrient=0.0, heat=0.0, signal=0.0, impulse=None):
        index = tuple(
            (np.floor(position).astype(int) % np.asarray(self.shape)).tolist()
        )
        self.v[index] = float(np.clip(self.v[index] + nutrient, 0, 1))
        self.temperature[index] += heat
        self.wave_velocity[index] += signal
        self.diagnostics["external_nutrient"] += float(nutrient)
        if impulse is not None:
            self.velocity[(slice(None), *index)] += np.asarray(impulse)

    def consume(self, position, amount):
        index = tuple(
            (np.floor(position).astype(int) % np.asarray(self.shape)).tolist()
        )
        taken = min(max(float(amount), 0.0), float(self.v[index]))
        self.v[index] -= taken
        return taken

    def _reaction(self, dt):
        c = self.config
        reaction = self.u * self.v * self.v
        du = c.diffusion_u * self.laplacian(self.u) - reaction + c.feed * (1 - self.u)
        dv = (
            c.diffusion_v * self.laplacian(self.v)
            + reaction
            - (c.feed + c.kill) * self.v
        )
        self.u = self._clip(self.u + dt * du)
        self.v = self._clip(self.v + dt * dv)

    def _fixed_reaction(self):
        c = self.config
        Q = self.Q
        quant = lambda x: int(Fraction(str(x)) * Q + Fraction(1, 2))
        dt, du, dv, feed, kill = map(
            quant, (c.dt, c.diffusion_u, c.diffusion_v, c.feed, c.kill)
        )
        u, v = self.fixed_u, self.fixed_v
        lap = lambda x: sum(
            np.roll(x, 1, a) + np.roll(x, -1, a) - 2 * x for a in range(self.ndim)
        )
        # dx must be integral-compatible; division represented by exact rational integers.
        dx2 = Fraction(str(c.dx)) ** 2
        lu = (lap(u) * dx2.denominator) // dx2.numerator
        lv = (lap(v) * dx2.denominator) // dx2.numerator
        reaction = (((u * v) // Q) * v) // Q
        nu = u + (dt * ((du * lu) // Q - reaction + (feed * (Q - u)) // Q)) // Q
        nv = v + (dt * ((dv * lv) // Q + reaction - ((feed + kill) * v) // Q)) // Q
        self.diagnostics["clipped_values"] += int(
            np.count_nonzero((nu < 0) | (nu > Q) | (nv < 0) | (nv > Q))
        )
        self.fixed_u = np.clip(nu, 0, Q)
        self.fixed_v = np.clip(nv, 0, Q)
        self.u = self.fixed_u.astype(float) / Q
        self.v = self.fixed_v.astype(float) / Q

    def _conway(self):
        neighbors = np.zeros(self.shape, dtype=np.int16)
        for shift in product((-1, 0, 1), repeat=self.ndim):
            if any(shift):
                neighbors += np.roll(self.life, shift, tuple(range(self.ndim)))
        birth = self.config.birth or ((3,) if self.ndim == 2 else (6,))
        survive = self.config.survive or ((2, 3) if self.ndim == 2 else (5, 6, 7))
        self.life = (
            (self.life.astype(bool) & np.isin(neighbors, survive))
            | (~self.life.astype(bool) & np.isin(neighbors, birth))
        ).astype(np.uint8)
        self.v = self.life.astype(float)

    def _lenia(self, dt):
        potential = np.fft.ifftn(np.fft.fftn(self.v) * self._lenia_kernel).real
        growth = (
            2
            * np.exp(
                -0.5
                * ((potential - self.config.lenia_mu) / self.config.lenia_sigma) ** 2
            )
            - 1
        )
        self.v = self._clip(self.v + dt * growth)

    def _advect(self, field, dt):
        coordinates = self._coordinates - dt * self.velocity / self.config.dx
        return map_coordinates(
            field, coordinates, order=1, mode="grid-wrap", prefilter=False
        )

    def _fluid(self, dt):
        c = self.config
        advected = np.stack([self._advect(v, dt) for v in self.velocity])
        vh = [np.fft.fftn(v) / (1 + c.viscosity * dt * self._k2) for v in advected]
        dot = sum(k * v for k, v in zip(self._k, vh))
        denominator = np.where(self._k2 == 0, 1, self._k2)
        self.pressure = np.fft.ifftn(-1j * dot / (dt * denominator)).real
        self.velocity = np.stack(
            [np.fft.ifftn(v - k * dot / denominator).real for k, v in zip(self._k, vh)]
        )
        self.v = self._clip(self._advect(self.v, dt))
        self.temperature = self._advect(
            self.temperature, dt
        ) + dt * c.thermal_diffusion * self.laplacian(self.temperature)

    def _wave(self, dt):
        c = self.config
        self.wave_velocity += dt * (
            c.wave_speed**2 * self.laplacian(self.wave)
            - c.wave_damping * self.wave_velocity
        )
        self.wave += dt * self.wave_velocity

    def _particles(self, dt):
        if not len(self.particle_positions):
            return
        flow = np.stack(
            [self.sample(v, self.particle_positions) for v in self.velocity], axis=1
        )
        gravity = np.zeros(self.ndim)
        gravity[-1] = -0.1
        self.particle_velocity += dt * (gravity + 0.2 * (flow - self.particle_velocity))
        self.particle_positions += dt * self.particle_velocity
        for axis, extent in enumerate(self.shape):
            low = self.particle_positions[:, axis] < 0
            high = self.particle_positions[:, axis] > extent - 1
            self.particle_velocity[low | high, axis] *= -0.7
            self.particle_positions[:, axis] = np.clip(
                self.particle_positions[:, axis], 0, extent - 1
            )

    def _nca(self, dt):
        # A fixed local neural cellular automaton, not a pretrained life model.
        perception = np.concatenate(
            (self.channels, np.stack([self.laplacian(x) for x in self.channels])),
            axis=0,
        )
        update = np.tanh(np.einsum("c...,co->o...", perception, self._nca_weights))
        self.channels = self._clip(self.channels + dt * update)
        self.v = self.channels[0].copy()

    def step(self, steps=1):
        if not isinstance(steps, int) or not 1 <= steps <= 10000:
            raise ValueError("steps must be in [1,10000]")
        c = self.config
        bound = max(
            2
            * self.ndim
            * max(c.diffusion_u, c.diffusion_v, c.thermal_diffusion)
            / c.dx**2,
            c.wave_speed * np.sqrt(self.ndim) / c.dx,
            1.0,
        )
        count = max(1, int(np.ceil(c.dt * bound / 0.5)))
        if count > c.max_substeps:
            raise ValueError("stability requires too many substeps")
        if (
            c.mode == "fixed_gray_scott"
            and c.dt * 2 * self.ndim * max(c.diffusion_u, c.diffusion_v) / c.dx**2 > 0.5
        ):
            raise ValueError("fixed-point stencil exceeds configured stability bound")
        for _ in range(steps):
            if c.mode == "conway":
                self._conway()
            elif c.mode == "fixed_gray_scott":
                self._fixed_reaction()
            else:
                for _ in range(count):
                    dt = c.dt / count
                    if c.mode in ("gray_scott", "hybrid"):
                        self._reaction(dt)
                    if c.mode == "lenia":
                        self._lenia(dt)
                    if c.mode == "nca":
                        self._nca(dt)
                    if c.mode in ("fluid", "hybrid", "particles"):
                        self._fluid(dt)
                    if c.mode in ("wave", "hybrid"):
                        self._wave(dt)
                    if c.mode in ("particles", "hybrid"):
                        self._particles(dt)
            self.tick += 1
            self.elapsed = self.tick * c.dt
            self.diagnostics["substeps"] = count
            if not all(
                np.isfinite(x).all()
                for x in (
                    self.u,
                    self.v,
                    self.velocity,
                    self.wave,
                    self.temperature,
                    self.particle_positions,
                    self.particle_velocity,
                    self.channels,
                )
            ):
                raise FloatingPointError("nonfinite physical state")
        return self.metrics()

    def metrics(self):
        divergence = np.fft.ifftn(
            sum(1j * k * np.fft.fftn(v) for k, v in zip(self._k, self.velocity))
        ).real
        return {
            "tick": self.tick,
            "mass_u": float(self.u.sum()),
            "mass_v": float(self.v.sum()),
            "kinetic": float(0.5 * np.sum(self.velocity**2)),
            "wave_energy_proxy": float(0.5 * np.sum(self.wave_velocity**2)),
            "spectral_divergence_rms": float(np.sqrt(np.mean(divergence**2))),
            **self.diagnostics,
        }

    def state_dict(self):
        arrays = {
            k: getattr(self, k).copy()
            for k in (
                "u",
                "v",
                "life",
                "fixed_u",
                "fixed_v",
                "velocity",
                "temperature",
                "pressure",
                "wave",
                "wave_velocity",
                "channels",
                "particle_positions",
                "particle_velocity",
            )
        }
        return {
            "config": asdict(self.config),
            "tick": self.tick,
            "rng_state": self.rng.state,
            "diagnostics": self.diagnostics.copy(),
            "arrays": arrays,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state):
        c = state["config"].copy()
        c["shape"] = tuple(c["shape"])
        c["birth"] = tuple(c["birth"])
        c["survive"] = tuple(c["survive"])
        obj = cls(PhysicsConfig(**c))
        for key, value in state["arrays"].items():
            if (
                key not in obj.state_dict()["arrays"]
                or np.shape(value) != getattr(obj, key).shape
            ):
                raise ValueError("field checkpoint shape mismatch")
            setattr(obj, key, np.asarray(value).copy())
        obj.tick = state["tick"]
        obj.elapsed = obj.tick * obj.config.dt
        obj.rng.state = state["rng_state"]
        obj.diagnostics = state["diagnostics"].copy()
        return obj

    def state_hash(self):
        # Strict modes hash only authoritative integer state and integer clock.
        if self.config.mode == "conway":
            return digest_arrays(
                {"life": self.life}, {"tick": self.tick, "rng": self.rng.state}
            )
        if self.config.mode == "fixed_gray_scott":
            return digest_arrays(
                {"u": self.fixed_u, "v": self.fixed_v},
                {"tick": self.tick, "rng": self.rng.state},
            )
        state = self.state_dict()
        return digest_arrays(
            state["arrays"], {"tick": self.tick, "rng": self.rng.state}
        )


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/bioma/pipeline.py
========================================================================================
"""Explicit local workflow: simulation -> observations -> signed replay artifacts."""

from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from nova.swarm.artifacts import ArtifactStore, SigningIdentity, ScientificLedger
from nova.swarm.holoseed import HoloSeed
from nova.scientific.dataset_synthesizer import DatasetSynthesizer
from nova.scientific.symbolic_regression import SymbolicRegressor
from .determinism import canonical_json, strict_json


def run_and_export(ecosystem, directory, ticks=100, *, tier="same_environment"):
    if not 1 <= ticks <= 4096:
        raise ValueError("run tick budget is [1,4096]")
    directory = Path(directory)
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    identity = SigningIdentity.load_or_create(directory / "private" / "signing.key")
    store = ArtifactStore(directory / "artifacts")
    observations = []
    world = ecosystem.world
    # A fixed, documented probe grid; no training labels are inferred from a proof.
    probes = (
        np.array(list(np.ndindex(*(2,) * world.ndim))) * (np.array(world.shape) // 2)
        + 1
    )
    for _ in range(ticks):
        before_u = world.u.copy()
        before_v = world.v.copy()
        lu = world.laplacian(before_u)
        lv = world.laplacian(before_v)
        ecosystem.step()
        observations.extend(
            {"kind": "organism_trajectory", **row}
            for row in ecosystem.observation_rows()
        )
        if world.config.mode in ("gray_scott", "hybrid", "fixed_gray_scott"):
            for position in probes:
                index = tuple(position)
                observations.append(
                    {
                        "kind": "chemical_probe",
                        "tick": world.tick,
                        "position": position.tolist(),
                        "features": [
                            float(before_u[index]),
                            float(before_v[index]),
                            float(lu[index]),
                            float(lv[index]),
                        ],
                        "variables": ["u", "v", "lap_u", "lap_v"],
                        "du_dt": float(
                            (world.u[index] - before_u[index]) / world.config.dt
                        ),
                        "dv_dt": float(
                            (world.v[index] - before_v[index]) / world.config.dt
                        ),
                        "coupled_organisms": bool(ecosystem.config.initial_population),
                        "trajectory": world.config.seed,
                    }
                )
    seed = HoloSeed.pack(ecosystem, tier=tier, identity=identity)
    seed_digest = store.put_json(seed)
    (directory / "holoseed.json").write_bytes(canonical_json(seed))
    ecosystem.save(directory / "ecosystem.npz")
    genomes = {
        o.genome.hash(): store.put_json(o.genome.state_dict())
        for o in ecosystem.organisms
    }
    dataset = DatasetSynthesizer(directory / "dataset")
    for observation in observations:
        dataset.append(
            observation,
            seed_digest=seed_digest,
            units={
                "space": "grid cells",
                "time": "nondimensional",
                "energy": "simulation proxy",
            },
        )
    dataset_manifest = dataset.manifest()
    dataset_digest = store.put_json(dataset_manifest)
    ledger = ScientificLedger(directory / "ledger.sqlite", identity)
    try:
        ledger.append(
            "reproduction",
            {
                "seed_digest": seed_digest,
                "tier": tier,
                "expected_state": seed["payload"]["expected_state_sha256"],
            },
        )
        for genome, digest in genomes.items():
            ledger.append("lineage", {"genome_hash": genome, "artifact": digest})
        ledger.append("dataset", {"manifest": dataset_digest, "rows": dataset.count})
        head = ledger.verify()
    finally:
        ledger.close()
    report = {
        "protocol": "NOVA-COSM-RUN-v1",
        "directory": str(directory),
        "ticks": world.tick,
        "seed_artifact": seed_digest,
        "peer_id": identity.peer_id,
        "dataset": dataset_manifest,
        "genomes": genomes,
        "ledger": head,
        "metrics": ecosystem.metrics(),
        "scientific_status": "generated simulation observations; not universal physical truths",
    }
    (directory / "run.json").write_bytes(canonical_json(report))
    return report


def discover_dataset(path, output, *, target="dv_dt", degree=3, max_terms=8):
    if target not in ("du_dt", "dv_dt"):
        raise ValueError("unsupported target")
    rows = []
    source = Path(path)
    if source.name != "observations.jsonl":
        raise ValueError("use a DatasetSynthesizer observations.jsonl file")
    if source.stat().st_size > 256 * 1024 * 1024:
        raise ValueError("dataset input byte budget exceeded")
    # Verify the existing dataset chain before interpreting scientific rows.
    DatasetSynthesizer(source.parent)
    with source.open("rb") as stream:
        for line in stream:
            if len(line) > 262144:
                raise ValueError("row size exceeded")
            envelope = strict_json(line)
            row = envelope["observation"]
            if row.get("kind") == "chemical_probe":
                rows.append((envelope["seed_digest"], row))
            if len(rows) > 50000:
                raise ValueError("discovery sample budget exceeded")
    if len(rows) < 12:
        raise ValueError("at least 12 chemical probes required")
    groups = [r["trajectory"] for _, r in rows]
    candidate = SymbolicRegressor(degree, max_terms).fit(
        np.array([r["features"] for _, r in rows]),
        np.array([r[target] for _, r in rows]),
        ["u", "v", "lap_u", "lap_v"],
        groups=groups if len(set(groups)) > 1 else None,
        provenance={
            "seed_artifacts": sorted({digest for digest, _ in rows}),
            "target": target,
            "scope": "finite-difference simulation probes; coupled fields may not admit polynomial closure",
        },
    )
    result = candidate.to_dict()
    seed_path = source.parent.parent / "holoseed.json"
    if seed_path.is_file():
        from nova.scientific.polynomial import Polynomial
        from nova.scientific.verification import PolynomialVerifier
        import hashlib

        raw = seed_path.read_bytes()
        if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() not in {digest for digest, _ in rows}:
            raise ValueError("local seed manifest does not match dataset provenance")
        payload = HoloSeed.validate(strict_json(raw))[0]
        physics = payload["physics"]
        if physics["mode"] in ("gray_scott", "hybrid"):
            expression = (
                f"({physics['diffusion_u']})*lap_u-u*v*v+({physics['feed']})*(1-u)"
                if target == "du_dt"
                else f"({physics['diffusion_v']})*lap_v+u*v*v-(({physics['feed']})+({physics['kill']}))*v"
            )
            expected = Polynomial.parse(expression, candidate.polynomial.variables)
            result["declared_model_comparison"] = (
                PolynomialVerifier()
                .identity(
                    candidate.polynomial,
                    expected,
                    model={"physics": physics, "target": target},
                )
                .to_dict()
            )
            result["verification_scope"] = (
                "coefficient identity against declared continuous Gray-Scott reaction/diffusion term only; not the coupled integrator or real-world physics"
            )

    out = Path(output)
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_bytes(canonical_json(result))
    return result


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/bioma/presets.py
========================================================================================
"""Named, bounded 3D demonstrations with explicit model semantics."""

from .physics_engine import PhysicsConfig
from .ecosystem import Ecosystem, EcosystemConfig

PRESETS = {
    "organisms": "hybrid",
    "ink": "gray_scott",
    "lenia": "lenia",
    "conway": "conway",
    "light": "wave",
    "particles": "particles",
    "fluid": "fluid",
    "nca": "nca",
    "fixed": "fixed_gray_scott",
}


def create_ecosystem(
    preset="organisms",
    *,
    seed=0,
    size=32,
    dimensions=3,
    population=None,
    genesis_genomes=None,
):
    if preset not in PRESETS:
        raise ValueError("unknown preset")
    if dimensions not in (2, 3) or not 8 <= size <= 64:
        raise ValueError("demo grid budget exceeded")
    if genesis_genomes is not None and population is None:
        population = len(genesis_genomes)
    count = (4 if preset == "organisms" else 0) if population is None else population
    world = Ecosystem(
        PhysicsConfig(mode=PRESETS[preset], shape=(size,) * dimensions, seed=seed),
        EcosystemConfig(initial_population=count),
        genesis_genomes=genesis_genomes,
    )
    # Excitation belongs to the seeded engine initialization, so a seed replay
    # must include it as a recorded event rather than an implicit preset change.
    if preset == "light":
        world.apply_event(
            {
                "tick": 0,
                "kind": "impulse",
                "position": [size / 2] * dimensions,
                "amount": 1.0,
            }
        )
    return world


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/brain/__init__.py
========================================================================================
"""Sparse E/PV/SST/VIP microcircuit and external-reference comparison tools."""

import csv
import numpy as np
from scipy import sparse
from scipy.signal import welch
from nova.neuron import AdExPopulation


class Microcircuit:
    def __init__(self, units=200, seed=0, backend="numpy", density=0.05):
        self.neurons = AdExPopulation(units, seed, backend)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        types = self.neurons.types
        matrix = sparse.random(
            units,
            units,
            density=density,
            random_state=rng,
            data_rvs=lambda n: rng.uniform(5, 20, n),
        ).tocoo()
        keep = (matrix.row != matrix.col) & (
            (types[matrix.row] != 3) | (types[matrix.col] == 2)
        )
        rows, cols = matrix.row[keep], matrix.col[keep]
        sign = np.where(types[rows] == 0, 1.0, -1.0)
        self.weights = sparse.csr_matrix(
            (matrix.data[keep] * sign, (rows, cols)), shape=(units, units)
        )
        self.sst_weights = sparse.diags((types == 2).astype(float)) @ self.weights
        self.somatic_weights = self.weights - self.sst_weights
        self.trace = np.zeros(units)

    def step(self, drive=300.0, dt=0.1):
        self.trace *= np.exp(-dt / 5)
        basal = drive + self.somatic_weights.T @ self.trace
        apical = self.sst_weights.T @ self.trace
        spikes, state = self.neurons.step((basal, apical), dt=dt)
        self.trace += spikes
        return spikes, state

    def check_dale(self):
        for i in range(self.weights.shape[0]):
            row = self.weights.getrow(i).data
            if not (
                np.all(row >= 0) if self.neurons.types[i] == 0 else np.all(row <= 0)
            ):
                return False
        return True


def spectral_metrics(spikes, dt_ms):
    population = np.asarray(spikes, dtype=float).mean(axis=1) * 1000 / dt_ms
    f, p = welch(population, fs=1000 / dt_ms, nperseg=min(2048, len(population)))
    total = np.trapezoid(p, f)
    band = (f >= 30) & (f <= 80)
    return {
        "mean_rate_hz": float(population.mean()),
        "peak_hz": float(f[np.argmax(p)]),
        "gamma_fraction": float(np.trapezoid(p[band], f[band]) / total)
        if total
        else 0.0,
    }


def compare_reference(observed, reference_csv):
    """CSV metric,value,tolerance,source; data provenance is mandatory."""
    results = []
    with open(reference_csv, newline="") as stream:
        for row in csv.DictReader(stream):
            if not row.get("source"):
                raise ValueError("biological reference needs provenance")
            metric = row["metric"]
            expected = float(row["value"])
            tol = float(row["tolerance"])
            if tol < 0:
                raise ValueError("negative tolerance")
            actual = observed.get(metric)
            results.append(
                dict(
                    metric=metric,
                    observed=actual,
                    reference=expected,
                    tolerance=tol,
                    source=row["source"],
                    status="unrun"
                    if actual is None
                    else "passed"
                    if abs(actual - expected) <= tol
                    else "failed",
                )
            )
    return results


def load_connectome(path, nodes):
    """Edge CSV source,target,weight; preserve signed topology, no synthetic data label."""
    rows = []
    cols = []
    values = []
    with open(path, newline="") as stream:
        for e in csv.DictReader(stream):
            u, v = int(e["source"]), int(e["target"])
            if not 0 <= u < nodes or not 0 <= v < nodes:
                raise ValueError("connectome node out of range")
            rows.append(u)
            cols.append(v)
            values.append(float(e["weight"]))
    return sparse.csr_matrix((values, (rows, cols)), shape=(nodes, nodes))


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/brain/latent.py
========================================================================================
"""A stateful AdEx microcircuit that actually produces the runtime latent code."""

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
from scipy import sparse
from nova.jepa.equilibrium import EquilibriumWorldModel, batch
from . import Microcircuit


class SpikingLatentEncoder:
    """Input currents -> E/PV/SST/VIP circuit -> spike/membrane latent readout.

    Readout combines windowed firing rate and subthreshold membrane evidence.
    Fixed afferent/readout coordinates; optional three-factor STDP changes only
    existing recurrent synapses, with Dale sign restored after every update.
    This model is not trained by EqProp and has no validated biological fit.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        inputs: int,
        latent_dim: int,
        *,
        units: int = 100,
        steps: int = 100,
        dt: float = 0.1,
        seed: int = 0,
        backend: str = "numpy",
    ):
        if (
            min(inputs, latent_dim, steps) < 1
            or units < max(20, latent_dim)
            or not 0 < dt <= 1
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid spiking encoder dimensions/window")
        self.inputs, self.latent_dim, self.units, self.steps, self.dt = (
            inputs,
            latent_dim,
            units,
            steps,
            dt,
        )
        self.seed, self.backend = seed, backend
        self.circuit = Microcircuit(units, seed, backend)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 41)
        self.afferent = rng.normal(0, 1 / np.sqrt(inputs), (inputs, units))
        self.readout = np.linalg.qr(rng.normal(size=(units, latent_dim)))[0][
            :, :latent_dim
        ]
        self.pre_trace = np.zeros(units)
        self.post_trace = np.zeros(units)
        self.last_spikes = np.zeros(units, dtype=bool)
        self.last_diagnostics = {}

    def encode(self, inputs) -> np.ndarray:
        outputs = []
        for sample in batch(inputs, self.inputs):
            drive = 300 + 150 * np.tanh(sample @ self.afferent)
            counts = np.zeros(self.units)
            for _ in range(self.steps):
                spikes, _ = self.circuit.step(drive, self.dt)
                counts += spikes
                self.last_spikes = spikes
                self.pre_trace *= np.exp(-self.dt / 20)
                self.pre_trace += spikes
                self.post_trace *= np.exp(-self.dt / 25)
                self.post_trace += spikes
            rates = counts * 1000 / (self.steps * self.dt)
            evidence = 0.01 * rates + (self.circuit.neurons.v + 70) / 50
            outputs.append(np.tanh(evidence @ self.readout))
            self.last_diagnostics = {
                "mean_rate_hz": float(rates.mean()),
                "window_ms": self.steps * self.dt,
                "dale": self.circuit.check_dale(),
                "units": self.units,
            }
        return np.stack(outputs)

    def learn_local(self, modulator: float, lr: float = 0.001):
        if not np.isfinite(modulator) or lr < 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid local update")
        weights = self.circuit.weights.tocoo(copy=True)
        rows, cols = weights.row, weights.col
        eligibility = (
            self.pre_trace[rows] * self.last_spikes[cols]
            - self.last_spikes[rows] * self.post_trace[cols]
        )
        weights.data += lr * float(np.clip(modulator, -1, 1)) * eligibility
        excitatory = self.circuit.neurons.types[rows] == 0
        weights.data = np.where(
            excitatory, np.clip(weights.data, 0, 50), np.clip(weights.data, -50, 0)
        )
        self.circuit.weights = weights.tocsr()
        self._refresh_weights()

    def _refresh_weights(self):
        self.circuit.sst_weights = (
            sparse.diags((self.circuit.neurons.types == 2).astype(float))
            @ self.circuit.weights
        )
        self.circuit.somatic_weights = self.circuit.weights - self.circuit.sst_weights

    def state_dict(self) -> dict:
        w = self.circuit.weights
        return {
            "inputs": self.inputs,
            "latent_dim": self.latent_dim,
            "units": self.units,
            "steps": self.steps,
            "dt": self.dt,
            "seed": self.seed,
            "backend": self.backend,
            "afferent": self.afferent.copy(),
            "readout": self.readout.copy(),
            "trace": self.circuit.trace.copy(),
            "neurons": self.circuit.neurons.state_dict(),
            "weight_data": w.data.copy(),
            "weight_indices": w.indices.copy(),
            "weight_indptr": w.indptr.copy(),
            "pre_trace": self.pre_trace.copy(),
            "post_trace": self.post_trace.copy(),
            "last_spikes": self.last_spikes.copy(),
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state):
        obj = cls(
            **{
                key: state[key]
                for key in (
                    "inputs",
                    "latent_dim",
                    "units",
                    "steps",
                    "dt",
                    "seed",
                    "backend",
                )
            }
        )
        for key in ("afferent", "readout", "pre_trace", "post_trace", "last_spikes"):
            setattr(obj, key, np.asarray(state[key]).copy())
        obj.circuit.trace = np.asarray(state["trace"]).copy()
        obj.circuit.neurons.load_state_dict(state["neurons"])
        obj.circuit.weights = sparse.csr_matrix(
            (state["weight_data"], state["weight_indices"], state["weight_indptr"]),
            shape=(obj.units, obj.units),
        )
        obj._refresh_weights()
        if not obj.circuit.check_dale():
            raise ValueError("checkpoint violates Dale sign constraint")
        return obj


class SpikingWorldModel(EquilibriumWorldModel):
    """N1 inference supplies z; the predictive world dynamics use nonlinear EqProp."""

    kind = "adex"

    def __init__(
        self,
        observation_dim,
        latent_dim,
        action_dim,
        *,
        seed=0,
        config=None,
        spiking_units=100,
        spiking_steps=100,
        spiking_backend="numpy",
    ):
        super().__init__(
            observation_dim, latent_dim, action_dim, seed=seed, config=config
        )
        self.spiking = SpikingLatentEncoder(
            observation_dim,
            latent_dim,
            units=max(spiking_units, latent_dim, 20),
            steps=spiking_steps,
            seed=seed,
            backend=spiking_backend,
        )

    def encode(self, x):
        result = self.spiking.encode(self._normalize(x))
        self.last_diagnostics = self.spiking.last_diagnostics.copy()
        return result

    def learn_transition(
        self, x, action, future, *, lr=0.01, train_encoder=False, **kwargs
    ):
        if train_encoder:
            raise ValueError(
                "AdEx supports explicit local STDP, not rate-network EqProp encoder training"
            )
        z = self.encode(x)
        state = self.spiking.state_dict()
        try:
            target = self.encode(future)
        finally:
            self.spiking = SpikingLatentEncoder.from_state_dict(state)
        return self.learn_latent(z, action, target, lr)

    def state_dict(self):
        return {**super().state_dict(), "spiking": self.spiking.state_dict()}

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state):
        from nova.jepa.equilibrium import EquilibriumConfig, EquilibriumNetwork

        sp = state["spiking"]
        obj = cls(
            state["observation_dim"],
            state["latent_dim"],
            state["action_dim"],
            config=EquilibriumConfig(**state["config"]),
            spiking_units=sp["units"],
            spiking_steps=sp["steps"],
            spiking_backend=sp["backend"],
        )
        for name in ("context", "target", "predictor"):
            setattr(obj, name, EquilibriumNetwork.from_state_dict(state[name]))
        obj.mean = np.asarray(state["mean"]).copy()
        obj.scale = np.asarray(state["scale"]).copy()
        obj.encoder_frozen = state["encoder_frozen"]
        obj.spiking = SpikingLatentEncoder.from_state_dict(sp)
        return obj

ARQUIVO: nova/checkpoint.py

"""Versioned NPZ/JSON state codec, atomic replacement, bounded loads, SHA-256 integrity.

The digest detects accidental corruption. It is not an authentication signature;
only restore checkpoints from trusted owners. No pickle/object arrays are used.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import zipfile
import numpy as np


class CheckpointFormatError(ValueError):
    pass


def _digest(payload, arrays):
    digest = hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(
            payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False
        ).encode()
    )
    for name, array in sorted(arrays.items()):
        digest.update(name.encode())
        digest.update(array.dtype.str.encode())
        digest.update(str(array.shape).encode())
        digest.update(array.tobytes(order="C"))
    return digest.hexdigest()


def save_state(path, state):
    arrays = {}

    def encode(value):
        if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
            if value.dtype.hasobject:
                raise TypeError("object arrays are forbidden")
            key = f"a{len(arrays):06d}"
            arrays[key] = np.array(value, copy=True, order="C")
            return {"type": "array", "key": key}
        if isinstance(value, np.generic):
            value = value.item()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            if not all(isinstance(k, str) for k in value):
                raise TypeError("state keys must be strings")
            return {"type": "dict", "items": [[k, encode(v)] for k, v in value.items()]}
        if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
            return {"type": "list", "items": [encode(v) for v in value]}
        if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, bool, int, float)):
            if isinstance(value, float) and not np.isfinite(value):
                raise ValueError("nonfinite state value")
            return {"type": "value", "value": value}
        raise TypeError(f"unsupported checkpoint type: {type(value).__name__}")

    payload = encode(state)
    manifest = {
        "format": "nova-state",
        "version": 2,
        "payload": payload,
        "sha256": _digest(payload, arrays),
    }
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fd, temp = tempfile.mkstemp(
        prefix=".nova-checkpoint-", suffix=".npz", dir=path.parent
    )
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as stream:
            np.savez_compressed(
                stream,
                manifest=np.array(json.dumps(manifest, allow_nan=False)),
                **arrays,
            )
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        os.replace(temp, path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temp):
            os.unlink(temp)


def load_state(path, *, max_bytes=1024**3, max_arrays=10000):
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as archive:
        entries = archive.infolist()
        if (
            len(entries) > max_arrays + 1
            or sum(e.file_size for e in entries) > max_bytes
        ):
            raise CheckpointFormatError("checkpoint exceeds load budget")
        if len({e.filename for e in entries}) != len(entries):
            raise CheckpointFormatError("duplicate archive entry")
    with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as archive:
        if "manifest" not in archive.files:
            raise CheckpointFormatError("not a v2 state archive")
        manifest = json.loads(str(archive["manifest"]))
        if manifest.get("format") != "nova-state" or manifest.get("version") != 2:
            raise CheckpointFormatError("unsupported state format")
        arrays = {k: archive[k].copy() for k in archive.files if k != "manifest"}
        if _digest(manifest["payload"], arrays) != manifest["sha256"]:
            raise CheckpointFormatError("checkpoint integrity mismatch")

    def decode(node):
        kind = node["type"]
        if kind == "array":
            return arrays[node["key"]].copy()
        if kind == "dict":
            return {k: decode(v) for k, v in node["items"]}
        if kind == "list":
            return [decode(v) for v in node["items"]]
        if kind == "value":
            return node["value"]
        raise CheckpointFormatError("unknown state node type")

    return decode(manifest["payload"])
