"""Research workflows and consent-scoped local identity operations."""

from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import importlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys

PHASES = {
    1: "01_linear_nullspace_audit",
    2: "02_jepa_latent_world",
    3: "03_continual_split_mnist",
    4: "04_planning_latent",
    5: "05_kernel_benchmark",
    6: "06_research_ablation",
    7: "07_brain_perception",
    8: "08_cognition",
    9: "09_identity_fidelity",
    10: "10_adaptive_capacity",
    11: "11_equilibrium_world",
    12: "12_energy_planning",
}


def main(argv=None):
    argv = sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else list(argv)
    if argv and argv[0] == "cosm":
        from nova.bioma.cli import main as cosm_main

        return cosm_main(argv[1:])
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="nova", description="NOVA research workflows")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    sub.add_parser(
        "cosm", help="Biocosm fields, 3D visualization, science and pinned peers"
    )
    demo = sub.add_parser("demo")
    demo.add_argument("--ticks", type=int, default=100)
    demo.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    demo.add_argument("--checkpoint", default="checkpoints/demo.npz")
    demo.add_argument("--resume")
    demo.add_argument("--world")
    demo.add_argument("--metrics", default="reports/demo.jsonl")
    demo.add_argument("--planning", action="store_true", default=None)
    demo.add_argument(
        "--profile", choices=["baseline", "adaptive", "brain"], default="baseline"
    )
    demo.add_argument("--config", help="YAML runtime configuration")
    demo.add_argument("--world-backend", choices=["torch", "equilibrium", "adex"])
    demo.add_argument("--planner", choices=["cem", "energy"])
    demo.add_argument(
        "--memory-policy", choices=["strict", "relax", "expand", "hybrid"]
    )
    demo.add_argument(
        "--online-learning", action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction, default=None
    )
    train = sub.add_parser("train-world")
    train.add_argument("--output", default="checkpoints/world.npz")
    train.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=900)
    train.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    train.add_argument(
        "--backend", choices=["torch", "equilibrium", "adex"], default="torch"
    )
    train.add_argument("--train-encoder", action="store_true")
    bench = sub.add_parser("benchmark")
    bench.add_argument("phase", type=int, choices=PHASES)
    bench.add_argument("args", nargs=argparse.REMAINDER)
    dash = sub.add_parser("dashboard")
    dash.add_argument("--metrics", default="reports/demo.jsonl")
    dash.add_argument("--output", default="reports/dashboard.html")
    key = sub.add_parser("keygen")
    key.add_argument("--output", required=True)
    identity = sub.add_parser("identity")
    identity.add_argument(
        "action",
        choices=[
            "ingest",
            "inspect",
            "review",
            "answer",
            "timeline",
            "delete-source",
            "erase",
        ],
    )
    identity.add_argument("--directory", default="private/vault")
    identity.add_argument("--consent", default="private/consent.json")
    identity.add_argument("--subject", required=True)
    identity.add_argument("--actor", required=True)
    identity.add_argument("--key-file")
    identity.add_argument("--input")
    identity.add_argument("--predicate")
    identity.add_argument("--source-id")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.command == "train-world":
        from nova.training import train_world

        result = train_world(
            args.output,
            args.steps,
            args.seed,
            backend=args.backend,
            train_encoder=args.train_encoder,
        )
    elif args.command == "demo":
        import numpy as np
        from nova.runtime import CognitiveRuntime
        from nova.profiles import runtime_config
        from nova.environments import LinearPoint
        from nova.training import load_world

        if args.ticks < 1:
            parser.error("--ticks must be positive")
        overrides = {"seed": args.seed}
        if args.config:
            import yaml

            content = yaml.safe_load(Path(args.config).read_text())
            if not isinstance(content, dict):
                parser.error("configuration must be a mapping")
            overrides.update(content.get("runtime", content))
        for field, value in (
            ("planning", args.planning),
            ("world_backend", args.world_backend),
            ("planner_backend", args.planner),
            ("world_online_learning", args.online_learning),
        ):
            if value is not None:
                overrides[field] = value
        if args.memory_policy:
            overrides.update(adaptive_memory=True, memory_policy=args.memory_policy)
        runtime = (
            CognitiveRuntime.load(args.resume)
            if args.resume
            else CognitiveRuntime(runtime_config(args.profile, overrides))
        )
        if runtime.config.world_backend == "torch":
            import torch

            torch.set_num_threads(2)
        if runtime.config.observation_dim != 12 or runtime.config.action_dim != 2:
            parser.error(
                "the built-in LinearPoint demo requires observation_dim=12 and action_dim=2"
            )
        if args.world:
            load_world(runtime, args.world)
        env = LinearPoint(seed=args.seed)
        reward = 0.0
        if runtime.external_state:
            saved = runtime.external_state
            env.state = np.asarray(saved["state"])
            env.rng.bit_generator.state = saved["rng"]
            obs = np.asarray(saved["observation"])
            reward = saved["reward"]
        else:
            obs = env.reset()
        for _ in range(args.ticks):
            outcome = runtime.tick(obs, reward)
            obs, reward, _, _ = env.step(outcome["action"])
        runtime.external_state = {
            "state": env.state.tolist(),
            "rng": env.rng.bit_generator.state,
            "observation": obs.tolist(),
            "reward": reward,
        }
        runtime.save(args.checkpoint)
        Path(args.metrics).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        runtime.write_metrics(args.metrics)
        result = {
            "profile": "resumed" if args.resume else args.profile,
            "world_backend": runtime.config.world_backend,
            "planner_backend": runtime.config.planner_backend,
            "adaptive_memory": runtime.config.adaptive_memory,
            "ticks": runtime.tick_index,
            "checkpoint": args.checkpoint,
            "metrics": args.metrics,
            "encoder": "loaded"
            if args.world
            else "checkpoint"
            if args.resume
            else "untrained demo",
            "last": runtime.metrics[-1],
        }
    elif args.command == "benchmark":
        original = sys.argv
        sys.argv = [original[0], *args.args]
        try:
            importlib.import_module("experiments." + PHASES[args.phase]).main()
        finally:
            sys.argv = original
        result = {"phase": args.phase, "report": f"reports/fase{args.phase}.md"}
    elif args.command == "dashboard":
        from nova.observability import dashboard

        dashboard(args.metrics, args.output)
        result = {"dashboard": args.output}
    elif args.command == "keygen":
        path = Path(args.output)
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        fd = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL, 0o600)
        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as stream:
            stream.write(os.urandom(32))
        result = {
            "key_file": str(path),
            "note": "Store securely; key bytes are never printed.",
        }
    else:
        from nova.identity import IdentityStore

        key_bytes = Path(args.key_file).read_bytes() if args.key_file else None
        store = IdentityStore(
            args.directory,
            args.consent,
            args.subject,
            args.actor,
            key_bytes,
            erasure_only=args.action in ("erase", "delete-source"),
        )
        if args.action == "ingest":
            if not args.input:
                parser.error("--input is required for ingest")
            result = store.ingest_file(args.input)
        elif args.action == "answer":
            if not args.predicate:
                parser.error("--predicate is required for answer")
            result = store.answer(args.predicate)
        elif args.action == "delete-source":
            if not args.source_id:
                parser.error("--source-id is required for delete-source")
            result = store.delete_source(args.source_id)
        else:
            result = getattr(store, args.action)()
    print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, allow_nan=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Linear retention with explicit, finite subspace capacity."""

from .protected import ProtectedLinear, linear_cka

__all__ = ["ProtectedLinear", "linear_cka"]

__all__ += ["AdaptiveSubspaceLinear", "AdaptiveConfig"]

ARQUIVO: nova/continual/adaptive.py

"""Finite adaptive capacity with explicit forgetting, anchors and feature versions."""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from collections import deque
from math import ceil
import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike
from scipy.linalg import expm
from .protected import ProtectedLinear
from nova.synapse import DiffusiveCascade


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AdaptiveConfig:
    policy: str = "hybrid"
    reserve_fraction: float = 0.2
    growth_step: int = 8
    max_features: int = 128
    max_state_bytes: int = 32 * 1024 * 1024
    trace_tau: float = 200.0
    evidence_tau: float = 1000.0
    release_threshold: float = 1e-5
    eigenvalue_floor: float = 1e-6
    slow_weight: float = 8.0
    reservoirs: int = 4

    def __post_init__(self):
        if self.policy not in ("strict", "relax", "expand", "hybrid"):
            raise ValueError("unknown capacity policy")
        numbers = (
            self.trace_tau,
            self.evidence_tau,
            self.slow_weight,
            self.release_threshold,
            self.eigenvalue_floor,
        )
        if (
            not all(np.isfinite(n) and n >= 0 for n in numbers)
            or min(self.trace_tau, self.evidence_tau) <= 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid time constants or scores")
        if (
            not 0 <= self.reserve_fraction < 1
            or min(
                self.growth_step,
                self.max_features,
                self.max_state_bytes,
                self.reservoirs,
            )
            < 1
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid capacity budget")


def basis(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    if not matrix.shape[1]:
        return np.empty((len(matrix), 0))
    u, s, _ = np.linalg.svd(matrix, full_matrices=False)
    rank = int(np.count_nonzero(s > 1e-12 * s[0])) if len(s) and s[0] > 0 else 0
    return u[:, :rank]


class AdaptiveSubspaceLinear(ProtectedLinear):
    """Project updates into the current nullspace; explicitly release or grow it.

    Decaying eigenvalues alone never changes U U.T. Capacity is recovered only
    by dropping directions (which permits forgetting), or by adding coordinates.
    A separate orthonormal anchor span is never released. Slow diffusion traces
    rank directions by usage; this is an engineering policy, not a retention law.
    Nonlinear feature blocks carry an immutable version. Old versioned inputs
    have zeros in newer blocks, so their coordinate mapping stays unchanged.
    No guarantee applies to re-encoding old data with a newer feature version.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        *,
        seed: int = 0,
        config: AdaptiveConfig | None = None,
    ):
        self.config = config or AdaptiveConfig(max_features=max(128, in_features))
        if in_features > self.config.max_features:
            raise ValueError("initial width exceeds max_features")
        self.base_features = in_features
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 19)
        self.feature_blocks: list[dict] = []
        self.anchor_U = np.empty((in_features, 0))
        self.events = deque(maxlen=128)
        self.released_total = 0
        self.expansions = 0
        self.clock = 0.0
        super().__init__(in_features, out_features, seed=seed)
        self.traces = DiffusiveCascade(in_features, self.config.reservoirs)
        self._age_dt = None
        self._age_transition = None
        if self.estimated_state_bytes(in_features) > self.config.max_state_bytes:
            raise ValueError("initial state exceeds capacity budget")

    @property
    def feature_version(self) -> int:
        return len(self.feature_blocks)

    def estimated_state_bytes(self, width: int) -> int:
        # Conservative reservation for U and anchor_U at full rank, weights,
        # residual/update, cascade, random features, and singular values.
        return int(
            16 * width * width
            + width * self.out_features * 17
            + 8 * width * self.config.reservoirs
            + 8 * self.base_features * max(0, width - self.base_features)
            + 24 * width
        )

    def encode(
        self, x: ArrayLike, version: int | ArrayLike | None = None
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        if x.ndim != 2 or x.shape[1] != self.base_features or not np.isfinite(x).all():
            raise ValueError("expected finite raw features [batch, base_features]")
        v = np.broadcast_to(
            self.feature_version if version is None else np.asarray(version), (len(x),)
        )
        if (
            np.any(v < 0)
            or np.any(v > self.feature_version)
            or np.any(v != np.floor(v))
        ):
            raise ValueError("unknown feature version")
        parts = [x]
        for index, block in enumerate(self.feature_blocks, 1):
            parts.append(
                np.tanh(x @ block["projection"] + block["bias"]) * (v >= index)[:, None]
            )
        return np.concatenate(parts, axis=1)

    def _replace_basis(self, new: np.ndarray, values: np.ndarray | None = None):
        old = self.U
        overlap = (old.T @ new) ** 2
        state = np.zeros_like(self.traces.state)
        state[: new.shape[1]] = overlap.T @ self.traces.state[: old.shape[1]]
        if values is None:
            values = np.sqrt(overlap.T @ (self.singular_values**2))
        self.U = new
        self.singular_values = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
        self.traces.state = state

    def consolidate(self, x: ArrayLike, *, anchor: bool = False) -> None:
        x = self._inputs(x)
        if not len(x):
            raise ValueError("empty calibration batch")
        if anchor:
            anchors = basis(np.concatenate((self.anchor_U, x.T), axis=1))
            if anchors.shape[1] > self.rank_max:
                raise ValueError("anchor rank exceeds configured rank")
            self.anchor_U = anchors
        old = self.U.copy()
        old_values = self.singular_values.copy()
        u, s, _ = np.linalg.svd(
            np.concatenate((old * old_values, x.T), axis=1), full_matrices=False
        )
        rank = int(np.count_nonzero(s > 1e-12 * s[0])) if s[0] > 0 else 0
        new = u[:, :rank]
        values = s[:rank]
        if self.anchor_U.shape[1]:
            residual = new - self.anchor_U @ (self.anchor_U.T @ new)
            residual -= self.anchor_U @ (self.anchor_U.T @ residual)
            if residual.shape[1]:
                ru, rs, _ = np.linalg.svd(residual, full_matrices=False)
                residual = ru[:, rs > 1e-10]
            new = np.concatenate((self.anchor_U, residual), axis=1)
            # Diagonal evidence transported into the anchor-aligned frame. This
            # is a priority approximation, not the full rotated covariance.
            values = np.sqrt(((u.T @ new) ** 2).T @ (s * s))
            rank = new.shape[1]
        self._replace_basis(new, values)
        activation = np.mean((x @ self.U) ** 2, axis=0)
        self.traces.state[:rank, 0] += activation
        self.events.append(
            {
                "time": self.clock,
                "event": "consolidate",
                "rank": rank,
                "anchor_rank": self.anchor_U.shape[1],
            }
        )
        # Release/growth are separate explicit operations, never hidden in SVD.

    def observe(self, x: ArrayLike):
        x = self._inputs(x)
        if len(x) and self.U.shape[1]:
            self.traces.state[: self.U.shape[1], 0] += np.mean(
                (x @ self.U) ** 2, axis=0
            )

    def age(self, dt: float = 1.0, *, consolidation: float = 0.0):
        if (
            not np.isfinite(dt)
            or dt < 0
            or not np.isfinite(consolidation)
            or not 0 <= consolidation <= 1
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid aging interval/consolidation")
        self.clock += dt
        # Exact homogeneous integration avoids an unstable large Euler step.
        if self._age_dt != dt:
            self._age_transition = expm(dt * self.traces.matrix).T
            self._age_dt = dt
        self.traces.state = self.traces.state @ self._age_transition
        self.traces.state *= np.exp(-dt / self.config.trace_tau)
        self.singular_values *= np.exp(-dt / (2 * self.config.evidence_tau))
        self.traces.consolidate(consolidation)
        if self.config.policy in ("relax", "hybrid") and self.U.shape[1]:
            score = self._priority(self.U)
            stale = (score < self.config.release_threshold) & (
                self.singular_values**2 < self.config.eigenvalue_floor
            )
            if stale.any():
                self.release(
                    int(stale.sum()), max_priority=self.config.release_threshold
                )

    def _priority(self, directions: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        overlap = (self.U.T @ directions) ** 2
        rank = self.U.shape[1]
        trace = self.traces.state[:rank]
        slow = (
            np.maximum(trace[:, 1:], 0) @ self.traces.capacity[1:]
            if trace.shape[1] > 1
            else np.zeros(rank)
        )
        evidence = self.singular_values**2
        score = (
            np.maximum(trace[:, 0], 0)
            + self.config.slow_weight * slow
            + evidence / (1 + evidence)
        )
        return overlap.T @ score

    def release(self, count: int, *, max_priority: float | None = None) -> int:
        if count < 0:
            raise ValueError("release count must be nonnegative")
        if self.config.policy not in ("relax", "hybrid") or count == 0:
            return 0
        rank = self.U.shape[1]
        residual = self.U - self.anchor_U @ (self.anchor_U.T @ self.U)
        unanchored = self.U.copy() if not self.anchor_U.shape[1] else basis(residual)
        # Reorthogonalize after numerical residual cancellation. Singular values
        # near machine precision relative to U are not new directions.
        if self.anchor_U.shape[1]:
            residual -= self.anchor_U @ (self.anchor_U.T @ residual)
            u, s, _ = np.linalg.svd(residual, full_matrices=False)
            unanchored = u[:, s > 1e-10]
        priority = self._priority(unanchored)
        eligible = np.argsort(priority, kind="stable")
        if max_priority is not None:
            eligible = eligible[priority[eligible] <= max_priority]
        removed = eligible[:count]
        keep = np.ones(unanchored.shape[1], dtype=bool)
        keep[removed] = False
        new = np.concatenate((self.anchor_U, unanchored[:, keep]), axis=1)
        self._replace_basis(new)
        actual = max(0, rank - new.shape[1])
        self.released_total += actual
        self.events.append(
            {
                "time": self.clock,
                "event": "release",
                "directions": actual,
                "retention": "released span is unprotected",
            }
        )
        return actual

    def expand(self, extra: int, *, seed: int | None = None) -> int:
        """Adaptive expansion always honors budgets, feature stamps and anchors."""
        return self.recruit(extra, seed=seed)

    def recruit(self, extra: int | None = None, *, seed: int | None = None) -> int:
        if self.config.policy not in ("expand", "hybrid"):
            return 0
        extra = min(
            self.config.growth_step if extra is None else extra,
            self.config.max_features - self.in_features,
        )
        while (
            extra > 0
            and self.estimated_state_bytes(self.in_features + extra)
            > self.config.max_state_bytes
        ):
            extra -= 1
        if extra <= 0:
            return 0
        if not isinstance(extra, (int, np.integer)):
            raise ValueError("extra must be an integer")
        old_width = self.in_features
        rng = self.rng if seed is None else np.random.default_rng(seed)
        projection = rng.normal(
            0, 1 / np.sqrt(self.base_features), (self.base_features, extra)
        )
        bias = rng.normal(0, 0.1, extra)
        super().expand(extra, seed=int(rng.integers(2**31)))
        # New coordinates initially contribute exactly zero, despite ternary base.
        self.dW[old_width:] = -self.W_base[old_width:].astype(float)
        self.anchor_U = np.pad(self.anchor_U, ((0, extra), (0, 0)))
        old_trace = self.traces.state
        self.traces = DiffusiveCascade(self.in_features, self.config.reservoirs)
        self.traces.state[:old_width] = old_trace
        self.feature_blocks.append({"projection": projection, "bias": bias})
        self.expansions += 1
        self.events.append(
            {
                "time": self.clock,
                "event": "recruit",
                "width": self.in_features,
                "version": self.feature_version,
            }
        )
        return extra

    def manage_capacity(self) -> dict:
        reserve = (
            max(1, ceil(self.config.reserve_fraction * self.in_features))
            if self.config.reserve_fraction
            else 0
        )
        if self.free_dims < reserve:
            self.recruit(max(self.config.growth_step, reserve - self.free_dims))
            reserve = ceil(self.config.reserve_fraction * self.in_features)
        if self.free_dims < reserve:
            self.release(reserve - self.free_dims)
        return self.capacity_metrics()

    def capacity_metrics(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "width": self.in_features,
            "rank": self.U.shape[1],
            "free_dims": self.free_dims,
            "anchor_rank": self.anchor_U.shape[1],
            "feature_version": self.feature_version,
            "released_total": self.released_total,
            "expansions": self.expansions,
            "state_budget_bytes": self.estimated_state_bytes(self.in_features),
            "capacity_exhausted": self.free_dims == 0,
        }

    def state_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "config": asdict(self.config),
            "base_features": self.base_features,
            "in_features": self.in_features,
            "out_features": self.out_features,
            "W_base": self.W_base.copy(),
            "dW": self.dW.copy(),
            "U": self.U.copy(),
            "singular_values": self.singular_values.copy(),
            "last_update": self.last_update.copy(),
            "anchor_U": self.anchor_U.copy(),
            "trace_state": self.traces.state.copy(),
            "blocks": self.feature_blocks,
            "rng": self.rng.bit_generator.state,
            "clock": self.clock,
            "events": list(self.events),
            "released_total": self.released_total,
            "expansions": self.expansions,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state: dict) -> "AdaptiveSubspaceLinear":
        obj = cls(
            state["base_features"],
            state["out_features"],
            config=AdaptiveConfig(**state["config"]),
        )
        obj.in_features = state["in_features"]
        obj.rank_max = obj.in_features
        for name in ("W_base", "dW", "U", "singular_values", "last_update", "anchor_U"):
            setattr(obj, name, np.asarray(state[name]).copy())
        obj.W_base.flags.writeable = False
        obj.traces = DiffusiveCascade(obj.in_features, obj.config.reservoirs)
        obj.traces.state = np.asarray(state["trace_state"]).copy()
        obj.feature_blocks = [
            {k: np.asarray(v).copy() for k, v in block.items()}
            for block in state["blocks"]
        ]
        obj.rng.bit_generator.state = state["rng"]
        obj.clock = state["clock"]
        obj.events = deque(state["events"], maxlen=128)
        obj.released_total = state["released_total"]
        obj.expansions = state["expansions"]
        return obj


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/continual/deep.py
========================================================================================
"""Continual-learning baselines and explicit representation-drift measurement."""

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F
from .protected import ProtectedLinear


class MLP(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, inputs=784, hidden=128, outputs=10):
        super().__init__()
        self.first = nn.Linear(inputs, hidden)
        self.last = nn.Linear(hidden, outputs)

    def features(self, x):
        return F.relu(self.first(x))

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.last(self.features(x))


class OnlineEWC:
    """Diagonal empirical Fisher from per-example gradients, accumulated per task."""

    def __init__(self, model, strength=20.0):
        self.strength = strength
        self.anchor = {n: p.detach().clone() for n, p in model.named_parameters()}
        self.fisher = {n: torch.zeros_like(p) for n, p in model.named_parameters()}

    def penalty(self, model):
        return (
            self.strength
            * 0.5
            * sum(
                (self.fisher[n] * (p - self.anchor[n]).square()).sum()
                for n, p in model.named_parameters()
            )
        )

    def consolidate(self, model, x, y):
        estimate = {n: torch.zeros_like(p) for n, p in model.named_parameters()}
        for xi, yi in zip(x, y):
            model.zero_grad()
            F.cross_entropy(model(xi[None]), yi[None]).backward()
            for n, p in model.named_parameters():
                estimate[n] += p.grad.detach().square() / len(x)
        for n, p in model.named_parameters():
            self.fisher[n] += estimate[n]
            self.anchor[n] = p.detach().clone()
        model.zero_grad()


class LayerwiseGPM:
    """Gradient projection on actual affine-layer inputs (bias coordinate included).

    Uses 95% activation energy by default; this is approximate retention and
    explicitly differs from the full-span float64 ProtectedLinear guarantee.
    """

    def __init__(self, model, energy=0.95):
        self.bases = [None, None]
        self.energy = energy

    @torch.no_grad()
    def consolidate(self, model, x):
        for i, activation in enumerate((x, model.features(x))):
            a = torch.cat((activation, torch.ones(len(x), 1)), 1).double().T
            old = self.bases[i]
            if old is not None:
                a -= old @ (old.T @ a)
            u, s, _ = torch.linalg.svd(a, full_matrices=False)
            if float(s.sum()) == 0:
                continue
            rank = (
                int(
                    torch.searchsorted(
                        s.square().cumsum(0) / s.square().sum(), self.energy
                    )
                )
                + 1
            )
            new = u[:, :rank]
            basis = new if old is None else torch.cat((old, new), 1)
            self.bases[i] = torch.linalg.qr(basis, mode="reduced")[0][:, : a.shape[0]]

    @torch.no_grad()
    def project(self, model):
        for layer, basis in zip((model.first, model.last), self.bases):
            if basis is None:
                continue
            grad = torch.cat((layer.weight.grad, layer.bias.grad[:, None]), 1).double()
            grad -= (grad @ basis) @ basis.T
            layer.weight.grad.copy_(grad[:, :-1])
            layer.bias.grad.copy_(grad[:, -1])


class FrozenProtectedClassifier:
    """Freeze the learned lower layer, transfer its head into continuous dW."""

    def __init__(self, model: MLP):
        self.encoder = model.first.requires_grad_(False)
        self.head = ProtectedLinear(model.last.in_features + 1, model.last.out_features)
        weights = (
            torch.cat((model.last.weight.T, model.last.bias[None]), 0).detach().numpy()
        )
        self.head.dW[:] = weights - self.head.W_base

    def features(self, x):
        with torch.no_grad():
            z = F.relu(self.encoder(x)).numpy().astype("float64")
        return np.c_[z, np.ones(len(z))]

    def logits(self, x):
        return self.head(self.features(x))

    def train_batch(self, x, y, lr=0.08):
        z = self.features(x)
        logits = self.head(z)
        exp = np.exp(logits - logits.max(1, keepdims=True))
        prob = exp / exp.sum(1, keepdims=True)
        prob[np.arange(len(y)), y.numpy()] -= 1
        grad = z.T @ prob / len(z)
        grad -= self.head.U @ (self.head.U.T @ grad)
        self.head.last_update = -lr * grad
        self.head.dW += self.head.last_update


def continual_metrics(accuracy, initial, isolated):
    """Rows = after task, columns = evaluation task; no task oracle at inference."""
    a = np.asarray(accuracy)
    n = len(a)
    return dict(
        acc=float(a[-1].mean()),
        fm=float(np.mean([a[j:-1, j].max() - a[-1, j] for j in range(n - 1)])),
        fwt=float(np.mean([a[j - 1, j] - initial[j] for j in range(1, n)])),
        plasticity=float(np.mean(np.diag(a) - isolated)),
    )


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/continual/protected.py
========================================================================================
"""Float64 updates in the orthogonal complement of calibrated input directions."""

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike, NDArray


class ProtectedLinear:
    """f(X) = X(W_base + dW); only dW learns, via g - U(U.T g).

    Protection concerns changes since consolidation, not U.T @ total dW = 0.
    Exact retention requires stable input coordinates and an untruncated basis.
    The covariance square-root update is a thin, incremental left-SVD update:
    SVD([U*S, X.T]), retaining singular values for subsequent batches.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        *,
        seed: int = 0,
        rank_max: int | None = None,
        energy_threshold: float | None = None,
    ):
        if min(in_features, out_features) < 1:
            raise ValueError("feature dimensions must be positive")
        self.in_features, self.out_features = in_features, out_features
        self.rank_max = in_features if rank_max is None else rank_max
        if not 0 <= self.rank_max <= in_features:
            raise ValueError("rank_max must be in [0, in_features]")
        if energy_threshold is not None and not 0 < energy_threshold <= 1:
            raise ValueError("energy_threshold must be in (0, 1]")
        self.energy_threshold = energy_threshold
        self.W_base = np.random.default_rng(seed).integers(
            -1, 2, (in_features, out_features), dtype=np.int8
        )
        self.W_base.flags.writeable = False
        self.dW = np.zeros((in_features, out_features), dtype=np.float64)
        self.U = np.empty((in_features, 0), dtype=np.float64)
        self.singular_values = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
        self.last_update = np.zeros_like(self.dW)

    def _inputs(self, x: ArrayLike) -> NDArray[np.float64]:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        if x.ndim != 2 or x.shape[1] != self.in_features or not np.isfinite(x).all():
            raise ValueError("expected finite [batch, in_features] inputs")
        return x

    def forward(self, x: ArrayLike) -> NDArray[np.float64]:
        x = self._inputs(x)
        return x @ self.W_base + x @ self.dW

    __call__ = forward

    def forward_addition_only(self, x: ArrayLike) -> NDArray[np.float64]:
        """Base uses gather-add; the continuous residual still uses dense matmul."""
        x = self._inputs(x)
        base = np.stack(
            [
                x[:, self.W_base[:, j] == 1].sum(1)
                - x[:, self.W_base[:, j] == -1].sum(1)
                for j in range(self.out_features)
            ],
            axis=1,
        )
        return base + x @ self.dW

    def consolidate(self, x: ArrayLike) -> None:
        x = self._inputs(x)
        if not len(x):
            raise ValueError("empty calibration batch")
        u, s, _ = np.linalg.svd(
            np.concatenate((self.U * self.singular_values, x.T), axis=1),
            full_matrices=False,
        )
        rank = int(np.count_nonzero(s > 1e-12 * s[0])) if s[0] > 0 else 0
        if self.energy_threshold is not None and rank:
            rank = min(
                rank,
                int(
                    np.searchsorted(
                        np.cumsum(s * s) / np.sum(s * s), self.energy_threshold
                    )
                )
                + 1,
            )
        rank = min(rank, self.rank_max)
        self.U, self.singular_values = u[:, :rank], s[:rank]

    def step(self, x: ArrayLike, y: ArrayLike, lr: float = 0.01) -> float:
        x = self._inputs(x)
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
        if (
            not len(x)
            or y.shape != (len(x), self.out_features)
            or not np.isfinite(y).all()
            or lr < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid targets, batch, or learning rate")
        error = self(x) - y
        grad = x.T @ error / len(x)
        grad -= self.U @ (self.U.T @ grad)
        self.last_update = -lr * grad
        self.dW += self.last_update
        return float(np.mean(error**2))

    @property
    def free_dims(self) -> int:
        return self.in_features - self.U.shape[1]

    @property
    def eps_ortho(self) -> float:
        """Requested total-residual diagnostic; can be nonzero after consolidation."""
        return float(np.linalg.norm(self.U.T @ self.dW))

    @property
    def eps_update(self) -> float:
        return float(np.linalg.norm(self.U.T @ self.last_update))

    def retention_error(self, x: ArrayLike, reference: ArrayLike) -> float:
        return float(np.max(np.abs(self(x) - reference)))

    def expand(self, extra: int, *, seed: int = 0) -> None:
        """Recruit input coordinates; old samples must be padded with zeros."""
        if extra < 1:
            raise ValueError("extra must be positive")
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.W_base = np.vstack(
            (
                self.W_base,
                rng.integers(-1, 2, (extra, self.out_features), dtype=np.int8),
            )
        )
        self.W_base.flags.writeable = False
        self.dW = np.pad(self.dW, ((0, extra), (0, 0)))
        self.last_update = np.zeros_like(self.dW)
        self.U = np.pad(self.U, ((0, extra), (0, 0)))
        self.in_features += extra
        self.rank_max += extra


def linear_cka(x: ArrayLike, y: ArrayLike) -> float:
    """Centered linear CKA = ||X.T Y||² / (||X.T X|| ||Y.T Y||)."""
    x, y = np.asarray(x, dtype=float), np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    x, y = x - x.mean(0), y - y.mean(0)
    denominator = np.linalg.norm(x.T @ x) * np.linalg.norm(y.T @ y)
    return float(np.linalg.norm(x.T @ y) ** 2 / denominator) if denominator else 0.0

"""Native kernels with explicit availability; no silent performance substitution."""

import numpy as np

try:
    import _native
except ImportError:
    _native = None


def available():
    return _native is not None


def require_native():
    if _native is None:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Build nova-core with maturin; see README native installation."
        )
    return _native


class TernaryLinear:
    def __init__(self, weights):
        weights = np.asarray(weights)
        if weights.ndim != 2 or not np.isin(weights, [-1, 0, 1]).all():
            raise ValueError("expected ternary matrix")
        self.shape = weights.shape
        self.block = require_native().TernaryBlock(
            weights.astype(np.int8).ravel().tolist(), *self.shape
        )

    @property
    def nbytes(self):
        return self.block.nbytes

    def __call__(self, x):
        return self.block.matmul(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64))


def fast_hadamard_transform(x, normalize=True):
    return np.asarray(
        require_native().fast_hadamard_transform(
            np.asarray(x, dtype=float).tolist(), normalize
        )
    )


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/environments.py
========================================================================================
"""Seeded virtual bodies with independent observation noise and distractors."""

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np


class LinearPoint:
    def __init__(self, seed=0, distractors=8, noise=0.01):
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.distractors, self.noise = distractors, noise
        self.state = np.zeros(4)

    def observe(self):
        return np.r_[
            self.state + self.rng.normal(0, self.noise, 4),
            self.rng.normal(size=self.distractors),
        ]

    def reset(self):
        self.state = self.rng.normal(0, 0.5, 4)
        return self.observe()

    def step(self, action):
        action = np.clip(np.asarray(action), -1, 1)
        self.state[2:] = 0.9 * self.state[2:] + 0.1 * action
        self.state[:2] += 0.1 * self.state[2:]
        return self.observe(), -float(self.state[:2] @ self.state[:2]), False, {}


class Pendulum:
    """theta'' = -g/l sin(theta) - damping*theta' + torque; semi-implicit Euler."""

    def __init__(self, seed=0, dt=0.02, noise=0.01):
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.dt, self.noise = dt, noise
        self.state = np.zeros(2)

    def observe(self):
        t, v = self.state
        return np.array([np.cos(t), np.sin(t), v]) + self.rng.normal(0, self.noise, 3)

    def reset(self):
        self.state = self.rng.uniform(-1, 1, 2)
        return self.observe()

    def step(self, action):
        t, v = self.state
        v += self.dt * (
            -9.81 * np.sin(t)
            - 0.1 * v
            + float(np.clip(np.asarray(action).item(), -2, 2))
        )
        self.state = np.array([t + self.dt * v, v])
        return self.observe(), -float(self.state @ self.state), False, {}


class GridWorld:
    ACTIONS = np.array([[0, 1], [0, -1], [1, 0], [-1, 0]])

    def __init__(self, size=7, seed=0, distractors=4):
        self.size, self.distractors = size, distractors
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.position = np.zeros(2, dtype=int)
        self.goal = np.array([size - 1, size - 1])

    def observe(self):
        return np.r_[
            self.position / (self.size - 1),
            self.goal / (self.size - 1),
            self.rng.normal(size=self.distractors),
        ]

    def reset(self):
        self.position[:] = 0
        return self.observe()

    def step(self, action):
        self.position = np.clip(
            self.position + self.ACTIONS[int(action)], 0, self.size - 1
        )
        done = bool(np.array_equal(self.position, self.goal))
        return self.observe(), 1.0 if done else -0.05, done, {}


def point_transitions(n=4096, seed=0, distractors=8):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    state = rng.normal(size=(n, 4)).astype("float32")
    action = rng.uniform(-1, 1, (n, 2)).astype("float32")
    future = state.copy()
    future[:, 2:] = 0.9 * state[:, 2:] + 0.1 * action
    future[:, :2] += 0.1 * future[:, 2:]
    x = np.c_[state, rng.normal(size=(n, distractors))].astype("float32")
    y = np.c_[future, rng.normal(size=(n, distractors))].astype("float32")
    return x, action, y, state


class PointMass3D:
    """Minimal 3D virtual body with walls, gravity and bounded thrust.

    A research control environment, not a rendered graphics engine. Observation
    contains position, velocity, relative goal and independent sensor distractors.
    """

    def __init__(self, seed=0, dt=0.05, extent=3.0, distractors=4):
        if dt <= 0 or extent <= 0:
            raise ValueError("positive dt and extent required")
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.dt = dt
        self.extent = extent
        self.distractors = distractors
        self.state = np.zeros(6)
        self.goal = np.array([1.0, 1.0, 1.0])

    def observe(self):
        return np.r_[
            self.state,
            self.goal - self.state[:3],
            self.rng.normal(0, 0.05, self.distractors),
        ]

    def reset(self):
        self.state = np.r_[self.rng.uniform(-0.5, 0.5, 3), np.zeros(3)]
        return self.observe()

    def step(self, action):
        action = np.asarray(action, dtype=float)
        if action.shape != (3,) or not np.isfinite(action).all():
            raise ValueError("three finite actuator values required")
        acceleration = (
            3 * np.clip(action, -1, 1)
            - 0.2 * self.state[3:]
            + np.array([0.0, 0.0, -1.0])
        )
        self.state[3:] += self.dt * acceleration
        self.state[:3] += self.dt * self.state[3:]
        collided = np.abs(self.state[:3]) > self.extent
        self.state[:3] = np.clip(self.state[:3], -self.extent, self.extent)
        self.state[3:][collided] *= -0.5
        distance = float(np.linalg.norm(self.goal - self.state[:3]))
        done = distance < 0.1 and np.linalg.norm(self.state[3:]) < 0.2
        return (
            self.observe(),
            -distance - 0.01 * float(action @ action),
            bool(done),
            {"collision": bool(collided.any())},
        )


class DelayCompensatedController:
    """PD control with a constant-velocity forward prediction over actuator delay."""

    def __init__(self, kp=1.0, kd=0.5, delay=0.1):
        if delay < 0:
            raise ValueError("delay must be nonnegative")
        self.kp, self.kd, self.delay = kp, kd, delay

    def __call__(self, position, velocity, goal):
        prediction = np.asarray(position) + self.delay * np.asarray(velocity)
        return np.clip(
            self.kp * (np.asarray(goal) - prediction) - self.kd * np.asarray(velocity),
            -1,
            1,
        )

"""Local optional normalization adapters; require consent before reading media."""

from pathlib import Path


def transcribe(store, path, model_directory, source):
    store._check()
    if not Path(model_directory).is_dir():
        raise ValueError("provide a locally installed Whisper model directory")
    from faster_whisper import WhisperModel

    model = WhisperModel(
        str(model_directory), device="cpu", compute_type="int8", local_files_only=True
    )
    segments, _ = model.transcribe(str(path))
    content = " ".join(segment.text for segment in segments)
    return store.ingest({**source, "kind": "transcript", "content": content})


def ingest_image(store, path, source):
    store._check()
    from PIL import Image
    import pytesseract

    with Image.open(path) as image:
        content = pytesseract.image_to_string(image)
    return store.ingest({**source, "kind": "document", "content": content})


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/identity/store.py
========================================================================================
"""Local identity vault with AES-GCM, provenance, review and source-level erasure.

Consent is an explicit local authorization record, not proof of legal authority.
Keys belong in the caller's secret manager. This local API has no network listener.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from collections import Counter
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import base64
import fcntl
import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import tempfile
from typing import Any
import numpy as np
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.ciphers.aead import AESGCM
from nova.continual import ProtectedLinear
from nova.values import OutputPolicy, SIMULATION_LABEL


class ConsentError(PermissionError):
    pass


def timestamp(value):
    result = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    if result.tzinfo is None:
        raise ValueError("timestamps require timezone")
    return result.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def atomic_write(path: Path, data: bytes):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fd, temp = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".nova-", dir=path.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as stream:
            stream.write(data)
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        os.chmod(temp, 0o600)
        os.replace(temp, path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temp):
            os.unlink(temp)


class ConsentRegistry:
    def __init__(self, path):
        self.path = Path(path)
        if self.path.name != "consent.json":
            raise ValueError("consent record must be named consent.json")

    def check(self, subject, actor, purpose="memory", allow_revoked=False):
        if not self.path.is_file():
            raise ConsentError("consent.json is required before processing data")
        data = json.loads(self.path.read_text())
        if data.get("subject_id") != subject or actor not in data.get(
            "authorized_principals", []
        ):
            raise ConsentError("principal or subject not authorized")
        if not data.get("granted") or not data.get("authority"):
            raise ConsentError("explicit consent and authority required")
        if not allow_revoked:
            if data.get("revoked") or purpose not in data.get("purposes", []):
                raise ConsentError("consent revoked or purpose not authorized")
            if timestamp(data["granted_at"]) > datetime.now(timezone.utc):
                raise ConsentError("consent is not yet valid")
            if data.get("expires_at") and timestamp(data["expires_at"]) <= datetime.now(
                timezone.utc
            ):
                raise ConsentError("consent expired")
        return data


def embedding(text, dimension=32):
    """Deterministic hashed bag of tokens; a retrieval baseline, not a language model."""
    result = np.zeros(dimension)
    for token in re.findall(r"\w+", text.casefold()):
        digest = hashlib.blake2b(token.encode(), digest_size=8).digest()
        index = int.from_bytes(digest[:4], "little") % dimension
        result[index] += 1 if digest[4] & 1 else -1
    return result / max(np.linalg.norm(result), 1.0)


class IdentityStore:
    ALLOWED_KINDS = frozenset(
        {
            "message",
            "diary",
            "transcript",
            "photo_caption",
            "calendar",
            "document",
            "interview",
        }
    )

    def __init__(
        self,
        directory,
        consent_path,
        subject_id,
        actor,
        key: bytes | None = None,
        *,
        erasure_only: bool = False,
    ):
        self.directory = Path(directory)
        self.consent = ConsentRegistry(consent_path)
        self.subject_id = subject_id
        self.actor = actor
        self.erasure_only = erasure_only
        self.consent.check(subject_id, actor, allow_revoked=erasure_only)
        if key is None:
            value = os.environ.get("NOVA_IDENTITY_KEY")
            if not value:
                raise ValueError(
                    "NOVA_IDENTITY_KEY must contain a base64 encoded 32-byte key"
                )
            key = base64.b64decode(value, validate=True)
        if len(key) != 32:
            raise ValueError("AES-256 requires a 32-byte key")
        self._key = hmac.digest(key, ("nova-profile:" + subject_id).encode(), "sha256")
        self.token = hashlib.sha256(subject_id.encode()).hexdigest()
        self.directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        os.chmod(self.directory, 0o700)
        self.path = self.directory / (self.token + ".enc")
        self.lock_path = self.directory / (self.token + ".lock")

    def _check(self, purpose="memory", allow_revoked=False):
        if self.erasure_only and not allow_revoked:
            raise ConsentError("this handle permits erasure only")
        return self.consent.check(self.subject_id, self.actor, purpose, allow_revoked)

    @contextmanager
    def _locked(self):
        fd = os.open(self.lock_path, os.O_CREAT | os.O_RDWR, 0o600)
        with os.fdopen(fd, "a+b") as stream:
            fcntl.flock(stream, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
            try:
                yield
            finally:
                fcntl.flock(stream, fcntl.LOCK_UN)

    def _audit(self, action):
        # No names, content, fact values or keys in access logs.
        row = {
            "profile": self.token,
            "actor": hashlib.sha256(self.actor.encode()).hexdigest(),
            "action": action,
            "at": now(),
        }
        fd = os.open(
            self.directory / "access.jsonl",
            os.O_APPEND | os.O_CREAT | os.O_WRONLY,
            0o600,
        )
        with os.fdopen(fd, "a") as stream:
            stream.write(json.dumps(row) + "\n")

    def _read(self):
        if not self.path.exists():
            return {"version": 1, "records": [], "reviewed": False, "derived": {}}
        blob = self.path.read_bytes()
        data = AESGCM(self._key).decrypt(blob[:12], blob[12:], self.token.encode())
        result = json.loads(data)
        if result.get("version") != 1:
            raise ValueError("unsupported vault version")
        return result

    def _write(self, data):
        nonce = os.urandom(12)
        raw = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False).encode()
        atomic_write(
            self.path,
            nonce + AESGCM(self._key).encrypt(nonce, raw, self.token.encode()),
        )

    def _minimize(self, text, participants):
        text = str(text)
        for name in sorted(set(participants), key=len, reverse=True):
            if name and name != self.subject_id:
                alias = hmac.new(self._key, name.encode(), hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()[
                    :10
                ]
                text = re.sub(
                    re.escape(name), f"[terceiro:{alias}]", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE
                )
        text = re.sub(r"[\w.+-]+@[\w.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}", "[email removido]", text)
        text = re.sub(r"(?<!\w)\+?\d[\d ()-]{7,}\d(?!\w)", "[telefone removido]", text)
        return text

    def _derive(self, records):
        facts = []
        events = []
        words = Counter()
        sentence_lengths = []
        values = []
        model = ProtectedLinear(32, 32, seed=0)
        model.dW[:] = -model.W_base.astype(float)
        for record in sorted(records, key=lambda r: r["id"]):
            source = record["id"]
            content = record["content"]
            words.update(re.findall(r"\w+", content.casefold()))
            sentence_lengths.extend(
                len(s.split()) for s in re.split(r"[.!?]+", content) if s.strip()
            )
            for fact in record["facts"]:
                facts.append(
                    {
                        **fact,
                        "subject": self.subject_id,
                        "source": source,
                        "timestamp": record["timestamp"],
                    }
                )
            for event in record["events"]:
                events.append({**event, "source": source})
            for value in record["values"]:
                values.append({**value, "source": source})
            x = embedding(content)[None]
            y = embedding(" ".join(f["value"] for f in record["facts"]))[None]
            for _ in range(20):
                model.step(x, y, 0.1)
            model.consolidate(x)
        style_allowed = "style" in json.loads(self.consent.path.read_text()).get(
            "purposes", []
        )
        return {
            "facts": facts,
            "events": sorted(events, key=lambda e: timestamp(e["timestamp"])),
            "style": {
                "common_words": words.most_common(20),
                "mean_sentence_words": float(np.mean(sentence_lengths))
                if sentence_lengths
                else 0.0,
            }
            if style_allowed
            else {},
            "values": values,
            "semantic_residual": model.dW.tolist(),
            "semantic_basis": model.U.tolist(),
            "free_dims": model.free_dims,
        }

    def ingest(self, source: dict[str, Any]):
        consent = self._check()
        if source.get("subject_id") not in [
            self.subject_id,
            *consent.get("aliases", []),
        ]:
            raise ConsentError("source is outside this subject scope")
        if source.get("kind") not in self.ALLOWED_KINDS:
            raise ValueError("unsupported source kind")
        if (
            not source.get("rights")
            or not source.get("id")
            or not source.get("content")
        ):
            raise ValueError("source id, content and rights are required")
        when = timestamp(source["timestamp"]).isoformat()
        participants = source.get("participants", [])
        clean = lambda text: self._minimize(text, participants)
        facts = []
        for fact in source.get("facts", []):
            if fact.get("subject_id", self.subject_id) != self.subject_id:
                raise ConsentError("third-party facts cannot enter this identity")
            confidence = float(fact.get("confidence", 1.0))
            if not 0 <= confidence <= 1:
                raise ValueError("invalid confidence")
            facts.append(
                {
                    "predicate": clean(fact["predicate"]),
                    "value": clean(fact["value"]),
                    "confidence": confidence,
                    "inference": bool(fact.get("inference", False)),
                }
            )
        events = []
        for event in source.get("events", []):
            events.append(
                {
                    "timestamp": timestamp(event.get("timestamp", when)).isoformat(),
                    "content": clean(event["content"]),
                    "location": clean(event.get("location", "")),
                    "participants": [self.subject_id],
                    "valence": float(np.clip(event.get("valence", 0.0), -1, 1)),
                    "anchor": bool(event.get("anchor", False)),
                }
            )
        values = []
        for value in source.get("values", []):
            if not value.get("evidence"):
                raise ValueError("value profile requires evidence")
            values.append(
                {
                    "dimension": clean(value["dimension"]),
                    "weight": float(np.clip(value["weight"], -1, 1)),
                    "evidence": clean(value["evidence"]),
                }
            )
        record = {
            "id": str(source["id"]),
            "kind": source["kind"],
            "rights": clean(source["rights"]),
            "timestamp": when,
            "content": clean(source["content"]),
            "facts": facts,
            "events": events,
            "values": values,
        }
        with self._locked():
            self._check()
            data = self._read()
            if any(r["id"] == record["id"] for r in data["records"]):
                raise ValueError(
                    "source id already exists; use a distinct correction source"
                )
            data["records"].append(record)
            data["derived"] = self._derive(data["records"])
            data["reviewed"] = False
            self._write(data)
            self._audit("ingest")
        return {
            "label": SIMULATION_LABEL,
            "source": record["id"],
            "review_required": True,
        }

    def ingest_file(self, path):
        self._check()  # Authorize before opening the data file.
        return self.ingest(json.loads(Path(path).read_text()))

    def review(self):
        self._check()
        with self._locked():
            data = self._read()
            data["reviewed"] = True
            self._write(data)
            self._audit("review")
        return {"label": SIMULATION_LABEL, "reviewed": True}

    def inspect(self):
        self._check()
        with self._locked():
            data = self._read()
            self._audit("inspect")
            return {"label": SIMULATION_LABEL, **data}

    def answer(self, predicate):
        self._check()
        with self._locked():
            data = self._read()
            self._audit("answer")
            if not data.get("reviewed"):
                return SIMULATION_LABEL + "\nOs dados aguardam revisão humana."
            facts = [
                f
                for f in data.get("derived", {}).get("facts", [])
                if f["predicate"] == predicate
            ]
            claims = [
                {
                    "text": f"{f['timestamp'][:10]}: {f['value']}",
                    "source": f["source"],
                    "inference": f["inference"],
                }
                for f in facts
            ]
            return OutputPolicy.render_claims(
                claims, identity_mode=True, consented=True
            )

    def timeline(self):
        data = self.inspect()
        return {
            "label": SIMULATION_LABEL,
            "events": data.get("derived", {}).get("events", []),
        }

    def delete_source(self, source_id):
        self._check(allow_revoked=True)
        with self._locked():
            data = self._read()
            before = len(data["records"])
            data["records"] = [r for r in data["records"] if r["id"] != source_id]
            data["derived"] = self._derive(data["records"])
            data["reviewed"] = False
            self._write(data)
            self._audit("delete_source")
            verified = not any(r["id"] == source_id for r in self._read()["records"])
        return {
            "label": SIMULATION_LABEL,
            "removed": before - len(data["records"]),
            "verified": verified,
            "derived_rebuilt": True,
        }

    def erase(self, revoke=True):
        self._check(allow_revoked=True)
        with self._locked():
            self.path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            if revoke:
                consent = json.loads(self.consent.path.read_text())
                consent["revoked"] = True
                consent["revoked_at"] = now()
                atomic_write(self.consent.path, json.dumps(consent).encode())
            self._audit("erase")
        return {
            "label": SIMULATION_LABEL,
            "verified": not self.path.exists(),
            "scope": "active vault and its derived state; external copies are outside this store",
        }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/jepa/__init__.py

"""Lazy backend exports: NumPy equilibrium inference does not import PyTorch."""

from importlib import import_module

__all__ = [
    "JEPA",
    "ContextEncoder",
    "TargetEncoder",
    "LatentPredictor",
    "EquilibriumNetwork",
    "EquilibriumWorldModel",
    "EquilibriumConfig",
]


def __getattr__(name):
    if name in ("JEPA", "ContextEncoder", "TargetEncoder", "LatentPredictor"):
        return getattr(import_module(".torch_backend", __name__), name)
    if name in ("EquilibriumNetwork", "EquilibriumWorldModel", "EquilibriumConfig"):
        return getattr(import_module(".equilibrium", __name__), name)
    raise AttributeError(name)


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/jepa/equilibrium.py
========================================================================================
"""Contractive rate-network latent learning via two-phase equilibrium correlations.

This backend uses NumPy and explicit local equations, with no autograd calls.
It is a separate hypothesis from the PyTorch JEPA baseline, not a claim that
latent prediction is automatically variational free-energy inference.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import copy
from time import perf_counter
import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike


def batch(x: ArrayLike, width: int) -> np.ndarray:
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.ndim == 1:
        x = x[None]
    if x.ndim != 2 or x.shape[1] != width or not len(x) or not np.isfinite(x).all():
        raise ValueError(f"expected finite nonempty [batch, {width}] array")
    return x


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EquilibriumConfig:
    state_dim: int = 32
    recurrent_radius: float = 0.5
    damping: float = 0.5
    beta: float = 0.02
    tolerance: float = 1e-8
    max_steps: int = 200
    update_clip: float = 1.0

    def __post_init__(self):
        if min(self.state_dim, self.max_steps) < 1 or not 0 < self.damping <= 1:
            raise ValueError("invalid relaxation configuration")
        if (
            not 0 <= self.recurrent_radius < 1
            or not 0 < self.beta < 1 - self.recurrent_radius
        ):
            raise ValueError("require recurrent_radius + beta < 1 for both phases")
        if (
            not np.isfinite(self.tolerance)
            or self.tolerance <= 0
            or not np.isfinite(self.update_clip)
            or self.update_clip <= 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid tolerance or update clip")


@dataclass
class EquilibriumResult:
    state: np.ndarray
    output: np.ndarray
    iterations: int
    residual: float
    converged: bool


class EquilibriumNetwork:
    """E(s,u)=sum integral_0^s atanh(v)dv - s.T W s/2 - s.T(B.T u+b).

    State is in (-1,1). Hessian diag(1/(1-s²))-W is positive definite
    when symmetric ||W||_2<1. Free equilibrium solves s=tanh(sW+uB+b).
    Nudging adds beta/2 ||P s-y||², or beta*g.T P s for local messages.
    Central +/- beta phases estimate energy-parameter derivatives. This is a
    bounded nonlinear rate model; its symmetric weights are not a Dale circuit.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        inputs: int,
        outputs: int,
        *,
        seed: int = 0,
        config: EquilibriumConfig | None = None,
    ):
        self.config = config or EquilibriumConfig(state_dim=max(32, outputs * 2))
        if inputs < 1 or not 1 <= outputs <= self.config.state_dim:
            raise ValueError("invalid network dimensions")
        self.inputs, self.outputs = inputs, outputs
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        n = self.config.state_dim
        matrix = rng.normal(0, 0.1, (n, n))
        self.W = (matrix + matrix.T) / 2
        self.B = rng.normal(0, 1 / np.sqrt(inputs), (inputs, n))
        self.bias = np.zeros(n)
        self._contract()
        self.last_diagnostics = {}
        self.updates = 0

    def _contract(self):
        values, vectors = np.linalg.eigh((self.W + self.W.T) / 2)
        values = np.clip(
            values, -self.config.recurrent_radius, self.config.recurrent_radius
        )
        self.W = (vectors * values) @ vectors.T

    def energy(self, state: ArrayLike, inputs: ArrayLike) -> np.ndarray:
        state = batch(state, self.config.state_dim)
        u = batch(inputs, self.inputs)
        if len(state) != len(u):
            raise ValueError("batch mismatch")
        s = np.clip(state, -1 + 1e-12, 1 - 1e-12)
        primitive = s * np.arctanh(s) + 0.5 * np.log1p(-s * s)
        return (
            primitive.sum(1)
            - 0.5 * np.sum((s @ self.W) * s, axis=1)
            - np.sum(s * (u @ self.B + self.bias), axis=1)
        )

    def relax(
        self,
        inputs: ArrayLike,
        *,
        targets: ArrayLike | None = None,
        beta: float = 0.0,
        output_gradient: ArrayLike | None = None,
        initial: ArrayLike | None = None,
        deadline: float | None = None,
    ) -> EquilibriumResult:
        u = batch(inputs, self.inputs)
        c = self.config
        if abs(beta) + c.recurrent_radius >= 1:
            raise ValueError("nudge violates contraction bound")
        if targets is not None and output_gradient is not None:
            raise ValueError("choose targets or output_gradient")
        y = None if targets is None else batch(targets, self.outputs)
        gradient = (
            None if output_gradient is None else batch(output_gradient, self.outputs)
        )
        if (y is not None and len(y) != len(u)) or (
            gradient is not None and len(gradient) != len(u)
        ):
            raise ValueError("batch mismatch")
        s = (
            np.zeros((len(u), c.state_dim))
            if initial is None
            else batch(initial, c.state_dim).copy()
        )
        if len(s) != len(u):
            raise ValueError("initial batch mismatch")
        drive = u @ self.B + self.bias
        residual = float("inf")
        completed = 0
        for step in range(c.max_steps):
            if deadline is not None and perf_counter() >= deadline:
                break
            current = s @ self.W + drive
            if y is not None:
                current[:, -self.outputs :] -= beta * (s[:, -self.outputs :] - y)
            if gradient is not None:
                current[:, -self.outputs :] -= beta * gradient
            fixed = np.tanh(current)
            residual = float(np.max(abs(fixed - s)))
            completed = step + 1
            s += (fixed - s) * c.damping
            if residual <= c.tolerance:
                break
        # If no iteration fit the budget, report a finite bound, not a success.
        if not completed:
            residual = float(np.max(abs(np.tanh(drive) - s)))
        result = EquilibriumResult(
            s,
            s[:, -self.outputs :].copy(),
            completed,
            residual,
            bool(completed and residual <= c.tolerance),
        )
        self.last_diagnostics = {
            "iterations": completed,
            "residual": residual,
            "converged": result.converged,
        }
        return result

    def predict(self, inputs: ArrayLike) -> np.ndarray:
        result = self.relax(inputs)
        if not result.converged:
            raise RuntimeError("equilibrium inference did not converge")
        return result.output

    def learn(
        self,
        inputs: ArrayLike,
        targets: ArrayLike | None = None,
        *,
        output_gradient: ArrayLike | None = None,
        lr: float = 0.01,
    ) -> dict:
        u = batch(inputs, self.inputs)
        if (targets is None) == (output_gradient is None):
            raise ValueError("supply targets or a local output gradient")
        if not np.isfinite(lr) or lr < 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid learning rate")
        free = self.relax(u)
        beta = self.config.beta
        plus = self.relax(
            u,
            targets=targets,
            output_gradient=output_gradient,
            beta=beta,
            initial=free.state,
        )
        minus = self.relax(
            u,
            targets=targets,
            output_gradient=output_gradient,
            beta=-beta,
            initial=free.state,
        )
        if not all(r.converged for r in (free, plus, minus)):
            return {
                "updated": False,
                "reason": "relaxation_not_converged",
                "residual": max(r.residual for r in (free, plus, minus)),
                "input_gradient": np.zeros_like(u),
                "prediction": free.output,
            }
        delta = (plus.state - minus.state) / (2 * beta)
        # This input message is evaluated before modifying B.
        input_gradient = -delta @ self.B.T
        dB = u.T @ delta / len(u)
        dW = (plus.state.T @ plus.state - minus.state.T @ minus.state) / (
            4 * beta * len(u)
        )
        db = delta.mean(0)
        magnitude = np.sqrt(np.sum(dB * dB) + np.sum(dW * dW) + np.sum(db * db))
        scale = min(1.0, self.config.update_clip / max(magnitude, 1e-12))
        self.B += lr * scale * dB
        self.W += lr * scale * dW
        self.bias += lr * scale * db
        self._contract()
        self.updates += 1
        loss = (
            None
            if targets is None
            else float(
                0.5
                * np.mean(
                    np.sum((free.output - batch(targets, self.outputs)) ** 2, axis=1)
                )
            )
        )
        return {
            "updated": True,
            "loss": loss,
            "input_gradient": input_gradient,
            "prediction": free.output,
            "gradient_norm": float(magnitude),
            "clip_scale": float(scale),
            "residual": max(r.residual for r in (free, plus, minus)),
        }

    def linearize(self, inputs: ArrayLike) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        """Implicit ds/du = (diag(1/(1-s²))-W)^(-1) B.T, not finite differences."""
        result = self.relax(inputs)
        if not result.converged:
            raise RuntimeError("cannot linearize a nonconverged equilibrium")
        jacobian = []
        for s in result.state:
            hessian = np.diag(1 / np.maximum(1 - s * s, 1e-12)) - self.W
            jacobian.append(np.linalg.solve(hessian, self.B.T)[-self.outputs :])
        return result.output, np.stack(jacobian)

    def state_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "inputs": self.inputs,
            "outputs": self.outputs,
            "config": asdict(self.config),
            "W": self.W.copy(),
            "B": self.B.copy(),
            "bias": self.bias.copy(),
            "updates": self.updates,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state: dict) -> "EquilibriumNetwork":
        result = cls(
            state["inputs"],
            state["outputs"],
            config=EquilibriumConfig(**state["config"]),
        )
        for name in ("W", "B", "bias"):
            setattr(result, name, np.asarray(state[name], dtype=float).copy())
        if (
            not np.allclose(result.W, result.W.T, atol=1e-10)
            or np.linalg.norm(result.W, 2) > result.config.recurrent_radius + 1e-10
        ):
            raise ValueError("checkpoint violates symmetric contraction constraint")
        result.updates = state["updates"]
        return result


class EquilibriumWorldModel:
    """EMA-target latent prediction with an actual equilibrium encoder/predictor.

    During offline learning, local EqProp messages can also train the context
    encoder, with explicit variance/covariance output forces. Online runtime
    learning freezes the encoder to preserve protected coordinates. No lifetime
    or performance advantage follows merely from avoiding autograd.
    """

    kind = "equilibrium"

    def __init__(
        self,
        observation_dim: int,
        latent_dim: int,
        action_dim: int,
        *,
        seed: int = 0,
        config: EquilibriumConfig | None = None,
    ):
        self.observation_dim, self.latent_dim, self.action_dim = (
            observation_dim,
            latent_dim,
            action_dim,
        )
        self.config = config or EquilibriumConfig(state_dim=max(32, latent_dim * 2))
        self.context = EquilibriumNetwork(
            observation_dim, latent_dim, seed=seed, config=self.config
        )
        self.target = copy.deepcopy(self.context)
        self.predictor = EquilibriumNetwork(
            latent_dim + action_dim, latent_dim, seed=seed + 1, config=self.config
        )
        self.mean = np.zeros(observation_dim)
        self.scale = np.ones(observation_dim)
        self.encoder_frozen = False
        self.last_diagnostics = {}

    def fit_normalizer(self, observations: ArrayLike):
        if self.encoder_frozen:
            raise ValueError("normalizer is frozen with encoder coordinates")
        x = batch(observations, self.observation_dim)
        self.mean = x.mean(0)
        self.scale = np.maximum(x.std(0), 0.05)

    def _normalize(self, x):
        return (batch(x, self.observation_dim) - self.mean) / self.scale

    def encode(self, x: ArrayLike) -> np.ndarray:
        output = self.context.predict(self._normalize(x))
        self.last_diagnostics = self.context.last_diagnostics.copy()
        return output

    def predict(self, z: ArrayLike, action: ArrayLike) -> np.ndarray:
        z = batch(z, self.latent_dim)
        a = batch(action, self.action_dim)
        if len(z) != len(a):
            raise ValueError("latent/action batch mismatch")
        return self.predictor.predict(np.c_[z, a])

    def linearize(
        self, z: ArrayLike, action: ArrayLike
    ) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        z = batch(z, self.latent_dim)
        a = batch(action, self.action_dim)
        output, jacobian = self.predictor.linearize(np.c_[z, a])
        return (
            output,
            jacobian[:, :, : self.latent_dim],
            jacobian[:, :, self.latent_dim :],
        )

    def learn_latent(self, z, action, future_z, lr=0.01) -> dict:
        return self.predictor.learn(
            np.c_[batch(z, self.latent_dim), batch(action, self.action_dim)],
            batch(future_z, self.latent_dim),
            lr=lr,
        )

    def learn_transition(
        self,
        x,
        action,
        future,
        *,
        lr=0.01,
        train_encoder=False,
        variance_weight=1.0,
        covariance_weight=0.01,
        ema=0.99,
    ) -> dict:
        if train_encoder and self.encoder_frozen:
            raise ValueError("encoder coordinates are frozen")
        normalized = self._normalize(x)
        z = self.context.predict(normalized)
        target = self.target.predict(self._normalize(future))
        result = self.learn_latent(z, action, target, lr)
        if train_encoder and result["updated"]:
            gradient = result["input_gradient"][:, : self.latent_dim]
            centered = z - z.mean(0)
            n, d = z.shape
            std = np.sqrt(np.mean(centered**2, axis=0) + 1e-4)
            if n > 1:
                # Output-gradient arrays are per-example cost gradients; network
                # learn performs its own batch mean on parameter correlations.
                gradient += (
                    variance_weight
                    * (-centered / np.maximum(std, 1e-8))
                    * (std < 0.5)
                    / d
                )
                cov = centered.T @ centered / (n - 1)
                off = cov - np.diag(np.diag(cov))
                gradient += (
                    covariance_weight * 4 * n / (d * d * (n - 1)) * (centered @ off)
                )
            result["encoder_updated"] = self.context.learn(
                normalized, output_gradient=gradient, lr=lr
            )["updated"]
            for name in ("W", "B", "bias"):
                setattr(
                    self.target,
                    name,
                    ema * getattr(self.target, name)
                    + (1 - ema) * getattr(self.context, name),
                )
        result["latent_std"] = float(z.std(0).mean())
        return result

    def freeze_encoder(self):
        self.encoder_frozen = True

    def state_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "kind": self.kind,
            "observation_dim": self.observation_dim,
            "latent_dim": self.latent_dim,
            "action_dim": self.action_dim,
            "config": asdict(self.config),
            "context": self.context.state_dict(),
            "target": self.target.state_dict(),
            "predictor": self.predictor.state_dict(),
            "mean": self.mean.copy(),
            "scale": self.scale.copy(),
            "encoder_frozen": self.encoder_frozen,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state: dict) -> "EquilibriumWorldModel":
        obj = cls(
            state["observation_dim"],
            state["latent_dim"],
            state["action_dim"],
            config=EquilibriumConfig(**state["config"]),
        )
        for name in ("context", "target", "predictor"):
            setattr(obj, name, EquilibriumNetwork.from_state_dict(state[name]))
        obj.mean = np.asarray(state["mean"]).copy()
        obj.scale = np.asarray(state["scale"]).copy()
        obj.encoder_frozen = state["encoder_frozen"]
        return obj

ARQUIVO: nova/jepa/torch_backend.py
