"""Latent prediction with a stopped, exponential-moving-average target."""

from copy import deepcopy
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F


class ContextEncoder(nn.Sequential):
    def __init__(self, observation_dim: int, latent_dim: int, hidden: int = 64):
        super().__init__(
            nn.Linear(observation_dim, hidden), nn.SiLU(), nn.Linear(hidden, latent_dim)
        )


class TargetEncoder(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, encoder: nn.Module):
        super().__init__()
        self.encoder = deepcopy(encoder).requires_grad_(False)

    def forward(self, x):
        with torch.no_grad():
            return self.encoder(x)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def update(self, encoder: nn.Module, decay: float = 0.99):
        for target, source in zip(
            self.encoder.parameters(), encoder.parameters(), strict=True
        ):
            target.lerp_(source, 1 - decay)
        for target, source in zip(
            self.encoder.buffers(), encoder.buffers(), strict=True
        ):
            target.copy_(source)


class LatentPredictor(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, latent_dim: int, action_dim: int, hidden: int = 64):
        super().__init__()
        self.net = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(latent_dim + action_dim, hidden),
            nn.SiLU(),
            nn.Linear(hidden, latent_dim),
        )

    def forward(self, z, action):
        return self.net(torch.cat((z, action), dim=-1))


class JEPA(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        observation_dim: int,
        latent_dim: int,
        action_dim: int,
        *,
        variance_weight: float = 1.0,
        covariance_weight: float = 0.01,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.context = ContextEncoder(observation_dim, latent_dim)
        self.target = TargetEncoder(self.context)
        self.predictor = LatentPredictor(latent_dim, action_dim)
        self.variance_weight, self.covariance_weight = (
            variance_weight,
            covariance_weight,
        )

    def loss(self, x, action, future):
        z = self.context(x)
        target = self.target(future)
        pred = self.predictor(z, action)
        prediction = F.smooth_l1_loss(pred, target)
        std = torch.sqrt(z.var(0, unbiased=False) + 1e-4)
        variance = F.relu(0.5 - std).mean()
        centered = z - z.mean(0)
        covariance = centered.T @ centered / max(len(z) - 1, 1)
        off_diagonal = covariance - torch.diag(torch.diag(covariance))
        loss = (
            prediction
            + self.variance_weight * variance
            + self.covariance_weight * off_diagonal.square().mean()
        )
        return loss, {
            "prediction": prediction.detach().item(),
            "latent_std": z.std(0).mean().detach().item(),
        }

    @torch.no_grad()
    def assert_noncollapsed(self, x, threshold=0.05):
        std = self.context(x).std(0).mean().item()
        if std < threshold:
            raise AssertionError(f"latent collapse: mean std {std:.6g} < {threshold}")
        return std

========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/kernels/triton_ternary.py

"""Optional GPU reference kernel: 2-bit weights, signed additions, no dense GEMM.

Requires Triton and CUDA. This intentionally simple kernel needs benchmarking;
compaction alone is not evidence of lower latency or energy.
"""

import torch


def pack_ternary(weights):
    if (
        weights.ndim != 2
        or not torch.isin(
            weights, torch.tensor([-1, 0, 1], device=weights.device)
        ).all()
    ):
        raise ValueError("expected ternary matrix")
    flat = weights.flatten()
    codes = torch.where(flat == -1, 2, flat).to(torch.uint8)
    padded = torch.zeros(
        ((len(codes) + 3) // 4) * 4, dtype=torch.uint8, device=weights.device
    )
    padded[: len(codes)] = codes
    padded = padded.reshape(-1, 4)
    return (
        padded[:, 0] | (padded[:, 1] << 2) | (padded[:, 2] << 4) | (padded[:, 3] << 6)
    )


def matmul(x, packed, out_features):
    if not x.is_cuda or not packed.is_cuda:
        raise ValueError("CUDA tensors required")
    if (
        x.ndim != 2
        or not x.is_contiguous()
        or packed.dtype != torch.uint8
        or out_features < 1
    ):
        raise ValueError("invalid tensor layout")
    if packed.numel() != (x.shape[1] * out_features + 3) // 4:
        raise ValueError("packed dimensions mismatch")
    import triton
    import triton.language as tl

    @triton.jit
    def kernel(X, W, Y, D: tl.constexpr, N: tl.constexpr, BLOCK: tl.constexpr):
        row = tl.program_id(0)
        cols = tl.program_id(1) * BLOCK + tl.arange(0, BLOCK)
        acc = tl.full((BLOCK,), 0, tl.float32)
        for k in range(D):
            index = k * N + cols
            bits = tl.load(W + index // 4, cols < N, other=0)
            code = (bits >> (2 * (index % 4))) & 3
            value = tl.load(X + row * D + k).to(tl.float32)
            acc += tl.where(code == 1, value, tl.where(code == 2, -value, 0.0))
        tl.store(Y + row * N + cols, acc, cols < N)

    y = torch.empty((len(x), out_features), device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
    kernel[(len(x), triton.cdiv(out_features, 64))](
        x, packed, y, x.shape[1], out_features, 64
    )
    return y

ARQUIVO: nova/language/__init__.py

"""Grounded user-facing verbalization; optional neural generations remain drafts."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from nova.values import OutputPolicy, SIMULATION_LABEL


@dataclass
class Interlocutor:
    beliefs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    goals: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    estimated_valence: float = 0.0
    turns: int = 0

    def update(self, *, beliefs=None, goals=None, valence=0.0):
        self.beliefs.update(beliefs or {})
        self.goals = list(goals or self.goals)
        self.estimated_valence = max(-1.0, min(1.0, valence))
        self.turns += 1


class GroundedVerbalizer:
    def render(self, claims, identity_mode=False, consented=False, style=None):
        # Style only changes formatting; it cannot create additional factual content.
        answer = OutputPolicy.render_claims(
            claims, identity_mode=identity_mode, consented=consented
        )
        if style and style.get("compact"):
            answer = answer.replace("\n", " ")
        return answer

    def stream(self, *args, interrupted=None, **kwargs):
        answer = self.render(*args, **kwargs)
        # Keep the permanent identity label intact as the first streaming chunk.
        if answer.startswith(SIMULATION_LABEL):
            yield SIMULATION_LABEL + "\n"
            answer = answer[len(SIMULATION_LABEL) :].lstrip()
        for word in answer.split():
            if interrupted is not None and interrupted():
                break
            yield word + " "


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class UnverifiedDraft:
    text: str
    source_ids: tuple[str, ...]
    requires_review: bool = True


class LocalLanguageAdapter:
    """Optional frozen local LLM with learned latent-prefix input (not auto-published).

    Requires separately installed transformers and local model weights. Generated
    text is an unverified draft; the grounded renderer remains the output gate.
    """

    def __init__(self, model_directory, latent_dim=8):
        import torch
        from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM

        self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
            model_directory, local_files_only=True
        )
        self.model = (
            AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_directory, local_files_only=True)
            .eval()
            .requires_grad_(False)
        )
        self.prefix = torch.nn.Linear(
            latent_dim, self.model.get_input_embeddings().embedding_dim
        )

    def draft(self, latent, claims, style=None, max_new_tokens=80):
        import json
        import torch

        prompt = json.dumps(
            {
                "facts": claims,
                "style": style or {},
                "instruction": "Verbalize only supplied facts and keep sources.",
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )
        tokens = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors="pt")
        with torch.no_grad():
            embeddings = self.model.get_input_embeddings()(tokens["input_ids"])
            prefix = self.prefix(torch.as_tensor(latent, dtype=embeddings.dtype))[
                None, None
            ]
            generated = self.model.generate(
                inputs_embeds=torch.cat((prefix, embeddings), 1),
                max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
                do_sample=False,
            )
        return UnverifiedDraft(
            self.tokenizer.decode(generated[0], skip_special_tokens=True),
            tuple(c["source"] for c in claims),
        )

ARQUIVO: nova/memory/__init__.py

"""Associative retrieval, bounded working state, and source-preserving episodes."""

from __future__ import annotations
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import numpy as np
from scipy.special import softmax, logsumexp


def kwta(x, k):
    """Keep k largest magnitudes per row; ties are deterministic."""
    x = np.asarray(x)
    n = x.shape[-1]
    k = max(1, round(k * n)) if isinstance(k, float) else k
    if not 1 <= k <= n:
        raise ValueError("k outside [1, dimensions]")
    indices = np.argsort(np.abs(x), axis=-1, kind="stable")[..., -k:]
    out = np.zeros_like(x)
    np.put_along_axis(out, indices, np.take_along_axis(x, indices, -1), -1)
    return out


class ModernHopfield:
    """Recall softmax(beta * q X.T) X; E(q)=||q||²/2-logsumexp(beta Xq)/beta."""

    def __init__(self, patterns, beta=10.0):
        self.patterns = np.asarray(patterns, dtype=float)
        if self.patterns.ndim != 2 or not len(self.patterns) or beta <= 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid patterns/beta")
        self.beta = beta

    def retrieve(self, query, iterations=1):
        q = np.asarray(query, dtype=float)
        for _ in range(iterations):
            q = softmax(self.beta * (q @ self.patterns.T), axis=-1) @ self.patterns
        return q

    def energy(self, query):
        q = np.asarray(query, dtype=float)
        return (
            np.sum(q * q, axis=-1) / 2
            - logsumexp(self.beta * (q @ self.patterns.T), axis=-1) / self.beta
        )


@dataclass
class Episode:
    id: str
    embedding: np.ndarray
    content: str
    source: str
    timestamp: str
    location: str = ""
    participants: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    valence: float = 0.0
    surprise: float = 0.0
    anchor: bool = False
    revisions: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    feature_version: int = 0


class EpisodicMemory:
    def __init__(self, capacity=1024, threshold=0.5):
        self.capacity, self.threshold = capacity, threshold
        self.episodes: list[Episode] = []

    def write(self, episode: Episode, force=False):
        if not episode.source:
            raise ValueError("episode requires source")
        if any(e.id == episode.id for e in self.episodes):
            raise ValueError("duplicate episode id")
        if not force and episode.surprise < self.threshold:
            return False
        if len(self.episodes) >= self.capacity:
            candidates = [e for e in self.episodes if not e.anchor]
            if not candidates:
                return False
            victim = min(candidates, key=lambda e: e.surprise + abs(e.valence))
            self.episodes = [e for e in self.episodes if e.id != victim.id]
        self.episodes.append(episode)
        return True

    def retrieve(self, query, limit=3, *, participant=None, location=None, before=None):
        candidates = [
            e
            for e in self.episodes
            if (participant is None or participant in e.participants)
            and (location is None or location == e.location)
            and (before is None or e.timestamp < before)
        ]
        q = np.asarray(query)
        norm = max(np.linalg.norm(q), 1e-12)
        return sorted(
            candidates,
            key=lambda e: float(
                q @ e.embedding / (norm * max(np.linalg.norm(e.embedding), 1e-12))
            ),
            reverse=True,
        )[:limit]

    def reconsolidate(self, episode_id, content):
        e = next(e for e in self.episodes if e.id == episode_id)
        e.revisions.append(e.content)
        e.content = content

    def sleep(self, learn, limit=32):
        chosen = sorted(
            self.episodes, key=lambda e: e.surprise + abs(e.valence), reverse=True
        )[:limit]
        for e in chosen:
            learn(e)
        # Exact duplicates only: conflicting or similar episodes must not be merged.
        seen = set()
        kept = []
        for e in self.episodes:
            key = (e.content, e.source, e.timestamp)
            if e.anchor or key not in seen:
                kept.append(e)
            seen.add(key)
        self.episodes = kept
        return len(chosen)


class WorkingMemory:
    def __init__(self, slots=7, dimensions=8, decay=0.95):
        if not 1 <= slots <= 10 or not 0 <= decay <= 1:
            raise ValueError("invalid working-memory configuration")
        self.state = np.zeros((slots, dimensions))
        self.decay = decay
        self.cursor = 0

    def update(self, value=None, gate=1.0):
        if not 0 <= gate <= 1:
            raise ValueError("gate must be in [0,1]")
        self.state *= self.decay
        if value is not None:
            self.state[self.cursor] = (1 - gate) * self.state[
                self.cursor
            ] + gate * np.asarray(value)
            self.cursor = (self.cursor + 1) % len(self.state)
        return self.state.copy()


class SensoryBuffer(deque):
    def __init__(self, capacity=32):
        super().__init__(maxlen=capacity)


class SemanticGraph:
    """Retain contradictory facts with distinct sources; never silently merge."""

    def __init__(self, capacity=None):
        if capacity is not None and capacity < 1:
            raise ValueError("capacity must be positive")
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.evicted = 0
        self.facts = []

    def add(
        self,
        subject,
        predicate,
        value,
        source,
        confidence=1.0,
        timestamp="",
        anchor=False,
    ):
        if not source or not 0 <= confidence <= 1:
            raise ValueError("source/confidence required")
        fact = dict(
            subject=subject,
            predicate=predicate,
            value=value,
            source=source,
            confidence=confidence,
            timestamp=timestamp,
        )
        if anchor:
            fact["anchor"] = True
        if fact in self.facts:
            return False
        if self.capacity is not None and len(self.facts) >= self.capacity:
            index = next(
                (i for i, f in enumerate(self.facts) if not f.get("anchor")), None
            )
            if index is None:
                return False
            self.facts.pop(index)
            self.evicted += 1
        self.facts.append(fact)
        return True

    def query(self, subject, predicate):
        return [
            f.copy()
            for f in self.facts
            if f["subject"] == subject and f["predicate"] == predicate
        ]


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/neuromod/__init__.py
========================================================================================
"""Bounded functional control signals, independently ablatable."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import numpy as np


@dataclass
class Neuromodulator:
    enabled: frozenset[str] = field(
        default_factory=lambda: frozenset({"NE", "DA", "ACh", "5HT"})
    )
    error_scale: float = 1.0
    surprise: float = 0.0
    reward_pe: float = 0.0
    uncertainty: float = 0.0
    patience: float = 0.5

    def update(self, error, reward=0.0, value=0.0, next_value=0.0, uncertainty=0.0):
        error = max(0.0, float(error))
        self.surprise = float(np.clip(error / max(self.error_scale, 1e-8), 0, 10))
        self.error_scale = 0.99 * self.error_scale + 0.01 * error
        self.reward_pe = float(np.clip(reward + 0.99 * next_value - value, -10, 10))
        self.uncertainty = float(np.clip(uncertainty, 0, 1))
        self.patience = float(
            np.clip(0.99 * self.patience + 0.01 * (1 - self.uncertainty), 0, 1)
        )
        return self

    def learning_rate(self, base):
        return base * (1 + min(self.surprise, 2)) if "NE" in self.enabled else base

    def write_threshold(self, base):
        return base / (1 + self.surprise) if "NE" in self.enabled else base

    def sensory_weight(self):
        return 0.5 + 0.5 * self.uncertainty if "ACh" in self.enabled else 0.5

    def consolidation(self):
        return float(1 / (1 + np.exp(-self.reward_pe))) if "DA" in self.enabled else 0.5

    def discount(self):
        return 0.9 + 0.099 * self.patience if "5HT" in self.enabled else 0.95

"""Interchangeable N0, N1 and small-compartment N2 research models."""

from .models import RateNeuron, AdExPopulation, HodgkinHuxleyCompartments, make_neuron

__all__ = ["RateNeuron", "AdExPopulation", "HodgkinHuxleyCompartments", "make_neuron"]


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/neuron/models.py
========================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
from scipy.special import expit
from nova.continual import ProtectedLinear
from nova.memory import kwta


class RateNeuron:
    """N0: y=ReLU(v_b*(1+tanh(v_a))); k-WTA after apical gain."""

    def __init__(self, inputs=8, units=32, seed=0, k=0.1, apical_gain=True):
        self.basal = ProtectedLinear(inputs, units, seed=seed)
        self.apical = ProtectedLinear(inputs, units, seed=seed + 1)
        self.k, self.apical_gain = k, apical_gain
        self.state = np.zeros(units)

    def step(self, inputs, modulators=None, dt=0.1):
        if dt <= 0:
            raise ValueError("dt must be positive")
        basal, apical = inputs
        vb = self.basal(np.asarray(basal)[None])[0]
        va = self.apical(np.asarray(apical)[None])[0]
        output = vb * (1 + np.tanh(va)) if self.apical_gain else vb + va
        self.state = kwta(np.maximum(output, 0), self.k)
        return self.state.copy(), {"rate": self.state.copy()}


class AdExPopulation:
    """N1, ms/mV/pA/pF: C dv/dt=-gL(v-EL)+gL*Delta*exp((v-VT)/Delta)-w+gc(d-v)+I.

    tau_w dw/dt=a(v-EL)-w; dendritic conductance coupling, optional NMDA sigmoid.
    Euler dt <= 1 ms. Types: 0=E, 1=PV, 2=SST, 3=VIP. Sign belongs to outgoing
    synapses and is enforced by Microcircuit. Parameters are research defaults.
    """

    def __init__(self, units=100, seed=0, backend="numpy", nmda=False):
        if units < 1:
            raise ValueError("units must be positive")
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.types = np.zeros(units, dtype=int)
        self.types[int(0.8 * units) : int(0.9 * units)] = 1
        self.types[int(0.9 * units) : int(0.95 * units)] = 2
        self.types[int(0.95 * units) :] = 3
        self.a = np.choose(self.types, [2.0, 0.0, 4.0, 2.0])
        self.b = np.choose(self.types, [40.0, 0.0, 80.0, 20.0])
        self.tau = np.choose(self.types, [200.0, 30.0, 250.0, 150.0]) * rng.lognormal(
            0, 0.1, units
        )
        self.v = np.full(units, -70.0)
        self.w = np.zeros(units)
        self.dendrite = self.v.copy()
        self.refractory = np.zeros(units)
        self.backend, self.nmda = backend, nmda

    def step(self, inputs, modulators=None, dt=0.1):
        if not 0 < dt <= 1:
            raise ValueError("N1 requires 0 < dt <= 1 ms")
        basal, apical = inputs
        basal = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(basal, dtype=float), self.v.shape)
        apical = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(apical, dtype=float), self.v.shape)
        if not np.isfinite(basal).all() or not np.isfinite(apical).all():
            raise ValueError("nonfinite currents")
        if self.backend == "rust":
            from nova.core import require_native

            args = [
                a.tolist()
                for a in (
                    self.v,
                    self.w,
                    self.dendrite,
                    self.refractory,
                    basal,
                    apical,
                    self.a,
                    self.b,
                    self.tau,
                )
            ]
            result = require_native().adex_step(*args, dt, self.nmda)
            self.v, self.w, self.dendrite, self.refractory, spikes = map(
                np.asarray, result
            )
        elif self.backend == "numpy":
            v, w, d = self.v, self.w, self.dendrite
            self.dendrite = (
                d
                + dt
                * (
                    -5 * (d + 70)
                    + 2 * (v - d)
                    + apical
                    + (30 * expit((d + 40) / 5) if self.nmda else 0)
                )
                / 100
            )
            self.w = w + dt * (self.a * (v + 70) - w) / self.tau
            updated = (
                v
                + dt
                * (
                    -10 * (v + 70)
                    + 20 * np.exp(np.minimum((v + 50) / 2, 10))
                    - w
                    + 2 * (d - v)
                    + basal
                )
                / 200
            )
            self.v = np.where(self.refractory > 0, -58.0, updated)
            self.refractory = np.maximum(self.refractory - dt, 0)
            spikes = self.v >= 20
            self.v[spikes] = -58
            self.w[spikes] += self.b[spikes]
            self.refractory[spikes] = 2
        else:
            raise ValueError("backend must be numpy or rust")
        return spikes.copy(), self.state_dict()

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            key: getattr(self, key).copy()
            for key in ("v", "w", "dendrite", "refractory", "types", "a", "b", "tau")
        }

    def load_state_dict(self, state):
        for key, value in state.items():
            if key not in self.state_dict() or np.shape(value) != self.v.shape:
                raise ValueError("invalid neuron checkpoint")
            setattr(self, key, np.asarray(value).copy())


class HodgkinHuxleyCompartments:
    """N2 small branched cable: Na/K HH gates plus phenomenological Ca and Ih.

    C dV/dt=I-gNa*m³h(V-50)-gK*n⁴(V+77)-gCa*c²(V-120)-gh*q(V+20)
             -gL(V+54.4)+sum gc(V_neighbor-V). Gates relax to voltage equilibria.
    Explicit RK4, dt <= .05 ms; not a fitted cellular morphology.
    """

    def __init__(self, compartments=4):
        if compartments < 1:
            raise ValueError("compartments must be positive")
        self.v = np.full(compartments, -65.0)
        self.gates = self._steady(self.v)[0]
        self.coupling = np.zeros((compartments, compartments))
        for i in range(1, compartments):
            self.coupling[i, (i - 1) // 2] = self.coupling[(i - 1) // 2, i] = 0.1

    @staticmethod
    def _ratio(x):
        x = np.asarray(x)
        out = np.ones_like(x)
        np.divide(x, -np.expm1(-x), out=out, where=np.abs(x) > 1e-7)
        return out

    def _steady(self, v):
        am = self._ratio((v + 40) / 10)
        bm = 4 * np.exp(-(v + 65) / 18)
        ah = 0.07 * np.exp(-(v + 65) / 20)
        bh = expit((v + 35) / 10)
        an = 0.1 * self._ratio((v + 55) / 10)
        bn = 0.125 * np.exp(-(v + 65) / 80)
        steady = np.stack(
            (
                am / (am + bm),
                ah / (ah + bh),
                an / (an + bn),
                expit((v + 20) / 6),
                expit(-(v + 80) / 8),
            )
        )
        tau = np.stack(
            (
                1 / (am + bm),
                1 / (ah + bh),
                1 / (an + bn),
                np.full_like(v, 5),
                np.full_like(v, 100),
            )
        )
        return steady, tau

    def step(self, inputs, modulators=None, dt=0.01):
        if not 0 < dt <= 0.05:
            raise ValueError("N2 requires 0 < dt <= .05 ms")
        basal, apical = inputs
        current = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(basal, dtype=float), self.v.shape).copy()
        current[1:] += np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(apical, dtype=float), self.v.shape)[
            1:
        ]
        before = self.v.copy()
        state = np.vstack((self.v, self.gates))

        def f(s):
            v = s[0]
            m, h, n, c, q = s[1:]
            steady, tau = self._steady(v)
            ionic = (
                120 * m**3 * h * (v - 50)
                + 36 * n**4 * (v + 77)
                + 0.05 * c * c * (v - 120)
                + 0.02 * q * (v + 20)
                + 0.3 * (v + 54.4)
            )
            dv = current - ionic + self.coupling @ v - self.coupling.sum(1) * v
            return np.vstack((dv, (steady - s[1:]) / tau))

        k1 = f(state)
        k2 = f(state + dt * k1 / 2)
        k3 = f(state + dt * k2 / 2)
        k4 = f(state + dt * k3)
        state += dt * (k1 + 2 * k2 + 2 * k3 + k4) / 6
        self.v, self.gates = state[0], state[1:]
        return (before < 0) & (self.v >= 0), {
            "v": self.v.copy(),
            "gates": self.gates.copy(),
        }


def make_neuron(level="N0", **kwargs):
    return {"N0": RateNeuron, "N1": AdExPopulation, "N2": HodgkinHuxleyCompartments}[
        level
    ](**kwargs)

"""Offline, self-contained metric explorer; no server or external assets."""

from pathlib import Path

def dashboard(metrics_path, output):
    rows = [
        json.loads(line)
        for line in Path(metrics_path).read_text().splitlines()
        if line.strip()
    ]
    payload = json.dumps(rows).replace("<", "\\u003c")
    html = """<!doctype html><html lang="pt-BR"><meta charset="utf-8"><title>NOVA — métricas</title>

for(const key of keys){const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=o.textContent=key;select.append(o)}
function draw(){const key=select.value,values=data.map(d=>d[key]),c=document.getElementById('chart'),ctx=c.getContext('2d');ctx.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);if(!values.length)return;

        "PAYLOAD", payload
    )
    Path(output).write_text(html)

"""Modality encoders, streaming log-mel audio, slots and predictive inference."""

from __future__ import annotations
import math
import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F


class VisionEncoder(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, latent_dim=16, channels=3):
        super().__init__()
        self.net = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(channels, 16, 3, padding=1),
            nn.SiLU(),
            nn.Conv2d(16, 32, 3, stride=2, padding=1),
            nn.SiLU(),
            nn.AdaptiveAvgPool2d(1),
            nn.Flatten(),
            nn.Linear(32, latent_dim),
        )

    def foveate(self, image, gaze=(0.0, 0.0), radius=0.3):
        if radius <= 0:
            raise ValueError("radius must be positive")
        h, w = image.shape[-2:]
        yy, xx = torch.meshgrid(
            torch.linspace(-1, 1, h, device=image.device),
            torch.linspace(-1, 1, w, device=image.device),
            indexing="ij",
        )
        mask = torch.exp(-((xx - gaze[0]) ** 2 + (yy - gaze[1]) ** 2) / (2 * radius**2))
        low = F.interpolate(
            F.interpolate(image, size=(max(1, h // 8), max(1, w // 8)), mode="area"),
            size=(h, w),
            mode="bilinear",
            align_corners=False,
        )
        return image * mask + low * (1 - mask)

    def forward(self, image, gaze=(0.0, 0.0)):
        return self.net(self.foveate(image, gaze))


def mel_filters(sample_rate, n_fft, bands):
    hz_to_mel = lambda hz: 2595 * math.log10(1 + hz / 700)
    points = 700 * (
        10 ** (torch.linspace(0, hz_to_mel(sample_rate / 2), bands + 2) / 2595) - 1
    )
    freq = torch.linspace(0, sample_rate / 2, n_fft // 2 + 1)
    rising = (freq[None] - points[:-2, None]) / (points[1:-1, None] - points[:-2, None])
    falling = (points[2:, None] - freq[None]) / (points[2:, None] - points[1:-1, None])
    return torch.minimum(rising, falling).clamp_min(0)


class StreamingAudioEncoder(nn.Module):
    """Uncentered STFT -> log-mel -> causal temporal convolution; chunk-invariant."""

    def __init__(self, latent_dim=16, sample_rate=16000, n_fft=256, hop=128, bands=24):
        super().__init__()
        if not 0 < hop <= n_fft:
            raise ValueError("hop must be in (0,n_fft]")
        self.n_fft, self.hop, self.bands = n_fft, hop, bands
        self.register_buffer("filters", mel_filters(sample_rate, n_fft, bands))
        self.register_buffer("window", torch.hann_window(n_fft))
        self.register_buffer("samples", torch.empty(0))
        self.register_buffer("history", torch.zeros(1, bands, 2))
        self.conv = nn.Conv1d(bands, latent_dim, 3)

    def reset(self):
        self.samples = self.samples.new_empty(0)
        self.history.zero_()

    def forward(self, chunk):
        if chunk.ndim != 1:
            raise ValueError("expected mono one-dimensional samples")
        samples = torch.cat((self.samples, chunk))
        if len(samples) < self.n_fft:
            self.samples = samples.detach()
            return samples.new_empty((0, self.conv.out_channels))
        frames = samples.unfold(0, self.n_fft, self.hop)
        spectrum = torch.fft.rfft(frames * self.window).abs().square()
        mel = torch.log1p(spectrum @ self.filters.T).T[None]
        sequence = torch.cat((self.history, mel), dim=2)
        output = self.conv(sequence).squeeze(0).T
        self.history = sequence[:, :, -2:].detach()
        self.samples = samples[len(frames) * self.hop :].detach()
        return output


class TextEncoder(nn.Module):
    """UTF-8 byte tokens (padding=256) and a small recurrent sensory encoder."""

    def __init__(self, latent_dim=16):
        super().__init__()
        self.embedding = nn.Embedding(257, 32, padding_idx=256)
        self.gru = nn.GRU(32, latent_dim, batch_first=True)

    def forward(self, texts):
        encoded = [list(t.encode("utf-8")) or [256] for t in texts]
        if not encoded:
            raise ValueError("empty batch")
        lengths = torch.tensor([len(t) for t in encoded])
        tokens = torch.full(
            (len(encoded), int(lengths.max())),
            256,
            dtype=torch.long,
            device=self.embedding.weight.device,
        )
        for i, tokens_i in enumerate(encoded):
            tokens[i, : len(tokens_i)] = torch.tensor(tokens_i, device=tokens.device)
        packed = nn.utils.rnn.pack_padded_sequence(
            self.embedding(tokens), lengths, batch_first=True, enforce_sorted=False
        )
        _, state = self.gru(packed)
        return state[-1]


class SlotAttention(nn.Module):
    """Iterative slot competition; previous slots provide temporal object context."""

    def __init__(self, dimension=16, slots=4, iterations=3):
        super().__init__()
        self.initial = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(1, slots, dimension) * 0.1)
        self.key = nn.Linear(dimension, dimension)
        self.query = nn.Linear(dimension, dimension)
        self.value = nn.Linear(dimension, dimension)
        self.update = nn.GRUCell(dimension, dimension)
        self.iterations = iterations

    def forward(self, tokens, previous=None):
        slots = (
            self.initial.expand(len(tokens), -1, -1) if previous is None else previous
        )
        k, v = self.key(tokens), self.value(tokens)
        for _ in range(self.iterations):
            score = (
                self.query(slots) @ k.transpose(-1, -2) / math.sqrt(tokens.shape[-1])
            )
            attention = score.softmax(dim=1) + 1e-8
            attention = attention / attention.sum(-1, keepdim=True)
            updates = attention @ v
            slots = self.update(
                updates.reshape(-1, tokens.shape[-1]),
                slots.reshape(-1, tokens.shape[-1]),
            ).reshape_as(slots)
        return slots


class MultimodalFusion(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dimension=16):
        super().__init__()
        self.cross = nn.Linear(dimension, dimension, bias=False)

    def cross_prediction_loss(self, a, b):
        return F.smooth_l1_loss(self.cross(a), b.detach()) + F.smooth_l1_loss(
            self.cross(b), a.detach()
        )

    def forward(self, modalities, precision=None):
        stack = torch.stack(modalities, dim=1)
        weights = (
            torch.ones(stack.shape[:2], device=stack.device)
            if precision is None
            else precision
        )
        if torch.any(weights < 0) or torch.any(weights.sum(1) <= 0):
            raise ValueError("precision must be nonnegative with positive sum")
        return (stack * weights[:, :, None]).sum(1) / weights.sum(1, keepdim=True)


class PredictiveHierarchy:
    """E=1/2 sum_l pi_l ||r_l-W_l r_(l+1)||²; relax representations by gradient descent."""

    def __init__(self, dimensions=(8, 4, 2), seed=0):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.weights = [
            rng.normal(0, 1 / np.sqrt(b), (a, b))
            for a, b in zip(dimensions[:-1], dimensions[1:])
        ]
        self.dimensions = dimensions

    def infer(self, observation, precision=None, steps=100, lr=0.03):
        x = np.asarray(observation, dtype=float)
        states = [x.copy()] + [
            np.zeros((*x.shape[:-1], d)) for d in self.dimensions[1:]
        ]
        precision = [1.0] * len(self.weights) if precision is None else precision
        if len(precision) != len(self.weights) or any(
            np.any(np.asarray(p) < 0) for p in precision
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid precision")
        history = []
        for _ in range(steps):
            errors = [
                states[i] - states[i + 1] @ w.T for i, w in enumerate(self.weights)
            ]
            history.append(
                float(
                    sum(
                        np.sum(np.asarray(p) * e * e) / 2
                        for p, e in zip(precision, errors)
                    )
                )
            )
            gradients = []
            for i in range(1, len(states)):
                grad = -(errors[i - 1] * precision[i - 1]) @ self.weights[i - 1]
                if i < len(errors):
                    grad += errors[i] * precision[i]
                gradients.append(grad)
            for i, grad in enumerate(gradients, 1):
                states[i] -= lr * grad
        return states, history

    def learn(self, states, lr=0.001):
        for i, w in enumerate(self.weights):
            error = states[i] - states[i + 1] @ w.T
            w += (
                lr
                * np.atleast_2d(error).T
                @ np.atleast_2d(states[i + 1])
                / len(np.atleast_2d(error))
            )


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/planning/__init__.py
========================================================================================
"""Receding-horizon CEM baseline, explicit feasibility and warm-start state."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from time import perf_counter
import numpy as np


@dataclass
class Plan:
    action: np.ndarray
    cost: float | None
    iterations: int
    rollouts: int
    diagnostics: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


class LatentPlanner:
    """CEM searches H*action_dim variables; latent_dim affects rollout cost.

    Reuses the shifted previous plan, retains a feasible incumbent, and rejects
    invalid trajectories. The time budget is soft and checked between model
    calls. A zero stop command is not certified safe when every candidate fails.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        predictor,
        cost,
        action_dim=1,
        horizon=12,
        rollouts=128,
        iterations=4,
        elite_fraction=0.1,
        seed=0,
        discount=0.99,
        constraint=None,
    ):
        if (
            min(action_dim, horizon, rollouts, iterations) < 1
            or not 0 < elite_fraction <= 1
            or not 0 < discount <= 1
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid search configuration")
        self.predictor, self.cost = predictor, cost
        self.action_dim, self.horizon, self.rollouts, self.iterations = (
            action_dim,
            horizon,
            rollouts,
            iterations,
        )
        self.elite_fraction, self.discount, self.constraint = (
            elite_fraction,
            discount,
            constraint,
        )
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.warm_actions = np.zeros((horizon, action_dim))
        self.last_diagnostics = {}

    def plan(self, z, budget_ms=None):
        if budget_ms is not None and (not np.isfinite(budget_ms) or budget_ms <= 0):
            raise ValueError("positive time budget required")
        z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
        if z.ndim != 1 or not np.isfinite(z).all():
            raise ValueError("finite latent vector required")
        start = perf_counter()
        mean = self.warm_actions.copy()
        std = np.ones_like(mean)
        best_cost = float("inf")
        best = None
        completed = 0
        calls = 0
        reason = "iterations"
        for iteration in range(self.iterations):
            if budget_ms is not None and (perf_counter() - start) * 1000 >= budget_ms:
                reason = "deadline"
                break
            actions = np.clip(
                self.rng.normal(mean, std, (self.rollouts, *mean.shape)), -1, 1
            )
            actions[0] = self.warm_actions if best is None else best
            states = np.broadcast_to(z, (self.rollouts, len(z))).copy()
            costs = np.zeros(self.rollouts)
            valid = np.ones(self.rollouts, dtype=bool)
            aborted = False
            for t in range(self.horizon):
                if (
                    budget_ms is not None
                    and (perf_counter() - start) * 1000 >= budget_ms
                ):
                    reason = "deadline"
                    aborted = True
                    break
                states = np.asarray(self.predictor(states, actions[:, t]))
                calls += self.rollouts
                if states.shape != (self.rollouts, len(z)):
                    raise ValueError("invalid model shape")
                valid &= np.isfinite(states).all(1)
                safe_states = np.where(np.isfinite(states), states, 0.0)
                step_cost = np.asarray(self.cost(safe_states), dtype=float)
                if step_cost.shape != (self.rollouts,):
                    raise ValueError("invalid cost shape")
                valid &= np.isfinite(step_cost)
                costs += self.discount**t * np.where(
                    np.isfinite(step_cost), step_cost, 0
                )
                if self.constraint is not None:
                    valid &= np.all(
                        safe_states
                        >= self.constraint.lower - self.constraint.tolerance,
                        axis=1,
                    )
                    valid &= np.all(
                        safe_states
                        <= self.constraint.upper + self.constraint.tolerance,
                        axis=1,
                    )
                states = safe_states
            if aborted:
                break
            eligible = np.flatnonzero(valid)
            if not len(eligible):
                reason = "no_feasible_rollout"
                completed = iteration + 1
                continue
            elite = eligible[
                np.argsort(costs[eligible])[
                    : max(1, int(self.rollouts * self.elite_fraction))
                ]
            ]
            if costs[elite[0]] < best_cost:
                best_cost = float(costs[elite[0]])
                best = actions[elite[0]].copy()
            mean = actions[elite].mean(0)
            std = np.maximum(actions[elite].std(0), 0.05)
            completed = iteration + 1
        if best is None:
            action = np.zeros(self.action_dim)
            self.warm_actions.fill(0)
        else:
            action = best[0].copy()
            self.warm_actions = np.vstack((best[1:], best[-1]))
        self.last_diagnostics = {
            "solver": "cem",
            "reason": reason,
            "feasible": best is not None,
            "model_calls": calls,
            "search_variables": self.horizon * self.action_dim,
            "iterations": completed,
            "latency_ms": (perf_counter() - start) * 1000,
            "fallback": None if best is not None else "no_verified_feasible_trajectory",
        }
        return Plan(
            action,
            None if best is None else best_cost,
            completed,
            completed * self.rollouts,
            self.last_diagnostics.copy(),
        )

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "warm_actions": self.warm_actions.copy(),
            "rng": self.rng.bit_generator.state,
        }

    def load_state_dict(self, state):
        if "warm_actions" in state:
            actions = np.asarray(state["warm_actions"], dtype=float)
            if actions.shape != self.warm_actions.shape:
                raise ValueError("invalid CEM checkpoint shape")
            self.warm_actions = actions.copy()
        self.rng.bit_generator.state = state["rng"]


def drift_diffusion(
    evidence, *, threshold=1.0, noise=0.1, dt=0.01, max_steps=1000, seed=0, urgency=0.0
):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    evidence = np.asarray(evidence, dtype=float)
    state = np.zeros_like(evidence)
    for step in range(max_steps):
        state += evidence * dt + noise * np.sqrt(dt) * rng.normal(size=state.shape)
        if state.max() >= max(0.1, threshold - urgency * step * dt):
            return int(state.argmax()), (step + 1) * dt
    return int(state.argmax()), max_steps * dt


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/planning/energy.py
========================================================================================
"""Action-space energy optimization with exact dynamics derivatives and budgets."""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from time import perf_counter
import numpy as np
from . import Plan


class TemporalSheaf:
    """Matrix-free temporal coboundary: D_e z = R_left z_e - R_right z_(e+1).

    E=weight/2 ||Dz||², gradient=weight D.T Dz. Default restrictions are the
    identity; nontrivial restrictions must be supplied explicitly. A consistency
    penalty is not a replacement for learned dynamics or a safety certificate.
    """

    def __init__(self, dimension: int, weight: float = 0.0, left=None, right=None):
        if dimension < 1 or not np.isfinite(weight) or weight < 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid sheaf regularizer")
        self.dimension, self.weight = dimension, weight
        self.identity = left is None and right is None
        self.left = np.eye(dimension) if left is None else np.asarray(left, dtype=float)
        self.right = (
            np.eye(dimension) if right is None else np.asarray(right, dtype=float)
        )
        if self.left.shape != (dimension, dimension) or self.right.shape != (
            dimension,
            dimension,
        ):
            raise ValueError("restriction shape mismatch")

    def energy_gradient(self, states: np.ndarray) -> tuple[float, np.ndarray]:
        states = np.asarray(states, dtype=float)
        if states.ndim != 2 or states.shape[1] != self.dimension:
            raise ValueError("invalid trajectory shape")
        gradient = np.zeros_like(states)
        if len(states) < 2 or not self.weight:
            return 0.0, gradient
        if self.identity:
            error = states[:-1] - states[1:]
            gradient[:-1] += self.weight * error
            gradient[1:] -= self.weight * error
            return float(0.5 * self.weight * np.sum(error * error)), gradient
        error = states[:-1] @ self.left.T - states[1:] @ self.right.T
        gradient[:-1] += self.weight * error @ self.left
        gradient[1:] -= self.weight * error @ self.right
        return float(0.5 * self.weight * np.sum(error * error)), gradient


class BoxStateConstraint:
    """Soft squared violation plus an explicit hard feasibility predicate."""

    def __init__(self, lower, upper, penalty=100.0, tolerance=1e-8):
        self.lower = np.asarray(lower, dtype=float)
        self.upper = np.asarray(upper, dtype=float)
        if (
            not np.isfinite(self.lower).all()
            or not np.isfinite(self.upper).all()
            or self.lower.shape != self.upper.shape
            or np.any(self.lower > self.upper)
            or penalty < 0
            or tolerance < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid state bounds")
        self.penalty, self.tolerance = penalty, tolerance

    def energy_gradient(self, states):
        low = np.minimum(states - self.lower, 0)
        high = np.maximum(states - self.upper, 0)
        return float(
            self.penalty * np.sum(low * low + high * high)
        ), 2 * self.penalty * (low + high)

    def feasible(self, states):
        return bool(
            np.all(states >= self.lower - self.tolerance)
            and np.all(states <= self.upper + self.tolerance)
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EnergyPlannerConfig:
    horizon: int = 12
    iterations: int = 20
    learning_rate: float = 0.3
    discount: float = 0.99
    action_penalty: float = 0.001
    slew_penalty: float = 0.01
    sheaf_weight: float = 0.0
    max_model_calls: int = 4096
    line_search_steps: int = 8
    gradient_tolerance: float = 1e-5

    def __post_init__(self):
        if (
            min(self.horizon, self.iterations, self.line_search_steps) < 1
            or self.max_model_calls < self.horizon
        ):
            raise ValueError("insufficient solver budget")
        if (
            not 0 < self.discount <= 1
            or self.learning_rate <= 0
            or self.gradient_tolerance <= 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid solver scales")
        if any(
            not np.isfinite(x) or x < 0
            for x in (self.action_penalty, self.slew_penalty, self.sheaf_weight)
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid energy weights")


class SolverBudgetExceeded(RuntimeError):
    pass


class EnergyPlanner:
    """Projected action descent through differentiable dynamics, with Armijo search.

    The search variables are H*action_dim, also for CEM. Increasing latent_dim
    increases model/Jacobian cost; it does not change that search dimension.
    Gradients use an explicit backward costate recursion, never torch.autograd.
    A model must supply predict(z,a) and linearize(z,a)->(next,F_z,F_a).
    Nonconvex trajectory optimization can stop at a local optimum.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model,
        cost_gradient,
        action_dim: int,
        latent_dim: int,
        *,
        config: EnergyPlannerConfig | None = None,
        constraint=None,
    ):
        if min(action_dim, latent_dim) < 1:
            raise ValueError("invalid model dimensions")
        self.model, self.cost_gradient, self.action_dim = (
            model,
            cost_gradient,
            action_dim,
        )
        self.config = config or EnergyPlannerConfig()
        self.constraint = constraint
        self.sheaf = TemporalSheaf(latent_dim, self.config.sheaf_weight)
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(
            0
        )  # common checkpoint interface; solver itself is deterministic
        self.warm_actions = np.zeros((self.config.horizon, action_dim))
        self.previous_action = np.zeros(action_dim)
        self.model_calls = 0
        self._deadline = None
        self.last_diagnostics = {}

    def _consume(self):
        if self.model_calls >= self.config.max_model_calls:
            raise SolverBudgetExceeded("model_call_budget")
        if self._deadline is not None and perf_counter() >= self._deadline:
            raise SolverBudgetExceeded("deadline")
        self.model_calls += 1

    def _rollout(self, z, actions, derivatives=False):
        states = []
        fz = []
        fa = []
        current = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)[None]
        for action in actions:
            self._consume()
            if derivatives:
                current, jz, ja = self.model.linearize(current, action[None])
                fz.append(jz[0])
                fa.append(ja[0])
            else:
                current = self.model.predict(current, action[None])
            current = np.asarray(current, dtype=float)
            if (
                current.shape != (1, self.sheaf.dimension)
                or not np.isfinite(current).all()
            ):
                raise ValueError("nonfinite/invalid model rollout")
            states.append(current[0].copy())
        return np.stack(states), fz, fa

    def _objective(self, states, actions):
        c = self.config
        cost, gradient = self.cost_gradient(states)
        cost = np.asarray(cost, dtype=float)
        gradient = np.asarray(gradient, dtype=float)
        if (
            cost.shape != (len(states),)
            or gradient.shape != states.shape
            or not np.isfinite(cost).all()
            or not np.isfinite(gradient).all()
        ):
            raise ValueError("cost_gradient returned invalid values")
        discount = c.discount ** np.arange(len(states))
        value = float(discount @ cost)
        gz = discount[:, None] * gradient
        sheaf_value, sheaf_gradient = self.sheaf.energy_gradient(states)
        value += sheaf_value
        gz += sheaf_gradient
        if self.constraint is not None:
            extra, grad = self.constraint.energy_gradient(states)
            value += extra
            gz += grad
        value += c.action_penalty * float(np.sum(actions * actions))
        ga = 2 * c.action_penalty * actions
        difference = actions - np.vstack((self.previous_action, actions[:-1]))
        value += c.slew_penalty * float(np.sum(difference * difference))
        ga += 2 * c.slew_penalty * difference
        ga[:-1] -= 2 * c.slew_penalty * difference[1:]
        return value, gz, ga

    def plan(self, z, budget_ms=None, initial_actions=None):
        z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
        if z.shape != (self.sheaf.dimension,) or not np.isfinite(z).all():
            raise ValueError("invalid initial state")
        if budget_ms is not None and (not np.isfinite(budget_ms) or budget_ms <= 0):
            raise ValueError("budget_ms must be positive")
        self.model_calls = 0
        start = perf_counter()
        self._deadline = None if budget_ms is None else start + budget_ms / 1000
        actions = (
            self.warm_actions.copy()
            if initial_actions is None
            else np.asarray(initial_actions, dtype=float).copy()
        )
        if actions.shape != self.warm_actions.shape or not np.isfinite(actions).all():
            raise ValueError("invalid initial action sequence")
        actions = np.clip(actions, -1, 1)
        best = None
        best_value = float("inf")
        completed = 0
        reason = "iterations"
        gradient_norm = None
        try:
            states, _, _ = self._rollout(z, actions)
            value, _, _ = self._objective(states, actions)
            if self.constraint is None or self.constraint.feasible(states):
                best = actions.copy()
                best_value = value
            for iteration in range(self.config.iterations):
                states, fz, fa = self._rollout(z, actions, derivatives=True)
                value, gz, grad = self._objective(states, actions)
                costate = np.zeros(len(z))
                for t in range(self.config.horizon - 1, -1, -1):
                    costate += gz[t]
                    grad[t] += fa[t].T @ costate
                    costate = fz[t].T @ costate
                # Projected stationarity, not raw gradient, handles active box constraints.
                projected = actions - np.clip(actions - grad, -1, 1)
                gradient_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(projected))
                if gradient_norm <= self.config.gradient_tolerance:
                    reason = "projected_stationary"
                    break
                accepted = False
                step = self.config.learning_rate
                for _ in range(self.config.line_search_steps):
                    candidate = np.clip(actions - step * grad, -1, 1)
                    trajectory, _, _ = self._rollout(z, candidate)
                    candidate_value, _, _ = self._objective(trajectory, candidate)
                    feasible = self.constraint is None or self.constraint.feasible(
                        trajectory
                    )
                    if feasible and candidate_value < best_value:
                        best = candidate.copy()
                        best_value = candidate_value
                    if candidate_value <= value + 1e-4 * float(
                        np.sum(grad * (candidate - actions))
                    ):
                        actions = candidate
                        accepted = True
                        break
                    step *= 0.5
                completed = iteration + 1
                if not accepted:
                    reason = "line_search_stalled"
                    break
        except SolverBudgetExceeded as error:
            reason = str(error)
        feasible = best is not None
        if feasible:
            action = best[0].copy()
            self.warm_actions = np.vstack((best[1:], best[-1]))
        else:
            # This is a stop command, NOT a claim that zero is safe in every world.
            action = np.zeros(self.action_dim)
            self.warm_actions.fill(0)
        self.previous_action = action.copy()
        self.last_diagnostics = {
            "solver": "energy",
            "reason": reason,
            "feasible": feasible,
            "model_calls": self.model_calls,
            "search_variables": self.config.horizon * self.action_dim,
            "iterations": completed,
            "projected_gradient_norm": gradient_norm,
            "latency_ms": (perf_counter() - start) * 1000,
            "fallback": None if feasible else "no_verified_feasible_trajectory",
        }
        return Plan(
            action,
            float(best_value) if feasible else None,
            completed,
            0,
            self.last_diagnostics.copy(),
        )

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "warm_actions": self.warm_actions.copy(),
            "previous_action": self.previous_action.copy(),
            "rng": self.rng.bit_generator.state,
        }

    def load_state_dict(self, state):
        warm = np.asarray(state["warm_actions"], dtype=float)
        previous = np.asarray(state["previous_action"], dtype=float)
        if (
            warm.shape != self.warm_actions.shape
            or previous.shape != self.previous_action.shape
        ):
            raise ValueError("planner checkpoint dimension mismatch")
        self.warm_actions = warm.copy()
        self.previous_action = previous.copy()
        self.rng.bit_generator.state = state["rng"]

"""Explicit opt-in research profiles; choosing a profile is not an evidence claim."""

from dataclasses import asdict
from .runtime import RuntimeConfig


def runtime_config(profile="baseline", overrides=None):
    options = {
        "baseline": {},
        "adaptive": dict(
            world_backend="equilibrium",
            adaptive_memory=True,
            planning=True,
            planner_backend="energy",
            world_online_learning=True,
            neuromodulation=True,
            planner_iterations=12,
            sleep_every=32,
        ),
        "brain": dict(
            world_backend="adex",
            adaptive_memory=True,
            planning=True,
            planner_backend="energy",
            world_online_learning=True,
            neuromodulation=True,
            planner_iterations=12,
            sleep_every=32,
        ),
    }
    if profile not in options:
        raise ValueError("unknown runtime profile")
    values = {**options[profile], **(overrides or {})}
    # Validate all names/types through the dataclass instead of silently ignoring typos.
    return RuntimeConfig(**values)


def describe(config: RuntimeConfig):
    return {
        "config": asdict(config),
        "status": "experimental; no new performance measurements in this revision",
        "autograd_used": config.world_backend == "torch",
        "retention_scope": "versioned retained activation span; released directions may change",
        "finite_capacity": True,
        "time_budget": "soft wall budget or deterministic operation budget",
    }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/reasoning/__init__.py

"""Budgeted search, exact arithmetic tools, and held-out confidence calibration."""

from __future__ import annotations
import ast
import operator
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize_scalar
from scipy.special import softmax


class Calculator:
    """Evaluate bounded arithmetic ASTs; no names, calls, attributes or code execution."""

    operations = {
        ast.Add: operator.add,
        ast.Sub: operator.sub,
        ast.Mult: operator.mul,
        ast.Div: operator.truediv,
        ast.Pow: operator.pow,
        ast.Mod: operator.mod,
    }

    def __call__(self, expression):
        if len(expression) > 512:
            raise ValueError("expression too long")
        tree = ast.parse(expression, mode="eval")
        if len(list(ast.walk(tree))) > 128:
            raise ValueError("expression too complex")

        def visit(node):
            if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and type(node.value) in (int, float):
                value = node.value
            elif isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp) and isinstance(
                node.op, (ast.UAdd, ast.USub)
            ):
                value = visit(node.operand) * (
                    1 if isinstance(node.op, ast.UAdd) else -1
                )
            elif isinstance(node, ast.BinOp) and type(node.op) in self.operations:
                a, b = visit(node.left), visit(node.right)
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Pow) and abs(b) > 16:
                    raise ValueError("exponent budget exceeded")
                value = self.operations[type(node.op)](a, b)
            else:
                raise ValueError("only arithmetic is allowed")
            if (
                isinstance(value, complex)
                or not np.isfinite(value)
                or abs(value) > 1e100
            ):
                raise ValueError("result outside numeric budget")
            return value

        return visit(tree.body)


class ToolRegistry:
    def __init__(self):
        self.tools = {"calculator": Calculator()}

    def register(self, name, callback):
        if name in self.tools:
            raise ValueError("duplicate tool")
        self.tools[name] = callback

    def call(self, name, *args, **kwargs):
        if name not in self.tools:
            raise ValueError("tool not registered")
        return self.tools[name](*args, **kwargs)


@dataclass
class SearchResult:
    path: list[tuple[int, int]]
    expanded: int
    verified: bool


def grid_search(grid, start, goal, budget=1000):
    """Exact BFS verifier, bounded by expansions; more budget preserves found solutions."""
    grid = np.asarray(grid, dtype=bool)
    if grid.ndim != 2 or budget < 0:
        raise ValueError("invalid grid/budget")
    for p in (start, goal):
        if not 0 <= p[0] < grid.shape[0] or not 0 <= p[1] < grid.shape[1] or grid[p]:
            raise ValueError("invalid endpoint")
    start, goal = tuple(start), tuple(goal)
    queue = deque([start])
    parent = {start: None}
    expanded = 0
    while queue and expanded < budget:
        node = queue.popleft()
        expanded += 1
        if node == goal:
            path = []
            while node is not None:
                path.append(node)
                node = parent[node]
            return SearchResult(path[::-1], expanded, True)
        for delta in ((0, 1), (1, 0), (-1, 0), (0, -1)):
            nxt = (node[0] + delta[0], node[1] + delta[1])
            if (
                0 <= nxt[0] < grid.shape[0]
                and 0 <= nxt[1] < grid.shape[1]
                and not grid[nxt]
                and nxt not in parent
            ):
                parent[nxt] = node
                queue.append(nxt)
    return SearchResult([], expanded, False)


def hierarchical_route(grid, start, subgoals, budget=1000):
    path = [tuple(start)]
    used = 0
    for goal in subgoals:
        result = grid_search(grid, path[-1], tuple(goal), max(0, budget - used))
        used += result.expanded
        if not result.verified:
            return SearchResult([], used, False)
        path.extend(result.path[1:])
    return SearchResult(path, used, True)


def expected_calibration_error(probabilities, labels, bins=10):
    p = np.asarray(probabilities)
    labels = np.asarray(labels)
    if (
        p.ndim != 2
        or len(p) == 0
        or len(labels) != len(p)
        or np.any(p < 0)
        or not np.allclose(p.sum(1), 1)
    ):
        raise ValueError("invalid probability distribution")
    confidence = p.max(1)
    correct = p.argmax(1) == labels
    value = 0.0
    index = np.minimum((confidence * bins).astype(int), bins - 1)
    for i in range(bins):
        selected = index == i
        if selected.any():
            value += selected.mean() * abs(
                confidence[selected].mean() - correct[selected].mean()
            )
    return float(value)


class TemperatureCalibrator:
    def __init__(self):
        self.temperature = 1.0

    def fit(self, logits, labels):
        logits = np.asarray(logits)
        labels = np.asarray(labels)

        def nll(log_temperature):
            p = softmax(logits / np.exp(log_temperature), axis=1)
            return -np.log(np.maximum(p[np.arange(len(p)), labels], 1e-12)).mean()

        result = minimize_scalar(nll, bounds=(-4, 4), method="bounded")
        if not result.success:
            raise RuntimeError("calibration did not converge")
        self.temperature = float(np.exp(result.x))
        return self

    def predict(self, logits):
        return softmax(np.asarray(logits) / self.temperature, axis=1)


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/reasoning/local_policy.py
========================================================================================
"""Amortized control with a fixed random feature map and local output delta rule."""

import numpy as np


class LocalPolicy:
    """a=tanh(tanh(zR+b)W+c); only the readout W,c adapts to a teacher action.

    This is a local supervised readout, not a replacement for all policy-learning
    algorithms. Hidden random features are frozen; no hidden-layer backward pass.
    """

    def __init__(self, inputs, outputs, seed=0, hidden=64):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.inputs, self.outputs = inputs, outputs
        self.projection = rng.normal(0, 1 / np.sqrt(inputs), (inputs, hidden))
        self.bias = rng.normal(0, 0.1, hidden)
        self.weights = np.zeros((hidden, outputs))
        self.output_bias = np.zeros(outputs)

    def features(self, x):
        return np.tanh(np.asarray(x) @ self.projection + self.bias)

    def predict(self, x):
        return np.tanh(self.features(x) @ self.weights + self.output_bias)

    def learn(self, x, target, lr=0.01):
        x = np.atleast_2d(x)
        target = np.atleast_2d(target)
        features = self.features(x)
        pred = np.tanh(features @ self.weights + self.output_bias)
        if target.shape != pred.shape or not np.isfinite(target).all() or lr < 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid policy target")
        delta = np.clip((pred - target) * (1 - pred * pred), -1, 1)
        self.weights -= lr * features.T @ delta / len(x)
        self.output_bias -= lr * delta.mean(0)
        return float(np.mean((pred - target) ** 2))

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "inputs": self.inputs,
            "outputs": self.outputs,
            **{
                key: getattr(self, key).copy()
                for key in ("projection", "bias", "weights", "output_bias")
            },
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state):
        obj = cls(
            state["inputs"], state["outputs"], hidden=np.shape(state["weights"])[0]
        )
        for key in ("projection", "bias", "weights", "output_bias"):
            setattr(obj, key, np.asarray(state[key]).copy())
        return obj

"""Opt-in hypotheses: mathematical checks do not establish task-level benefit."""

import numpy as np


class PhaseOscillatorLayer:
    """dtheta_i/dt=omega_i+K/N sum_j A_ij sin(theta_j-theta_i-alpha_ij)."""

    def __init__(
        self, size=32, coupling=2.0, seed=0, adjacency=None, lag=None, backend="numpy"
    ):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.theta = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size)
        self.omega = rng.normal(0, 0.1, size)
        self.coupling = coupling
        self.adjacency = (
            np.ones((size, size))
            if adjacency is None
            else np.asarray(adjacency, dtype=float)
        )
        self.lag = (
            np.zeros((size, size)) if lag is None else np.asarray(lag, dtype=float)
        )
        if self.adjacency.shape != (size, size) or self.lag.shape != (size, size):
            raise ValueError("invalid coupling shape")
        self.backend = backend

    def step(self, dt=0.01):
        if dt <= 0:
            raise ValueError("dt must be positive")
        if self.backend == "rust":
            from nova.core import require_native

            self.theta = np.asarray(
                require_native().kuramoto_step(
                    self.theta.tolist(),
                    self.omega.tolist(),
                    self.adjacency.ravel().tolist(),
                    self.lag.ravel().tolist(),
                    self.coupling,
                    dt,
                )
            )
        elif self.backend == "numpy":
            derivative = self.omega + self.coupling * np.mean(
                self.adjacency
                * np.sin(self.theta[None, :] - self.theta[:, None] - self.lag),
                axis=1,
            )
            self.theta = (self.theta + dt * derivative) % (2 * np.pi)
        else:
            raise ValueError("backend must be numpy or rust")
        return self.theta.copy()

    @property
    def order(self):
        return float(abs(np.exp(1j * self.theta).mean()))


class QuadraticEquilibriumPropagation:
    """E(s)=1/2 s.T A s-s.T Bx; C=1/2 ||P s-y||².

    Free and nudged equilibria are reached by actual relaxation. Parameter
    gradient is -(s_beta-s_free)x.T/beta, compared to implicit backprop.
    A is symmetric positive definite; this is a small quadratic validation model.
    """

    def __init__(self, inputs=3, state=5, outputs=2, seed=0):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        m = rng.normal(0, 0.1, (state, state))
        self.A = np.eye(state) + m.T @ m
        self.B = rng.normal(0, 0.2, (state, inputs))
        self.P = np.eye(state)[-outputs:]
        self.last_iterations = (0, 0)

    def relax(
        self, x, y=None, beta=0.0, initial=None, tolerance=1e-11, max_steps=10000
    ):
        if beta < 0:
            raise ValueError("beta must be nonnegative")
        s = np.zeros(len(self.A)) if initial is None else initial.copy()
        matrix = self.A + beta * self.P.T @ self.P
        drive = self.B @ x + (beta * self.P.T @ y if y is not None else 0)
        dt = 0.8 / np.linalg.eigvalsh(matrix).max()
        for i in range(max_steps):
            gradient = matrix @ s - drive
            if np.linalg.norm(gradient) < tolerance:
                return s, i
            s -= dt * gradient
        raise RuntimeError("equilibrium did not converge")

    def gradient(self, x, y, beta=1e-4):
        if beta <= 0:
            raise ValueError("beta must be positive")
        free, n0 = self.relax(x)
        nudged, nb = self.relax(x, y, beta, free)
        self.last_iterations = (n0, nb)
        return -np.outer(nudged - free, x) / beta


class SheafCoherence:
    """Cellular sheaf coboundary D_e x = R_eu x_u - R_ev x_v; L=D.T D."""

    def __init__(self, nodes, stalk_dim, edges, restrictions=None):
        self.D = np.zeros((len(edges) * stalk_dim, nodes * stalk_dim))
        for i, (u, v) in enumerate(edges):
            if not 0 <= u < nodes or not 0 <= v < nodes or u == v:
                raise ValueError("invalid edge")
            ru, rv = (
                (np.eye(stalk_dim), np.eye(stalk_dim))
                if restrictions is None
                else restrictions[i]
            )
            self.D[
                i * stalk_dim : (i + 1) * stalk_dim, u * stalk_dim : (u + 1) * stalk_dim
            ] = ru
            self.D[
                i * stalk_dim : (i + 1) * stalk_dim, v * stalk_dim : (v + 1) * stalk_dim
            ] = -rv
        self.L = self.D.T @ self.D

    def energy(self, x):
        return float(np.linalg.norm(self.D @ np.asarray(x).ravel()) ** 2 / 2)

    def diffuse(self, x, steps=20, dt=None):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
        shape = x.shape
        x = x.ravel().copy()
        maximum = np.linalg.eigvalsh(self.L).max()
        if maximum == 0:
            return x.reshape(shape)
        dt = 0.9 / maximum if dt is None else dt
        if dt <= 0 or dt * maximum >= 2:
            raise ValueError("unstable diffusion step")
        for _ in range(steps):
            x -= dt * self.L @ x
        return x.reshape(shape)
