"""Integrated cognitive cycle with optional adaptive memory and physical latent inference."""

from __future__ import annotations
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict, replace
import json
from pathlib import Path
from time import perf_counter
import numpy as np
from nova.continual import ProtectedLinear
from nova.memory import (
    SensoryBuffer,
    WorkingMemory,
    EpisodicMemory,
    Episode,
    SemanticGraph,
)
from nova.synapse import DiffusiveCascade
from nova.neuromod import Neuromodulator
from nova.planning import LatentPlanner
from nova.values import InternalState
from nova.values.progress import LearningProgress
from nova.checkpoint import save_state, load_state, CheckpointFormatError


@dataclass
class RuntimeConfig:
    seed: int = 0
    observation_dim: int = 12
    latent_dim: int = 8
    action_dim: int = 2
    memory_capacity: int = 128
    sleep_every: int = 100
    tick_budget_ms: float = 100.0
    planning: bool = False
    neuromodulation: bool = False
    deterministic: bool = True
    world_backend: str = "torch"
    world_online_learning: bool = False
    adaptive_memory: bool = False
    memory_policy: str = "hybrid"
    memory_growth: int = 8
    max_memory_features: int = 128
    memory_reserve: float = 0.2
    max_memory_bytes: int = 32 * 1024 * 1024
    planner_backend: str = "cem"
    planner_horizon: int = 6
    planner_iterations: int = 3
    planner_rollouts: int = 64
    solver_max_calls: int = 512
    sheaf_weight: float = 0.0
    spiking_units: int = 100
    spiking_steps: int = 100
    spiking_backend: str = "numpy"
    spiking_plasticity: bool = False
    history_limit: int = 2048
    goal_latent: list[float] | None = None
    state_lower: list[float] | None = None
    state_upper: list[float] | None = None

    def __post_init__(self):
        if (
            min(
                self.observation_dim,
                self.latent_dim,
                self.action_dim,
                self.memory_capacity,
                self.sleep_every,
                self.history_limit,
                self.planner_horizon,
                self.planner_iterations,
                self.planner_rollouts,
                self.spiking_units,
                self.spiking_steps,
            )
            < 1
        ):
            raise ValueError("dimensions and budgets must be positive")
        if not np.isfinite(self.tick_budget_ms) or self.tick_budget_ms <= 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid time budget")
        if self.world_backend not in (
            "torch",
            "equilibrium",
            "adex",
        ) or self.planner_backend not in ("cem", "energy"):
            raise ValueError("unknown world/planner backend")
        if self.spiking_plasticity and self.world_backend != "adex":
            raise ValueError("spiking plasticity requires AdEx backend")
        if self.world_online_learning and self.world_backend == "torch":
            raise ValueError(
                "online world learning is provided by the local EqProp backends"
            )


class _Dynamics:
    def __init__(self, runtime):
        self.runtime = runtime

    def predict(self, z, a):
        return self.runtime.predict(z, a)

    def linearize(self, z, a):
        return self.runtime.linearize(z, a)


class CognitiveRuntime:
    """Versioned memory + real latent backend + amortized/energy-based control.

    Baseline behavior remains selectable. Adaptive and physical backends are
    experimental and unbenchmarked in this revision. Encoders are frozen for
    online rate-model training. A stateful AdEx representation has history
    dependence; stored-activation protection is not raw-observation retention.
    """

    def __init__(self, config: RuntimeConfig | None = None):
        self.config = config or RuntimeConfig()
        c = self.config
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(c.seed)
        if c.world_backend == "torch":
            import torch
            from torch import nn
            from nova.jepa import JEPA

            with torch.random.fork_rng():
                torch.manual_seed(c.seed)
                self.world = JEPA(c.observation_dim, c.latent_dim, c.action_dim)
                self.policy = nn.Sequential(
                    nn.Linear(c.latent_dim, 32),
                    nn.Tanh(),
                    nn.Linear(32, c.action_dim),
                    nn.Tanh(),
                )
            self.world.context.requires_grad_(False)
            self.world.target.requires_grad_(False)
        else:
            from nova.jepa.equilibrium import EquilibriumWorldModel
            from nova.brain.latent import SpikingWorldModel
            from nova.reasoning.local_policy import LocalPolicy

            if c.world_backend == "adex":
                self.world = SpikingWorldModel(
                    c.observation_dim,
                    c.latent_dim,
                    c.action_dim,
                    seed=c.seed,
                    spiking_units=c.spiking_units,
                    spiking_steps=c.spiking_steps,
                    spiking_backend=c.spiking_backend,
                )
            else:
                self.world = EquilibriumWorldModel(
                    c.observation_dim, c.latent_dim, c.action_dim, seed=c.seed
                )
            self.world.freeze_encoder()
            self.policy = LocalPolicy(c.latent_dim, c.action_dim, seed=c.seed)
        if c.adaptive_memory:
            from nova.continual.adaptive import AdaptiveSubspaceLinear, AdaptiveConfig

            cfg = AdaptiveConfig(
                policy=c.memory_policy,
                growth_step=c.memory_growth,
                max_features=c.max_memory_features,
                max_state_bytes=c.max_memory_bytes,
                reserve_fraction=c.memory_reserve,
            )
            self.continual = AdaptiveSubspaceLinear(
                c.latent_dim, c.latent_dim, seed=c.seed, config=cfg
            )
        else:
            self.continual = ProtectedLinear(c.latent_dim, c.latent_dim, seed=c.seed)
        self.cascade = DiffusiveCascade(c.latent_dim)
        self.mod = (
            Neuromodulator() if c.neuromodulation else Neuromodulator(frozenset())
        )
        self.internal = InternalState()
        self.progress = LearningProgress()
        self.sensory = SensoryBuffer()
        self.working = WorkingMemory(dimensions=c.latent_dim)
        self.episodic = EpisodicMemory(c.memory_capacity)
        self.semantic = SemanticGraph(capacity=4 * c.memory_capacity)
        self.rest = self.rng.normal(0, 0.03, c.latent_dim)
        self.z = self.rest.copy()
        self.prediction = self.rest.copy()
        self.last_action = np.zeros(c.action_dim)
        self.z_version = 0
        self.tick_index = 0
        self.previous_observation = None
        self.metrics = deque(maxlen=c.history_limit)
        self.external_state = {}
        self.last_solver = {}
        self.last_world_update = {}
        self.goal = (
            np.zeros(c.latent_dim)
            if c.goal_latent is None
            else np.asarray(c.goal_latent, dtype=float)
        )
        if self.goal.shape != (c.latent_dim,) or not np.isfinite(self.goal).all():
            raise ValueError("goal shape mismatch")
        self._create_planner()

    def _create_planner(self):
        c = self.config
        constraint = None
        if c.state_lower is not None or c.state_upper is not None:
            from nova.planning.energy import BoxStateConstraint

            if c.state_lower is None or c.state_upper is None:
                raise ValueError("both state bounds required")
            constraint = BoxStateConstraint(c.state_lower, c.state_upper)
            if constraint.lower.shape != (c.latent_dim,):
                raise ValueError("state constraint dimension mismatch")
        if c.planner_backend == "energy":
            from nova.planning.energy import EnergyPlanner, EnergyPlannerConfig

            cfg = EnergyPlannerConfig(
                horizon=c.planner_horizon,
                iterations=c.planner_iterations,
                max_model_calls=c.solver_max_calls,
                sheaf_weight=c.sheaf_weight,
            )
            self.planner = EnergyPlanner(
                _Dynamics(self),
                self.planning_cost_gradient,
                c.action_dim,
                c.latent_dim,
                config=cfg,
                constraint=constraint,
            )
        else:
            self.planner = LatentPlanner(
                self.predict,
                self.planning_cost,
                c.action_dim,
                seed=c.seed,
                rollouts=c.planner_rollouts,
                horizon=c.planner_horizon,
                iterations=c.planner_iterations,
                constraint=constraint,
            )

    def _encode(self, observation):
        if self.config.world_backend != "torch":
            return self.world.encode(observation)[0]
        import torch

        with torch.no_grad():
            return (
                self.world.context(torch.as_tensor(observation, dtype=torch.float32))
                .numpy()
                .astype(float)
            )

    def predict(self, z, a):
        if self.config.world_backend != "torch":
            return self.world.predict(z, a)
        import torch

        with torch.no_grad():
            return self.world.predictor(
                torch.as_tensor(z, dtype=torch.float32),
                torch.as_tensor(a, dtype=torch.float32),
            ).numpy()

    def linearize(self, z, a):
        if self.config.world_backend != "torch":
            return self.world.linearize(z, a)
        # Explicit derivative of the baseline Linear -> SiLU -> Linear predictor.
        z, a = np.asarray(z, dtype=float), np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
        network = self.world.predictor.net
        w1, b1 = network[0].weight.detach().numpy(), network[0].bias.detach().numpy()
        w2, b2 = network[2].weight.detach().numpy(), network[2].bias.detach().numpy()
        pre = np.c_[z, a] @ w1.T + b1
        from scipy.special import expit

        sigmoid = expit(pre)
        derivative = sigmoid * (1 + pre * (1 - sigmoid))
        jacobian = np.einsum("oh,bh,hi->boi", w2, derivative, w1)
        output = (pre * sigmoid) @ w2.T + b2
        return (
            output,
            jacobian[:, :, : self.config.latent_dim],
            jacobian[:, :, self.config.latent_dim :],
        )

    @staticmethod
    def cost(z):
        return np.sum(np.asarray(z) ** 2, axis=-1)

    @staticmethod
    def cost_gradient(z):
        return CognitiveRuntime.cost(z), 2 * np.asarray(z)

    def planning_cost(self, z):
        return np.sum((np.asarray(z) - self.goal) ** 2, axis=-1)

    def planning_cost_gradient(self, z):
        return self.planning_cost(z), 2 * (np.asarray(z) - self.goal)

    def set_goal(self, latent):
        goal = np.asarray(latent, dtype=float)
        if goal.shape != self.goal.shape or not np.isfinite(goal).all():
            raise ValueError("invalid goal")
        self.goal = goal.copy()
        self.planner.warm_actions.fill(0)

    def _policy_predict(self, z):
        if self.config.world_backend != "torch":
            return self.policy.predict(z)
        import torch

        with torch.no_grad():
            return self.policy(torch.as_tensor(z, dtype=torch.float32)).numpy()

    def _policy_learn(self, z, action):
        if self.config.world_backend != "torch":
            return self.policy.learn(z, action, lr=0.001)
        import torch

        optimizer = torch.optim.SGD(self.policy.parameters(), lr=0.001)
        loss = (
            (
                self.policy(torch.as_tensor(z, dtype=torch.float32))
                - torch.as_tensor(action, dtype=torch.float32)
            )
            .square()
            .mean()
        )
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()
        optimizer.step()
        return float(loss.detach())

    def _memory_input(self, z, version=None):
        x = np.atleast_2d(z)
        return self.continual.encode(x, version) if self.config.adaptive_memory else x

    @property
    def feature_version(self):
        return self.continual.feature_version if self.config.adaptive_memory else 0

    def tick(self, observation=None, reward=0.0):
        start = perf_counter()
        idle = observation is None
        if not np.isfinite(reward):
            raise ValueError("nonfinite reward")
        if not idle:
            observation = np.asarray(observation, dtype=float)
            if (
                observation.shape != (self.config.observation_dim,)
                or not np.isfinite(observation).all()
            ):
                raise ValueError("invalid observation")
        previous = self.z.copy()
        previous_version = self.z_version
        previous_action = self.last_action.copy()
        self.tick_index += 1
        if idle:
            self.z = 0.99 * self.z + 0.01 * self.rest
        else:
            self.sensory.append(observation.copy())
            self.z = np.asarray(self._encode(observation), dtype=float)
        self.z_version = self.feature_version
        error = float(np.mean((self.z - self.prediction) ** 2))
        uncertainty = float(np.clip(error, 0, 1))
        self.mod.update(
            error, reward, -self.cost(previous), -self.cost(self.z), uncertainty
        )
        recalled = self.episodic.retrieve(self.z, limit=1)
        sensory_weight = (
            self.mod.sensory_weight() if self.config.neuromodulation else 0.9
        )
        context = (
            self.z
            if not recalled
            else sensory_weight * self.z + (1 - sensory_weight) * recalled[0].embedding
        )
        self.working.update(context)
        slow = (
            not idle
            and self.config.planning
            and (uncertainty > 0.1 or self.mod.surprise > 0.5)
            and self.internal.energy > 0.1
        )
        self.last_solver = {}
        if idle:
            action = np.zeros(self.config.action_dim)
            mode = "idle"
        elif slow:
            if self.config.neuromodulation:
                if self.config.planner_backend == "energy":
                    self.planner.config = replace(
                        self.planner.config, discount=self.mod.discount()
                    )
                else:
                    self.planner.discount = self.mod.discount()
            result = self.planner.plan(
                context,
                None if self.config.deterministic else self.config.tick_budget_ms * 0.7,
            )
            action = result.action
            mode = "deliberate"
            self.last_solver = result.diagnostics
            if result.cost is not None:
                self._policy_learn(context, action)
        else:
            action = self._policy_predict(context)
            mode = "fast"
        world_loss = None
        if not idle:
            memory_input = self._memory_input(previous, previous_version)
            self.continual.step(
                memory_input, self.z[None], self.mod.learning_rate(0.001)
            )
            if self.config.adaptive_memory:
                self.continual.observe(memory_input)
                self.continual.age(1.0, consolidation=0.01 * self.mod.consolidation())
            self.cascade.step(np.full(self.config.latent_dim, error), dt=0.1)
            self.episodic.threshold = self.mod.write_threshold(0.05)
            self.episodic.write(
                Episode(
                    str(self.tick_index),
                    self.z.copy(),
                    f"observation at tick {self.tick_index}",
                    f"sensor:{self.tick_index}",
                    f"{self.tick_index:012d}",
                    surprise=error,
                    valence=self.internal.valence,
                    feature_version=self.z_version,
                )
            )
            if (
                self.config.world_online_learning
                and self.previous_observation is not None
            ):
                update = self.world.learn_latent(
                    previous[None],
                    previous_action[None],
                    self.z[None],
                    lr=self.mod.learning_rate(0.001),
                )
                world_loss = update.get("loss")
                self.last_world_update = {
                    key: value
                    for key, value in update.items()
                    if not isinstance(value, np.ndarray)
                }
            if self.config.spiking_plasticity:
                self.world.spiking.learn_local(self.mod.reward_pe)
            self.previous_observation = observation.copy()
        self.last_action = np.asarray(action, dtype=float).copy()
        self.prediction = self.predict(self.z[None], self.last_action[None])[0]
        self.internal.update(
            error,
            0.8 if slow else 0.1,
            len(self.episodic.episodes) / self.config.memory_capacity,
            reward,
        )
        self.internal.curiosity = 0.0 if idle else self.progress.update(error)
        if self.tick_index % self.config.sleep_every == 0:
            self.sleep()
        elapsed = (perf_counter() - start) * 1000
        metric = {
            "tick": self.tick_index,
            "mode": mode,
            "error": error,
            "free_dims": self.continual.free_dims,
            "memory": len(self.episodic.episodes),
            "latency_ms": elapsed,
            "budget_exceeded": elapsed > self.config.tick_budget_ms,
            "energy": self.internal.energy,
            "curiosity": self.internal.curiosity,
            "progress_standard_error": self.progress.standard_error,
            "world_backend": self.config.world_backend,
            "planner_backend": self.config.planner_backend,
            "world_loss": world_loss,
            "semantic_facts": len(self.semantic.facts),
            "semantic_evicted": self.semantic.evicted,
            "feature_version": self.feature_version,
            "memory_width": self.continual.in_features,
            "solver_model_calls": self.last_solver.get("model_calls", 0),
            "solver_feasible": self.last_solver.get("feasible"),
            "world_converged": getattr(self.world, "last_diagnostics", {}).get(
                "converged"
            ),
        }
        if self.config.adaptive_memory:
            metric.update(self.continual.capacity_metrics())
        self.metrics.append(metric)
        return {
            "action": self.last_action.copy(),
            "mode": mode,
            "latent": self.z.copy(),
            "metrics": metric,
            "solver": self.last_solver.copy(),
        }

    def remember_anchor(self, episode: Episode):
        if not episode.anchor:
            raise ValueError("anchor must be explicit")
        if episode.embedding.shape != (self.config.latent_dim,):
            raise ValueError("anchor latent shape mismatch")
        x = self._memory_input(episode.embedding, episode.feature_version)
        if not self.episodic.write(episode, force=True):
            raise ValueError("episode capacity contains only anchors")
        if self.config.adaptive_memory:
            self.continual.consolidate(x, anchor=True)
        else:
            self.continual.consolidate(x)

    def sleep(self):
        self.episodic.sleep(
            lambda e: self.semantic.add(
                e.id,
                "observed",
                e.content,
                e.source,
                timestamp=e.timestamp,
                anchor=e.anchor,
            )
        )
        if self.episodic.episodes:
            x = np.stack([e.embedding for e in self.episodic.episodes])
            versions = np.array([e.feature_version for e in self.episodic.episodes])
            self.continual.consolidate(self._memory_input(x, versions))
        if self.config.adaptive_memory:
            self.continual.manage_capacity()
        self.cascade.consolidate(self.mod.consolidation() * 0.01)
        self.internal.sleep()

    def state_dict(self):
        if self.config.world_backend == "torch":
            world = {k: v.detach().numpy() for k, v in self.world.state_dict().items()}
            policy = {
                k: v.detach().numpy() for k, v in self.policy.state_dict().items()
            }
        else:
            world = self.world.state_dict()
            policy = self.policy.state_dict()
        memory = (
            self.continual.state_dict()
            if self.config.adaptive_memory
            else {
                **{
                    k: getattr(self.continual, k).copy()
                    for k in ("W_base", "dW", "U", "singular_values", "last_update")
                },
                "rank_max": self.continual.rank_max,
                "energy_threshold": self.continual.energy_threshold,
            }
        )
        return {
            "kind": "nova-runtime",
            "config": asdict(self.config),
            "world": world,
            "policy": policy,
            "memory": memory,
            "cascade": self.cascade.state.copy(),
            "working": self.working.state.copy(),
            "working_cursor": self.working.cursor,
            "z": self.z.copy(),
            "prediction": self.prediction.copy(),
            "rest": self.rest.copy(),
            "last_action": self.last_action.copy(),
            "goal": self.goal.copy(),
            "z_version": self.z_version,
            "tick": self.tick_index,
            "rng": self.rng.bit_generator.state,
            "planner": self.planner.state_dict()
            if hasattr(self.planner, "state_dict")
            else {"rng": self.planner.rng.bit_generator.state},
            "mod": {**asdict(self.mod), "enabled": sorted(self.mod.enabled)},
            "internal": asdict(self.internal),
            "progress": self.progress.state_dict(),
            "episodes": [asdict(e) for e in self.episodic.episodes],
            "facts": self.semantic.facts,
            "semantic_evicted": self.semantic.evicted,
            "sensory": list(self.sensory),
            "previous_observation": self.previous_observation,
            "episodic_threshold": self.episodic.threshold,
            "metrics": list(self.metrics),
            "external_state": self.external_state,
            "last_solver": self.last_solver,
            "last_world_update": self.last_world_update,
        }

    def save(self, path):
        save_state(path, self.state_dict())

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state):
        if state.get("kind") != "nova-runtime":
            raise ValueError("not a runtime checkpoint")
        obj = cls(RuntimeConfig(**state["config"]))
        if obj.config.world_backend == "torch":
            import torch

            for name in ("world", "policy"):
                getattr(obj, name).load_state_dict(
                    {
                        k: torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(v).copy())
                        for k, v in state[name].items()
                    }
                )
        else:
            from nova.jepa.equilibrium import EquilibriumWorldModel
            from nova.brain.latent import SpikingWorldModel
            from nova.reasoning.local_policy import LocalPolicy

            model_class = (
                SpikingWorldModel
                if obj.config.world_backend == "adex"
                else EquilibriumWorldModel
            )
            obj.world = model_class.from_state_dict(state["world"])
            obj.policy = LocalPolicy.from_state_dict(state["policy"])
        if obj.config.adaptive_memory:
            from nova.continual.adaptive import AdaptiveSubspaceLinear

            obj.continual = AdaptiveSubspaceLinear.from_state_dict(state["memory"])
        else:
            for name in ("W_base", "dW", "U", "singular_values", "last_update"):
                setattr(obj.continual, name, np.asarray(state["memory"][name]).copy())
            obj.continual.W_base.flags.writeable = False
            obj.continual.rank_max = state["memory"]["rank_max"]
            obj.continual.energy_threshold = state["memory"]["energy_threshold"]
        for name in ("z", "prediction", "rest", "last_action"):
            setattr(obj, name, np.asarray(state[name]).copy())
        obj.goal = np.asarray(state.get("goal", obj.goal)).copy()
        obj.z_version = state["z_version"]
        obj.tick_index = state["tick"]
        obj.rng.bit_generator.state = state["rng"]
        obj.cascade.state = np.asarray(state["cascade"]).copy()
        obj.working.state = np.asarray(state["working"]).copy()
        obj.working.cursor = state["working_cursor"]
        mod = state["mod"].copy()
        mod["enabled"] = frozenset(mod["enabled"])
        obj.mod = Neuromodulator(**mod)
        obj.internal = InternalState(**state["internal"])
        obj.progress = LearningProgress.from_state_dict(state["progress"])
        obj.episodic.episodes = [
            Episode(**{**e, "participants": tuple(e["participants"])})
            for e in state["episodes"]
        ]
        obj.episodic.threshold = state["episodic_threshold"]
        obj.semantic.facts = state["facts"]
        obj.semantic.evicted = state.get("semantic_evicted", 0)
        obj.sensory.extend(state["sensory"])
        obj.previous_observation = state["previous_observation"]
        obj.metrics = deque(state["metrics"], maxlen=obj.config.history_limit)
        obj.external_state = state["external_state"]
        obj.last_solver = state.get("last_solver", {})
        obj.last_world_update = state.get("last_world_update", {})
        obj._create_planner()
        if hasattr(obj.planner, "load_state_dict"):
            obj.planner.load_state_dict(state["planner"])
        else:
            obj.planner.rng.bit_generator.state = state["planner"]["rng"]
        return obj

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path):
        try:
            return cls.from_state_dict(load_state(path))
        except CheckpointFormatError as error:
            if str(error) != "not a v2 state archive":
                raise
        # Explicit migration of the published v1 runtime format.
        with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as arrays:
            if "metadata" not in arrays.files:
                raise ValueError("unknown checkpoint format")
            meta = json.loads(str(arrays["metadata"]))
            if meta.get("version") != 1:
                raise ValueError("unsupported legacy version")
            obj = cls(RuntimeConfig(**meta["config"]))
            state = obj.state_dict()
            for name, module in [("world", obj.world), ("policy", obj.policy)]:
                state[name] = {
                    k: arrays[f"{name}.{k}"].copy() for k in module.state_dict()
                }
            for name in ("W_base", "dW", "U", "singular_values", "last_update"):
                state["memory"][name] = arrays[f"continual.{name}"].copy()
            for name in ("z", "prediction", "rest", "last_action"):
                state[name] = arrays[name].copy()
            state.update(
                cascade=arrays["cascade"].copy(),
                working=arrays["working"].copy(),
                working_cursor=meta["working_cursor"],
                tick=meta["tick"],
                rng=meta["rng"],
                mod=meta["mod"],
                internal=meta["internal"],
                episodes=[
                    {**e, "embedding": np.asarray(e["embedding"])}
                    for e in meta["episodes"]
                ],
                facts=meta["facts"],
                sensory=[np.asarray(x) for x in meta["sensory"]],
                previous_observation=None
                if meta["previous_observation"] is None
                else np.asarray(meta["previous_observation"]),
                episodic_threshold=meta["episodic_threshold"],
                metrics=meta["metrics"],
                external_state=meta.get("external_state", {}),
            )
            state["planner"] = {"rng": meta["planner_rng"]}
            return cls.from_state_dict(state)

    def write_metrics(self, path):
        Path(path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        Path(path).write_text(
            "".join(json.dumps(m, allow_nan=False) + "\n" for m in self.metrics)
        )

"""Candidate discovery and scoped verification; empirical fit is not a theorem."""


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/dataset_synthesizer.py
========================================================================================
"""Append-only scientific rows with explicit evidence classes and provenance."""

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import os
import re
from .polynomial import Polynomial
from .verification import PolynomialVerifier
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, strict_json


class DatasetSynthesizer:
    """Generated observations are not upgraded to facts by a remote signature.

    Optional formal claims are locally rechecked using the exact polynomial
    checker. A proof covers the encoded model identity, not every numeric row.
    """

    def __init__(self, directory, *, max_rows=100000, max_row_bytes=262144):
        self.directory = Path(directory)
        self.directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.path = self.directory / "observations.jsonl"
        self.max_rows = max_rows
        self.max_row_bytes = max_row_bytes
        self.count = 0
        self.head = "0" * 64
        if self.path.exists():
            with self.path.open("rb") as stream:
                for line in stream:
                    if len(line) > max_row_bytes:
                        raise ValueError("existing row exceeds budget")
                    row = strict_json(line, max_row_bytes)
                    digest = row.pop("row_sha256")
                    if (
                        row["previous"] != self.head
                        or hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(row)).hexdigest() != digest
                    ):
                        raise ValueError("dataset chain corrupted")
                    self.head = digest
                    self.count += 1
            if self.count > max_rows:
                raise ValueError("dataset row budget exceeded")

    def append(self, observation, *, seed_digest, units, claim=None):
        if self.count >= self.max_rows:
            raise ValueError("dataset row budget exceeded")
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", seed_digest):
            raise ValueError("source seed artifact digest required")
        verification = None
        label = "simulation_observation"
        if claim:
            variables = claim["variables"]
            lhs = Polynomial.parse(claim["lhs"], variables)
            rhs = Polynomial.parse(claim["rhs"], variables)
            verification = (
                PolynomialVerifier()
                .identity(lhs, rhs, model=claim.get("model"))
                .to_dict()
            )
            label = (
                "observation_with_model_identity"
                if verification["status"] == "proved"
                else "observation_with_unproved_candidate"
            )
        row = {
            "schema": "NOVA-DATASET-ROW-v1",
            "index": self.count,
            "previous": self.head,
            "seed_digest": seed_digest,
            "observation": observation,
            "units": dict(units),
            "evidence_class": label,
            "verification": verification,
        }
        raw = canonical_json(row)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        row["row_sha256"] = digest
        encoded = canonical_json(row) + b"\n"
        if len(encoded) > self.max_row_bytes:
            raise ValueError("scientific row exceeds budget")
        with self.path.open("ab") as stream:
            stream.write(encoded)
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        self.head = digest
        self.count += 1
        return digest

    def manifest(self):
        result = {
            "schema": "NOVA-DATASET-v1",
            "rows": self.count,
            "head": self.head,
            "file": "observations.jsonl",
            "evidence_policy": "simulation observations and scoped model identities; no universal real-world truth guarantee",
        }
        (self.directory / "manifest.json").write_bytes(canonical_json(result))
        return result


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/polynomial.py
========================================================================================
"""Bounded exact rational polynomials parsed from an arithmetic-only AST."""

from __future__ import annotations
import ast
from fractions import Fraction
import re
import numpy as np


class Polynomial:
    max_terms = 512
    max_degree = 8

    def __init__(self, variables, terms=None):
        self.variables = tuple(variables)
        if (
            not 1 <= len(self.variables) <= 16
            or len(set(self.variables)) != len(self.variables)
            or any(
                not re.fullmatch(r"[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]{0,31}", v)
                for v in self.variables
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid polynomial variables")
        self.terms = {tuple(m): Fraction(c) for m, c in (terms or {}).items() if c}
        if len(self.terms) > self.max_terms:
            raise ValueError("polynomial term budget exceeded")
        for m, c in self.terms.items():
            if (
                len(m) != len(self.variables)
                or any(not isinstance(p, int) or p < 0 for p in m)
                or sum(m) > self.max_degree
                or max(abs(c.numerator).bit_length(), c.denominator.bit_length()) > 512
            ):
                raise ValueError("polynomial degree/coefficient budget exceeded")

    def constant(self, value):
        return Polynomial(self.variables, {(0,) * len(self.variables): Fraction(value)})

    def _coerce(self, other):
        if not isinstance(other, Polynomial):
            other = self.constant(other)
        if other.variables != self.variables:
            raise ValueError("variable context mismatch")
        return other

    def __add__(self, other):
        other = self._coerce(other)
        terms = self.terms.copy()
        for m, c in other.terms.items():
            terms[m] = terms.get(m, Fraction(0)) + c
        return Polynomial(self.variables, terms)

    def __neg__(self):
        return Polynomial(self.variables, {m: -c for m, c in self.terms.items()})

    def __sub__(self, other):
        return self + -self._coerce(other)

    def __mul__(self, other):
        other = self._coerce(other)
        terms = {}
        if len(self.terms) * len(other.terms) > 65536:
            raise ValueError("polynomial expansion budget exceeded")
        for a, c in self.terms.items():
            for b, d in other.terms.items():
                m = tuple(x + y for x, y in zip(a, b))
                terms[m] = terms.get(m, Fraction(0)) + c * d
        return Polynomial(self.variables, terms)

    def derivative(self, index):
        result = {}
        for m, c in self.terms.items():
            if m[index]:
                n = list(m)
                n[index] -= 1
                result[tuple(n)] = c * m[index]
        return Polynomial(self.variables, result)

    def evaluate(self, x):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
        if x.shape[-1] != len(self.variables):
            raise ValueError("polynomial input dimension mismatch")
        out = np.zeros(x.shape[:-1])
        for powers, c in self.terms.items():
            out += float(c) * np.prod(x ** np.asarray(powers), axis=-1)
        return out

    def expression(self):
        if not self.terms:
            return "0"
        terms = []
        for powers, c in sorted(self.terms.items()):
            factors = [f"({c.numerator}/{c.denominator})"]
            factors.extend(
                name if power == 1 else f"({name}**{power})"
                for name, power in zip(self.variables, powers)
                if power
            )
            terms.append("*".join(factors))
        return "(" + "+".join(terms) + ")"

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "variables": list(self.variables),
            "terms": [[list(m), str(c)] for m, c in sorted(self.terms.items())],
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data):
        return cls(data["variables"], {tuple(m): Fraction(c) for m, c in data["terms"]})

    @classmethod
    def parse(cls, expression, variables):
        if not isinstance(expression, str) or len(expression) > 16384:
            raise ValueError("expression too large")
        tree = ast.parse(expression, mode="eval")
        if len(list(ast.walk(tree))) > 2048:
            raise ValueError("expression tree budget exceeded")
        base = cls(variables)
        names = {
            name: cls(variables, {tuple(int(i == j) for i in range(len(variables))): 1})
            for j, name in enumerate(variables)
        }

        def visit(node):
            if isinstance(node, ast.Name) and node.id in names:
                return names[node.id]
            if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and type(node.value) in (int, float):
                return base.constant(Fraction(str(node.value)))
            if isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp) and isinstance(
                node.op, (ast.USub, ast.UAdd)
            ):
                value = visit(node.operand)
                return -value if isinstance(node.op, ast.USub) else value
            if isinstance(node, ast.BinOp):
                left = visit(node.left)
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Pow):
                    if (
                        not isinstance(node.right, ast.Constant)
                        or type(node.right.value) != int
                        or not 0 <= node.right.value <= cls.max_degree
                    ):
                        raise ValueError("invalid exponent")
                    value = base.constant(1)
                    for _ in range(node.right.value):
                        value = value * left
                    return value
                right = visit(node.right)
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Add):
                    return left + right
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Sub):
                    return left - right
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Mult):
                    return left * right
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Div):
                    zero = (0,) * len(variables)
                    if set(right.terms) != {zero}:
                        raise ValueError("division requires nonzero rational constant")
                    return left * base.constant(1 / right.terms[zero])
            raise ValueError("only bounded polynomial arithmetic is supported")

        return visit(tree.body)


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/symbolic_regression.py
========================================================================================
"""Sparse polynomial discovery with held-out groups and rationalized coefficients."""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from fractions import Fraction
from itertools import combinations_with_replacement
import hashlib
import numpy as np
from .polynomial import Polynomial


@dataclass
class SymbolicCandidate:
    polynomial: Polynomial
    metrics: dict
    provenance: dict

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "polynomial": self.polynomial.to_dict(),
            "expression": self.polynomial.expression(),
            "metrics": self.metrics,
            "provenance": self.provenance,
            "status": "empirical_candidate",
        }


class SymbolicRegressor:
    """Greedy sparse least squares over a declared polynomial library.

    Train/holdout splitting is by trajectory groups when supplied, otherwise by
    ordered blocks. A formula fitting a latent model is not automatically a law
    in physical coordinates. Rationalization is checked on held-out data.
    """

    def __init__(
        self, degree=3, max_terms=8, max_features=256, complexity_penalty=1e-5
    ):
        if (
            not 1 <= degree <= 5
            or not 1 <= max_terms <= 32
            or not 1 <= max_features <= 512
        ):
            raise ValueError("symbolic search budget exceeded")
        self.degree, self.max_terms, self.max_features, self.penalty = (
            degree,
            max_terms,
            max_features,
            complexity_penalty,
        )

    def fit(self, x, y, variables, groups=None, provenance=None):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
        if (
            x.ndim != 2
            or x.shape[1] != len(variables)
            or not 1 <= x.shape[1] <= 8
            or y.shape != (len(x),)
            or len(x) < 12
            or not np.isfinite(x).all()
            or not np.isfinite(y).all()
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid symbolic observations")
        if groups is None:
            train = np.arange(len(x)) < int(0.75 * len(x))
            split = "ordered_block"
        else:
            groups = np.asarray(groups)
            unique = list(dict.fromkeys(groups.tolist()))
            if len(unique) < 2:
                raise ValueError("held-out trajectory groups required")
            train = np.isin(groups, unique[: max(1, int(0.75 * len(unique)))])
            split = "trajectory_groups"
        test = ~train
        if train.sum() < 4 or test.sum() < 2:
            raise ValueError("insufficient held-out observations")
        powers = [(0,) * x.shape[1]]
        for degree in range(1, self.degree + 1):
            for indices in combinations_with_replacement(range(x.shape[1]), degree):
                p = [0] * x.shape[1]
                for i in indices:
                    p[i] += 1
                powers.append(tuple(p))
                if len(powers) > self.max_features:
                    raise ValueError(
                        "feature library exceeds budget; lower degree/dimension"
                    )
        design = np.stack([np.prod(x ** np.asarray(p), axis=1) for p in powers], axis=1)
        if not np.isfinite(design).all():
            raise ValueError("overflow in symbolic library")
        selected = []
        residual = y[train].copy()
        scale = np.maximum(np.linalg.norm(design[train], axis=0), 1e-12)
        available = set(range(len(powers)))
        best = float(np.mean(residual**2))
        for _ in range(min(self.max_terms, len(powers))):
            index = max(
                sorted(available),
                key=lambda i: abs(float(design[train, i] @ residual)) / scale[i],
            )
            trial = selected + [index]
            coef = np.linalg.lstsq(design[train][:, trial], y[train], rcond=1e-10)[0]
            error = y[train] - design[train][:, trial] @ coef
            objective = float(np.mean(error**2)) + self.penalty * len(trial)
            if selected and objective >= best:
                break
            selected = trial
            available.remove(index)
            residual = error
            best = objective
        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(design[train][:, selected], y[train], rcond=1e-10)[0]
        polynomial = Polynomial(
            variables,
            {
                powers[i]: Fraction(float(c)).limit_denominator(10000)
                for i, c in zip(selected, coef)
            },
        )
        prediction = polynomial.evaluate(x)
        baseline = float(np.mean((y[test] - y[train].mean()) ** 2))
        condition = float(np.linalg.cond(design[train][:, selected]))
        condition = condition if np.isfinite(condition) else None
        return SymbolicCandidate(
            polynomial,
            {
                "train_mse": float(np.mean((prediction[train] - y[train]) ** 2)),
                "heldout_mse": float(np.mean((prediction[test] - y[test]) ** 2)),
                "mean_baseline_mse": baseline,
                "terms": len(polynomial.terms),
                "train_samples": int(train.sum()),
                "heldout_samples": int(test.sum()),
                "split": split,
                "design_condition": condition,
            },
            {
                **(provenance or {}),
                "feature_variables": list(variables),
                "data_sha256": hashlib.sha256(x.tobytes() + y.tobytes()).hexdigest(),
            },
        )

    def distill_latent(self, world, z, actions, coordinates, dt=1.0):
        z = np.asarray(z)
        actions = np.asarray(actions)
        if dt <= 0 or len(coordinates) + actions.shape[1] > 8:
            raise ValueError("explicit low-dimensional observable selection required")
        future = world.predict(z, actions)
        x = np.c_[z[:, coordinates], actions]
        names = [f"z{i}" for i in coordinates] + [
            f"a{i}" for i in range(actions.shape[1])
        ]
        return [
            self.fit(
                x,
                (future[:, index] - z[:, index]) / dt,
                names,
                provenance={
                    "scope": "selected_latent_coordinates",
                    "coordinate": index,
                    "dt": dt,
                    "warning": "unobserved latent coordinates may invalidate closure; not a physical-coordinate theorem",
                },
            )
            for index in coordinates
        ]

ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/verification.py

"""Exact model-relative proofs and optional SMT; no truth-by-checksum shortcut."""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import hashlib
from .polynomial import Polynomial
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VerificationReceipt:
    status: str
    backend: str
    claim_sha256: str
    model_sha256: str
    assumptions: tuple[str, ...]
    details: dict

    def to_dict(self):
        return asdict(self)


def receipt(status, backend, claim, model=None, assumptions=(), details=None):
    return VerificationReceipt(
        status,
        backend,
        hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(claim)).hexdigest(),
        hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(model or {})).hexdigest(),
        tuple(assumptions),
        details or {},
    )


class PolynomialVerifier:
    def identity(self, lhs: Polynomial, rhs: Polynomial, *, model=None):
        difference = lhs - rhs
        claim = {
            "kind": "polynomial_identity",
            "lhs": lhs.to_dict(),
            "rhs": rhs.to_dict(),
        }
        return receipt(
            "proved" if not difference.terms else "refuted",
            "exact-rational-normalization-v1",
            claim,
            model,
            ("over rational/real variables in the encoded polynomial model",),
            {"residual": difference.to_dict()},
        )

    def invariant(self, invariant: Polynomial, vector_field: list[Polynomial]):
        if len(vector_field) != len(invariant.variables):
            raise ValueError("vector field dimension mismatch")
        derivative = invariant.constant(0)
        for i, component in enumerate(vector_field):
            derivative = derivative + invariant.derivative(i) * component
        model = {
            "variables": list(invariant.variables),
            "vector_field": [p.to_dict() for p in vector_field],
        }
        claim = {"kind": "continuous_ode_invariant", "invariant": invariant.to_dict()}
        return receipt(
            "proved" if not derivative.terms else "refuted",
            "exact-rational-lie-derivative-v1",
            claim,
            model,
            (
                "continuous polynomial ODE exactly as encoded; no claim about floating integrator or real world",
            ),
            {"lie_derivative": derivative.to_dict()},
        )


class Z3Verifier:
    def __init__(self, timeout_ms=5000):
        if not 1 <= timeout_ms <= 30000:
            raise ValueError("SMT timeout out of range")
        self.timeout = timeout_ms

    def identity(self, lhs: Polynomial, rhs: Polynomial, assumptions=()):
        if lhs.variables != rhs.variables or any(
            p.variables != lhs.variables for p in assumptions
        ):
            raise ValueError("variable context mismatch")
        claim = {
            "kind": "polynomial_identity",
            "lhs": lhs.to_dict(),
            "rhs": rhs.to_dict(),
        }
        model = {"domain": [p.to_dict() for p in assumptions]}
        try:
            import z3
        except ImportError:
            return receipt(
                "unavailable",
                "z3",
                claim,
                model,
                details={"reason": "install nova-research[science]"},
            )
        variables = {name: z3.Real(name) for name in lhs.variables}

        def encode(p):
            value = z3.RealVal(0)
            for powers, c in p.terms.items():
                term = z3.RealVal(f"{c.numerator}/{c.denominator}")
                for name, power in zip(p.variables, powers):
                    term *= variables[name] ** power
                value += term
            return value

        solver = z3.Solver()
        solver.set(timeout=self.timeout)
        for assumption in assumptions:
            solver.add(encode(assumption) >= 0)
        # Distinguish an empty assumption domain from a nonvacuous theorem.
        domain = solver.check()
        if domain != z3.sat:
            return receipt(
                "unknown" if domain == z3.unknown else "vacuous",
                "z3",
                claim,
                model,
                details={"domain": str(domain)},
            )
        solver.add(encode(lhs) != encode(rhs))
        result = solver.check()
        details = {"solver_version": z3.get_version_string(), "result": str(result)}
        if result == z3.sat:
            details["counterexample"] = {
                name: str(solver.model().eval(v, model_completion=True))
                for name, v in variables.items()
            }
        return receipt(
            "proved"
            if result == z3.unsat
            else "refuted"
            if result == z3.sat
            else "unknown",
            "z3",
            claim,
            model,
            ("only within the encoded nonempty real-polynomial domain",),
            details,
        )


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/verifier_lean.py
========================================================================================
"""Lean adapter for internally generated polynomial identities, never peer code."""

from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
from .polynomial import Polynomial
from .verification import receipt


class LeanVerifier:
    """Requires a user-provisioned Lean4 + Mathlib project. No network downloads.

    Only bounded expressions regenerated from Polynomial objects are compiled.
    Remote .lean files, tactics, #eval and arbitrary imports are not accepted.
    """

    def __init__(self, project, timeout=30):
        self.project = Path(project).resolve()
        self.timeout = timeout
        if not 1 <= timeout <= 120:
            raise ValueError("Lean timeout out of range")

    @staticmethod
    def _expression(p):
        pieces = []
        for powers, c in sorted(p.terms.items()):
            factors = [f"(({c.numerator} : ℝ) / {c.denominator})"]
            factors.extend(
                f"({name} ^ {power})"
                for name, power in zip(p.variables, powers)
                if power
            )
            pieces.append(" * ".join(factors))
        return " + ".join(pieces) or "(0 : ℝ)"

    def identity(self, lhs: Polynomial, rhs: Polynomial):
        if lhs.variables != rhs.variables:
            raise ValueError("variable context mismatch")
        claim = {
            "kind": "polynomial_identity",
            "lhs": lhs.to_dict(),
            "rhs": rhs.to_dict(),
        }
        if not self.project.is_dir() or not shutil.which("lake"):
            return receipt(
                "unavailable",
                "lean4-mathlib",
                claim,
                details={"reason": "local lake/Mathlib project required"},
            )
        names = " ".join(lhs.variables)
        source = f"import Mathlib\nset_option maxHeartbeats 200000\nexample ({names} : ℝ) : {self._expression(lhs)} = {self._expression(rhs)} := by\n  ring\n"
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
            prefix=".nova-proof-", dir=self.project
        ) as directory:
            path = Path(directory) / "Claim.lean"
            path.write_text(source)
            try:
                result = subprocess.run(
                    ["lake", "env", "lean", str(path)],
                    cwd=self.project,
                    capture_output=True,
                    text=True,
                    timeout=self.timeout,
                    check=False,
                )
                version = subprocess.run(
                    ["lake", "env", "lean", "--version"],
                    cwd=self.project,
                    capture_output=True,
                    text=True,
                    timeout=10,
                    check=False,
                )
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                return receipt(
                    "unknown", "lean4-mathlib", claim, details={"reason": "timeout"}
                )
        return receipt(
            "proved" if result.returncode == 0 else "not_proved",
            "lean4-mathlib",
            claim,
            assumptions=(
                "generated real-polynomial identity, Mathlib ring tactic and local toolchain",
            ),
            details={
                "returncode": result.returncode,
                "lean_version": version.stdout.strip()[:512],
                "diagnostics": (result.stdout + result.stderr)[-8192:],
            },
        )

"""Content-addressed blobs, Ed25519 identities and a locally append-only ledger."""

from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import base64
import hashlib
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sqlite3
import tempfile
from cryptography.exceptions import InvalidSignature
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import (
    Ed25519PrivateKey,
    Ed25519PublicKey,
)
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import serialization
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, strict_json


HEX = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")


def atomic(path, data):
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fd, name = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=path.parent, prefix=".nova-artifact-")
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as stream:
            stream.write(data)
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        os.replace(name, path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(name):
            os.unlink(name)


class SigningIdentity:
    def __init__(self, key):
        self.key = key

    @classmethod
    def load_or_create(cls, path):
        path = Path(path)
        if path.exists():
            return cls(Ed25519PrivateKey.from_private_bytes(path.read_bytes()))
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        key = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()
        fd = os.open(path, os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as stream:
            stream.write(
                key.private_bytes(
                    serialization.Encoding.Raw,
                    serialization.PrivateFormat.Raw,
                    serialization.NoEncryption(),
                )
            )
        return cls(key)

    @property
    def public_key(self):
        return self.key.public_key().public_bytes(
            serialization.Encoding.Raw, serialization.PublicFormat.Raw
        )

    @property
    def peer_id(self):
        return hashlib.sha256(self.public_key).hexdigest()

    def sign(self, payload):
        raw = canonical_json(payload)
        return {
            "payload": payload,
            "public_key": base64.b64encode(self.public_key).decode(),
            "signature": base64.b64encode(self.key.sign(raw)).decode(),
        }

    @staticmethod
    def verify(envelope, expected_peer=None):
        key = base64.b64decode(envelope["public_key"], validate=True)
        signature = base64.b64decode(envelope["signature"], validate=True)
        peer = hashlib.sha256(key).hexdigest()
        if expected_peer is not None and peer != expected_peer:
            raise ValueError("signer identity mismatch")
        try:
            Ed25519PublicKey.from_public_bytes(key).verify(
                signature, canonical_json(envelope["payload"])
            )
        except InvalidSignature as error:
            raise ValueError("invalid envelope signature") from error
        return peer, envelope["payload"]


class ArtifactStore:
    def __init__(
        self, directory, max_bytes=8 * 1024 * 1024, max_total_bytes=256 * 1024 * 1024
    ):
        self.directory = Path(directory)
        self.directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.max_bytes = max_bytes
        self.max_total = max_total_bytes

    def put(self, data: bytes):
        if len(data) > self.max_bytes:
            raise ValueError("artifact exceeds size budget")
        digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        path = self.directory / digest
        if not path.exists():
            total = sum(
                p.stat().st_size
                for p in self.directory.iterdir()
                if p.is_file() and HEX.fullmatch(p.name)
            )
            if total + len(data) > self.max_total:
                raise ValueError("artifact store quota exceeded")
            atomic(path, data)
        return digest

    def get(self, digest):
        if not HEX.fullmatch(digest):
            raise ValueError("invalid content address")
        path = self.directory / digest
        if path.stat().st_size > self.max_bytes:
            raise ValueError("stored artifact exceeds size budget")
        data = path.read_bytes()
        if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != digest:
            raise ValueError("artifact integrity mismatch")
        return data

    def put_json(self, data):
        return self.put(canonical_json(data))

    def get_json(self, digest):
        return strict_json(self.get(digest), self.max_bytes)

    def inventory(self, limit=128):
        return sorted(
            p.name
            for p in self.directory.iterdir()
            if p.is_file() and HEX.fullmatch(p.name)
        )[:limit]


class ScientificLedger:
    """Signed local hash chain. A trusted external head is needed to detect rollback.

    This is not a global blockchain, Byzantine consensus, or immutable storage.
    Foreign discovery receipts are claims until locally reverified.
    """

    def __init__(self, path, identity: SigningIdentity):
        self.identity = identity
        Path(path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.db = sqlite3.connect(path, timeout=10)
        self.db.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        self.db.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS entries (sequence INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, digest TEXT UNIQUE, envelope BLOB NOT NULL)"
        )
        self.db.commit()

    def append(self, kind, body):
        if kind not in (
            "discovery",
            "lineage",
            "dataset",
            "peer_claim",
            "verification",
            "reproduction",
        ):
            raise ValueError("unknown ledger entry kind")
        if len(canonical_json(body)) > 262144:
            raise ValueError("ledger entry exceeds budget")
        try:
            self.db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            last = self.db.execute(
                "SELECT sequence,digest FROM entries ORDER BY sequence DESC LIMIT 1"
            ).fetchone()
            payload = {
                "protocol": "NOVA-LEDGER-v1",
                "sequence": 0 if last is None else last[0] + 1,
                "previous": "0" * 64 if last is None else last[1],
                "origin": self.identity.peer_id,
                "kind": kind,
                "body": body,
                "recorded_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            }
            envelope = self.identity.sign(payload)
            raw = canonical_json(envelope)
            digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
            self.db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO entries VALUES (?,?,?)", (payload["sequence"], digest, raw)
            )
            self.db.commit()
            return digest
        except BaseException:
            self.db.rollback()
            raise

    def verify(self, expected_head=None):
        previous = "0" * 64
        count = 0
        for sequence, digest, raw in self.db.execute(
            "SELECT sequence,digest,envelope FROM entries ORDER BY sequence"
        ):
            if len(raw) > 524288:
                raise ValueError("ledger entry size exceeded")
            _, payload = SigningIdentity.verify(
                strict_json(raw, 524288), self.identity.peer_id
            )
            if (
                sequence != count
                or payload["sequence"] != sequence
                or payload["previous"] != previous
                or hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != digest
            ):
                raise ValueError("ledger chain mismatch")
            previous = digest
            count += 1
        if expected_head is not None and expected_head != previous:
            raise ValueError("ledger rollback/head mismatch")
        return {"entries": count, "head": previous, "scope": "local signed chain only"}

    def close(self):
        self.db.close()
