ARQUIVO: nova/swarm/holoseed.py
========================================================================================
"""Replay manifests bind seeds to algorithms, parameters, genomes and event logs."""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import asdict
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from nova import __version__
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, environment_fingerprint
from nova.bioma.physics_engine import PhysicsConfig
from nova.bioma.ecosystem import Ecosystem, EcosystemConfig
from .artifacts import SigningIdentity


def engine_digest():
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    for file in sorted(root.rglob("*.py")):
        digest.update(file.relative_to(root).as_posix().encode())
        digest.update(file.read_bytes())
    return digest.hexdigest()


class HoloSeed:
    """A full bounded manifest, not a 64-byte encoding of arbitrary state.

    A SHA-256 address is 32 bytes; it locates referenced content but cannot
    reconstruct that content without the matching artifacts and engine.
    Float64 does not imply portable bit-identical execution.
    """

    protocol = "NOVA-HOLOSEED-v1"

    @classmethod
    def pack(
        cls,
        ecosystem: Ecosystem,
        *,
        initial_genomes=None,
        tier="same_environment",
        identity=None,
    ):
        if tier not in ("strict_integer", "same_environment"):
            raise ValueError("unsupported replay tier")
        world = ecosystem.world
        if tier == "strict_integer" and (
            world.config.mode not in ("conway", "fixed_gray_scott")
            or ecosystem.config.initial_population != 0
            or ecosystem.organisms
            or ecosystem.events
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "strict integer replay requires a field-only integer mode and no external events"
            )
        if initial_genomes is not None:
            raise ValueError(
                "set custom genomes at Ecosystem genesis, not after simulation"
            )
        expected = (
            world.state_hash() if tier == "strict_integer" else ecosystem.state_hash()
        )
        payload = {
            "protocol": cls.protocol,
            "engine_version": __version__,
            "engine_sha256": engine_digest(),
            "physics": asdict(world.config),
            "ecosystem": asdict(ecosystem.config),
            "seed": world.config.seed,
            "ticks": world.tick,
            "events": ecosystem.events,
            "reproducibility": tier,
            "environment": environment_fingerprint(),
            "expected_state_sha256": expected,
            "genesis": "seeded_engine_v1",
            "initial_genomes": ecosystem.genesis_genomes,
            "note": "Digest equality verifies this encoded replay, not a scientific theorem.",
        }
        if len(canonical_json(payload)) > 260000:
            raise ValueError(
                "genesis manifest exceeds packet budget; use a checkpoint artifact"
            )
        return (
            identity.sign(payload)
            if identity
            else {"payload": payload, "unsigned": True}
        )

    @classmethod
    def validate(
        cls,
        envelope,
        *,
        max_ticks=10000,
        max_cells=262144,
        max_population=32,
        require_signature=False,
        max_cell_updates=67108864,
        max_agent_ticks=32768,
    ):
        if len(canonical_json(envelope)) > 262144:
            raise ValueError("manifest exceeds packet budget")
        if require_signature or not envelope.get("unsigned"):
            SigningIdentity.verify(envelope)
        payload = envelope["payload"]
        if (
            payload.get("protocol") != cls.protocol
            or payload.get("genesis") != "seeded_engine_v1"
        ):
            raise ValueError("unknown seed protocol/genesis")
        if (
            not isinstance(payload["ticks"], int)
            or not 0 <= payload["ticks"] <= max_ticks
        ):
            raise ValueError("replay tick budget exceeded")
        physics = payload["physics"].copy()
        physics["shape"] = tuple(physics["shape"])
        physics["birth"] = tuple(physics["birth"])
        physics["survive"] = tuple(physics["survive"])
        config = PhysicsConfig(**physics)
        eco = EcosystemConfig(**payload["ecosystem"])
        if (
            int(np.prod(config.shape)) > max_cells
            or eco.max_population > max_population
            or len(payload["events"]) > 10000
            or int(np.prod(config.shape)) * payload["ticks"] > max_cell_updates
            or eco.max_population * payload["ticks"] > max_agent_ticks
        ):
            raise ValueError("replay resource budget exceeded")
        if payload["seed"] != config.seed:
            raise ValueError("seed mismatch")
        if payload["reproducibility"] == "strict_integer" and (
            config.mode not in ("conway", "fixed_gray_scott")
            or eco.initial_population
            or payload["events"]
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid strict-integer replay declaration")
        if payload["reproducibility"] not in ("strict_integer", "same_environment"):
            raise ValueError("unknown reproducibility tier")
        genomes = payload.get("initial_genomes")
        if genomes is not None:
            from nova.bioma.genetics import MorphogeneticCPPN

            if not isinstance(genomes, list) or len(genomes) != eco.initial_population:
                raise ValueError("genesis genome population mismatch")
            for genome in genomes:
                MorphogeneticCPPN.from_state_dict(genome)
        return payload, config, eco

    @classmethod
    def reproduce(cls, envelope, **budgets):
        payload, physics, config = cls.validate(envelope, **budgets)
        if payload["engine_sha256"] != engine_digest():
            raise ValueError("engine digest differs; replay is not claimed equivalent")
        if payload["engine_version"] != __version__:
            raise ValueError("engine version mismatch")
        if (
            payload["reproducibility"] == "same_environment"
            and payload["environment"] != environment_fingerprint()
        ):
            raise ValueError("floating environment fingerprint mismatch")
        ecosystem = Ecosystem(
            physics, config, genesis_genomes=payload.get("initial_genomes")
        )
        events = {}
        for event in payload["events"]:
            events.setdefault(event["tick"], []).append(event)
        if any(
            not isinstance(t, int) or not 0 <= t <= payload["ticks"] for t in events
        ):
            raise ValueError("event log clock out of range")
        for tick in range(payload["ticks"]):
            for event in events.get(tick, []):
                ecosystem.apply_event(event)
            ecosystem.step()
        for event in events.get(payload["ticks"], []):
            ecosystem.apply_event(event)
        digest = (
            ecosystem.world.state_hash()
            if payload["reproducibility"] == "strict_integer"
            else ecosystem.state_hash()
        )
        return ecosystem, {
            "status": "matched"
            if digest == payload["expected_state_sha256"]
            else "mismatch",
            "expected": payload["expected_state_sha256"],
            "observed": digest,
            "tier": payload["reproducibility"],
            "scientific_truth_proved": False,
        }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/swarm/p2p_node.py
========================================================================================
"""Opt-in pinned-peer RPC and XOR routing over framed TLS or WebSockets.

No unauthenticated peer code, automatic simulation, dynamic imports, arbitrary
URLs, or received proof scripts are executed. Received claims remain unverified.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import asyncio
import base64
from collections import OrderedDict
from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib
import ssl
import struct
import time
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, strict_json
from .artifacts import ArtifactStore, SigningIdentity, HEX


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Peer:
    peer_id: str
    endpoint: str

    def __post_init__(self):
        u = urlsplit(self.endpoint)
        if (
            not HEX.fullmatch(self.peer_id)
            or u.scheme not in ("tls", "tcp", "wss", "ws")
            or not u.hostname
            or not u.port
            or u.username
            or u.password
            or u.query
            or u.fragment
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid pinned peer endpoint")
        if u.scheme in ("tcp", "ws") and u.hostname not in (
            "127.0.0.1",
            "localhost",
            "::1",
        ):
            raise ValueError("remote peers require TLS/wss")


class RoutingTable:
    """Bounded XOR-distance routing; manually pinned peers, not a global DHT claim."""

    def __init__(self, identity, peers=(), capacity=64):
        if len(peers) > capacity:
            raise ValueError("routing budget exceeded")
        self.identity = identity
        self.peers = {p.peer_id: p for p in peers}
        self.capacity = capacity

    def closest(self, key, limit=8):
        if not HEX.fullmatch(key):
            raise ValueError("invalid routing key")
        return sorted(
            self.peers.values(), key=lambda p: int(p.peer_id, 16) ^ int(key, 16)
        )[:limit]


class PeerNode:
    max_frame = 1024 * 1024

    def __init__(
        self,
        identity: SigningIdentity,
        store: ArtifactStore,
        peers=(),
        *,
        max_connections=8,
        clock_skew=120,
    ):
        self.identity, self.store = identity, store
        self.routing = RoutingTable(identity.peer_id, peers)
        self.allowed = set(self.routing.peers) | {identity.peer_id}
        self.semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(max_connections)
        self.seen = OrderedDict()
        self.rates = {}
        self.clock_skew = clock_skew
        self.received = []
        self.sequence = 0
        self.consensus = {}
        self.subspaces = {}

    def register_consensus(self, gossip):
        """Opt in to a shared, locally declared model coordinate system."""
        self.consensus[gossip.model_id] = gossip

    def register_subspace(self, coordinate_id, basis, rank_budget):
        from .subspace_exchange import package_subspace

        packet = package_subspace(basis, coordinate_id)
        self.subspaces[coordinate_id] = {"packet": packet, "rank_budget": rank_budget}

    def message(self, method, body):
        if method not in (
            "inventory",
            "get",
            "put",
            "find",
            "announce",
            "belief",
            "subspace",
        ):
            raise ValueError("unsupported RPC method")
        self.sequence += 1
        payload = {
            "protocol": "NOVA-SWARM-v1",
            "origin": self.identity.peer_id,
            "method": method,
            "body": body,
            "time": int(time.time()),
            "nonce": hashlib.sha256(
                self.identity.public_key
                + struct.pack(">QQ", time.time_ns(), self.sequence)
            ).hexdigest(),
        }
        return self.identity.sign(payload)

    def validate(self, envelope):
        peer, payload = SigningIdentity.verify(envelope)
        if (
            peer not in self.allowed
            or payload.get("origin") != peer
            or payload.get("protocol") != "NOVA-SWARM-v1"
        ):
            raise ValueError("peer is not admitted")
        now = time.time()
        if abs(now - payload["time"]) > self.clock_skew or not HEX.fullmatch(
            payload["nonce"]
        ):
            raise ValueError("expired/invalid envelope")
        key = (peer, payload["nonce"])
        if key in self.seen:
            raise ValueError("replayed envelope")
        bucket = [t for t in self.rates.get(peer, []) if now - t < 60]
        if len(bucket) >= 120:
            raise ValueError("peer rate budget exceeded")
        bucket.append(now)
        self.rates[peer] = bucket
        self.seen[key] = now
        while len(self.seen) > 4096:
            self.seen.popitem(last=False)
        return peer, payload

    async def handle(self, raw):
        if isinstance(raw, str):
            raw = raw.encode()
        peer, payload = self.validate(strict_json(raw, self.max_frame))
        method = payload["method"]
        body = payload["body"]
        if method == "inventory":
            result = {"hashes": self.store.inventory()}
        elif method == "get":
            data = self.store.get(body["digest"])
            if len(data) > 512 * 1024:
                raise ValueError("network object transfer budget exceeded")
            result = {"digest": body["digest"], "data": base64.b64encode(data).decode()}
        elif method == "put":
            if len(body["data"]) > 700000:
                raise ValueError("network object too large")
            data = base64.b64decode(body["data"], validate=True)
            if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != body["digest"]:
                raise ValueError("object digest mismatch")
            # A blob is inert content, never an imported module or runnable script.
            digest = self.store.put(data)
            result = {"stored": digest, "verification": "not_scientifically_verified"}
        elif method == "find":
            result = {
                "peers": [
                    {"peer_id": p.peer_id, "endpoint": p.endpoint}
                    for p in self.routing.closest(body["key"])
                ]
            }
        elif method == "belief":
            model = self.consensus.get(body.get("model_id"))
            if model is None:
                result = {"status": "model_not_registered", "truth_proved": False}
            else:
                accepted = model.accept(peer, body)
                result = {
                    "accepted": accepted,
                    "belief": model.step() if accepted else model.packet(),
                    "truth_proved": False,
                }
        elif method == "subspace":
            from .subspace_exchange import merge_subspaces, package_subspace
            import numpy as np

            coordinate_id = body.get("coordinate_id")
            local = self.subspaces.get(coordinate_id)
            if local is None:
                result = {"status": "coordinate_system_not_registered"}
            else:
                merged = merge_subspaces(
                    np.asarray(local["packet"]["basis"]),
                    body,
                    coordinate_id,
                    local["rank_budget"],
                )
                result = {k: v for k, v in merged.items() if k != "basis"}
                if merged["basis"] is not None:
                    local["packet"] = package_subspace(merged["basis"], coordinate_id)
                    result["packet"] = local["packet"]
        elif method == "announce":
            if not HEX.fullmatch(body["digest"]):
                raise ValueError("invalid announcement")
            self.received.append(
                {"peer": peer, "digest": body["digest"], "status": "quarantined_claim"}
            )
            self.received = self.received[-128:]
            result = {"accepted": "inventory_only", "executed": False}
        else:
            raise ValueError("unknown RPC method")
        return canonical_json(
            self.identity.sign(
                {
                    "protocol": "NOVA-SWARM-REPLY-v1",
                    "request_nonce": payload["nonce"],
                    "body": result,
                }
            )
        )

    async def _read(self, reader):
        length = struct.unpack(">I", await asyncio.wait_for(reader.readexactly(4), 10))[
            0
        ]
        if length > self.max_frame:
            raise ValueError("frame size exceeded")
        return await asyncio.wait_for(reader.readexactly(length), 10)

    async def _serve_connection(self, reader, writer):
        # Reject excess accepted connections instead of accumulating waiters.
        if self.semaphore.locked():
            writer.close()
            return
        async with self.semaphore:
            try:
                reply = await self.handle(await self._read(reader))
                writer.write(struct.pack(">I", len(reply)) + reply)
                await writer.drain()
            except (
                ValueError,
                KeyError,
                TypeError,
                OSError,
                asyncio.IncompleteReadError,
                asyncio.TimeoutError,
            ):
                pass
            finally:
                writer.close()
                await writer.wait_closed()

    async def serve(
        self,
        host="127.0.0.1",
        port=8766,
        *,
        certfile=None,
        keyfile=None,
        transport="tcp",
    ):
        local = host in ("127.0.0.1", "localhost", "::1")
        if not local and not (certfile and keyfile):
            raise ValueError("remote listening requires TLS certificate/key")
        context = None
        if certfile:
            context = ssl.SSLContext(ssl.PROTOCOL_TLS_SERVER)
            context.minimum_version = ssl.TLSVersion.TLSv1_2
            context.load_cert_chain(certfile, keyfile)
        if transport == "websocket":
            from websockets.asyncio.server import serve

            async def handler(socket):
                if self.semaphore.locked():
                    await socket.close(code=1013, reason="connection budget exceeded")
                    return
                async with self.semaphore:
                    try:
                        raw = await asyncio.wait_for(socket.recv(), 10)
                        await socket.send(await self.handle(raw))
                    except (
                        ValueError,
                        KeyError,
                        TypeError,
                        OSError,
                        asyncio.TimeoutError,
                    ):
                        await socket.close(code=1008, reason="request rejected")

            async with serve(
                handler,
                host,
                port,
                ssl=context,
                max_size=self.max_frame,
                max_queue=4,
                open_timeout=10,
            ):
                await asyncio.Future()
        elif transport == "tcp":
            server = await asyncio.start_server(
                self._serve_connection,
                host,
                port,
                ssl=context,
                limit=self.max_frame + 4,
                backlog=16,
            )
            async with server:
                await server.serve_forever()
        else:
            raise ValueError("unknown transport")

    async def request(self, peer_id, method, body):
        if peer_id not in self.routing.peers:
            raise ValueError("peer endpoint must be manually pinned")
        peer = self.routing.peers[peer_id]
        url = urlsplit(peer.endpoint)
        envelope = self.message(method, body)
        raw = canonical_json(envelope)
        if len(raw) > self.max_frame:
            raise ValueError("request frame exceeded")
        context = ssl.create_default_context() if url.scheme in ("tls", "wss") else None
        if url.scheme in ("ws", "wss"):
            from websockets.asyncio.client import connect

            async with connect(
                peer.endpoint, ssl=context, max_size=self.max_frame, open_timeout=10
            ) as socket:
                await socket.send(raw)
                reply = await asyncio.wait_for(socket.recv(), 10)
        else:
            reader, writer = await asyncio.wait_for(
                asyncio.open_connection(
                    url.hostname,
                    url.port,
                    ssl=context,
                    server_hostname=url.hostname if context else None,
                ),
                10,
            )
            try:
                writer.write(struct.pack(">I", len(raw)) + raw)
                await writer.drain()
                reply = await self._read(reader)
            finally:
                writer.close()
                await writer.wait_closed()
        _, payload = SigningIdentity.verify(strict_json(reply, self.max_frame), peer_id)
        if (
            payload.get("protocol") != "NOVA-SWARM-REPLY-v1"
            or payload.get("request_nonce") != envelope["payload"]["nonce"]
        ):
            raise ValueError("reply/request mismatch")
        return payload["body"]

    async def synchronize(self, peer_id, limit=8):
        inventory = await self.request(peer_id, "inventory", {})
        downloaded = []
        for digest in inventory["hashes"][:limit]:
            if not HEX.fullmatch(digest):
                continue
            try:
                self.store.get(digest)
                continue
            except FileNotFoundError:
                pass
            packet = await self.request(peer_id, "get", {"digest": digest})
            data = base64.b64decode(packet["data"], validate=True)
            if len(data) > 512 * 1024 or hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != digest:
                raise ValueError("received object digest/size mismatch")
            self.store.put(data)
            downloaded.append(digest)
        return {
            "downloaded": downloaded,
            "scientific_verification": "pending_local_reverification",
            "automatic_execution": False,
        }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/swarm/sheaf_consensus.py
========================================================================================
"""Local sheaf discrepancy descent; no Byzantine agreement or truth oracle."""

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np


class SheafGossip:
    def __init__(
        self, dimension, model_id, initial=None, *, max_neighbors=32, huber_delta=1.0
    ):
        if (
            not 1 <= dimension <= 128
            or not 1 <= max_neighbors <= 256
            or huber_delta <= 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("gossip size/scale budget exceeded")
        self.dimension, self.model_id, self.max_neighbors = (
            dimension,
            model_id,
            max_neighbors,
        )
        self.value = (
            np.zeros(dimension)
            if initial is None
            else np.asarray(initial, dtype=float).copy()
        )
        if self.value.shape != (dimension,) or not np.isfinite(self.value).all():
            raise ValueError("invalid belief")
        self.neighbors = {}
        self.huber_delta = huber_delta
        self.sequence = 0

    def accept(self, peer, payload, left=None, right=None):
        if payload.get("model_id") != self.model_id:
            return False
        value = np.asarray(payload["value"], dtype=float)
        sequence = payload["sequence"]
        if (
            value.shape != self.value.shape
            or not np.isfinite(value).all()
            or not isinstance(sequence, int)
            or sequence < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid belief packet")
        if peer not in self.neighbors and len(self.neighbors) >= self.max_neighbors:
            return False
        old = self.neighbors.get(peer)
        if old and sequence <= old["sequence"]:
            return False
        left = np.eye(self.dimension) if left is None else np.asarray(left, dtype=float)
        right = (
            np.eye(self.dimension) if right is None else np.asarray(right, dtype=float)
        )
        if (
            left.shape != (self.dimension, self.dimension)
            or right.shape != left.shape
            or not np.isfinite(left).all()
            or not np.isfinite(right).all()
        ):
            raise ValueError("restriction mismatch")
        self.neighbors[peer] = {
            "sequence": sequence,
            "value": value.copy(),
            "left": left,
            "right": right,
        }
        return True

    def step(self, step_size=0.1):
        if not 0 < step_size <= 1:
            raise ValueError("invalid gossip step")
        gradient = np.zeros_like(self.value)
        energy = 0.0
        bound = 0.0
        for neighbor in self.neighbors.values():
            left, right = neighbor["left"], neighbor["right"]
            difference = left @ self.value - right @ neighbor["value"]
            norm = np.linalg.norm(difference)
            scale = min(1.0, self.huber_delta / max(norm, 1e-12))
            gradient += left.T @ (difference * scale)
            energy += (
                0.5 * norm * norm
                if norm <= self.huber_delta
                else self.huber_delta * (norm - 0.5 * self.huber_delta)
            )
            bound += np.linalg.norm(left, 2) ** 2
        self.value -= min(step_size, 0.45 / max(bound, 1.0)) * gradient
        self.sequence += 1
        return {
            "model_id": self.model_id,
            "sequence": self.sequence,
            "value": self.value.tolist(),
            "discrepancy_before": float(energy),
            "scope": "local numerical consistency only; agreement is not a proof of truth",
        }

    def packet(self):
        return {
            "model_id": self.model_id,
            "sequence": self.sequence,
            "value": self.value.tolist(),
        }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/swarm/subspace_exchange.py
========================================================================================
"""Coordinate-checked exchange of protection constraints, not instant skill transfer."""

import numpy as np
from nova.bioma.determinism import digest_arrays


def coordinate_fingerprint(encoder_state, feature_schema):
    arrays = {k: np.asarray(v) for k, v in encoder_state.items()}
    return digest_arrays(arrays, {"feature_schema": feature_schema})


def package_subspace(u, coordinate_id, *, max_rank=128):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=float)
    if (
        u.ndim != 2
        or not 1 <= u.shape[0] <= 512
        or u.shape[1] > min(max_rank, u.shape[0])
        or not np.isfinite(u).all()
    ):
        raise ValueError("subspace size/value budget exceeded")
    if not np.allclose(u.T @ u, np.eye(u.shape[1]), atol=1e-9):
        raise ValueError("basis is not orthonormal")
    return {
        "protocol": "NOVA-SUBSPACE-v1",
        "coordinate_id": coordinate_id,
        "dimension": u.shape[0],
        "basis": u.tolist(),
        "meaning": "input directions to protect; does not encode target functions or skills",
    }


def merge_subspaces(local_u, packet, coordinate_id, rank_budget):
    if (
        packet.get("protocol") != "NOVA-SUBSPACE-v1"
        or packet["coordinate_id"] != coordinate_id
    ):
        raise ValueError("encoder/coordinate schema mismatch")
    remote = np.asarray(packet["basis"], dtype=float)
    local = np.asarray(local_u, dtype=float)
    package_subspace(remote, coordinate_id)
    if (
        local.ndim != 2
        or local.shape[0] != remote.shape[0]
        or not np.allclose(local.T @ local, np.eye(local.shape[1]), atol=1e-9)
    ):
        raise ValueError("local coordinate/basis mismatch")
    u, s, _ = np.linalg.svd(
        np.concatenate((local, remote), axis=1), full_matrices=False
    )
    rank = int(np.count_nonzero(s > 1e-12 * s[0])) if len(s) and s[0] else 0
    if rank > rank_budget:
        return {
            "status": "capacity_rejected",
            "required_rank": rank,
            "rank_budget": rank_budget,
            "basis": None,
        }
    return {
        "status": "merged_constraints",
        "basis": u[:, :rank],
        "rank": rank,
        "transferred_skill": False,
    }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/synapse/__init__.py
========================================================================================
"""Explicit diffusion cascade, three-factor plasticity, and homeostasis."""

import numpy as np
from scipy.linalg import expm


class DiffusiveCascade:
    """C_i du_i/dt = g_(i-1)(u_(i-1)-u_i)+g_i(u_(i+1)-u_i)-lambda_i*u_i+I_i.

    C_i=b**i, g_i=kappa*b**(-i), reflecting endpoints and configurable leak.
    This declared diffusion model is not the absent conversation's exact equations.
    """

    def __init__(self, synapses=1, reservoirs=4, b=2.0, kappa=1.0, leak=0.001):
        if min(synapses, reservoirs) < 1 or b <= 1 or kappa < 0 or leak < 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid cascade")
        self.capacity = b ** np.arange(reservoirs)
        self.state = np.zeros((synapses, reservoirs))
        self.matrix = np.diag(-np.full(reservoirs, leak) / self.capacity)
        for i in range(reservoirs - 1):
            g = kappa * b ** (-i)
            self.matrix[i, i] -= g / self.capacity[i]
            self.matrix[i, i + 1] += g / self.capacity[i]
            self.matrix[i + 1, i] += g / self.capacity[i + 1]
            self.matrix[i + 1, i + 1] -= g / self.capacity[i + 1]

    def step(self, drive=0.0, dt=0.1, method="rk4"):
        if dt <= 0:
            raise ValueError("dt must be positive")
        force = np.zeros_like(self.state)
        force[:, 0] = drive
        f = lambda u: u @ self.matrix.T + force
        if method == "euler":
            if dt * max(abs(np.linalg.eigvals(self.matrix))) > 1:
                raise ValueError("unstable Euler step")
            self.state += dt * f(self.state)
        elif method == "rk4":
            if dt * max(abs(np.linalg.eigvals(self.matrix))) > 2.5:
                raise ValueError("unstable RK4 step")
            k1 = f(self.state)
            k2 = f(self.state + dt * k1 / 2)
            k3 = f(self.state + dt * k2 / 2)
            k4 = f(self.state + dt * k3)
            self.state += dt * (k1 + 2 * k2 + 2 * k3 + k4) / 6
        else:
            raise ValueError("method must be euler or rk4")
        return self.state[:, 0].copy()

    def analytic(self, t):
        return self.state @ expm(t * self.matrix).T

    def expressed(self, beta=None):
        return self.state @ (
            np.eye(1, self.state.shape[1])[0] if beta is None else np.asarray(beta)
        )

    def consolidate(self, amount):
        """Conserve capacity-weighted mass while moving fast state to slow."""
        fraction = float(np.clip(amount, 0, 1))
        if self.state.shape[1] > 1:
            mass = self.state[:, 0] * fraction * self.capacity[0]
            self.state[:, 0] -= mass / self.capacity[0]
            self.state[:, -1] += mass / self.capacity[-1]


class ThreeFactorRule:
    """Spike STDP eligibility: pre_trace*post - post_trace*pre, gated by M(t)."""

    def __init__(self, pre, post, tau=20.0):
        self.pre = np.zeros(pre)
        self.post = np.zeros(post)
        self.tau = tau

    def step(self, pre, post, modulator, dt=1.0, lr=0.001):
        self.pre *= np.exp(-dt / self.tau)
        self.post *= np.exp(-dt / self.tau)
        eligibility = np.outer(self.pre, post) - np.outer(pre, self.post)
        self.pre += pre
        self.post += post
        return lr * modulator * eligibility


def oja(weights, pre, post, lr=0.001, modulator=1.0):
    return weights + lr * modulator * (np.outer(pre, post) - weights * np.square(post))


def homeostatic_scale(weights, rates, target=5.0, eta=0.01):
    return weights * np.exp(np.clip(eta * (target - np.asarray(rates)), -0.1, 0.1))


def prune(weights, rigidity, threshold=0.001):
    result = np.array(weights, copy=True)
    result[(abs(result) < threshold) & (np.asarray(rigidity) < threshold)] = 0
    return result


class NativeDiffusiveCascade(DiffusiveCascade):
    """Same declared equations, contiguous Rust/Rayon RK4 implementation."""

    def step(self, drive=0.0, dt=0.1, method="rk4"):
        if method != "rk4":
            return super().step(drive, dt, method)
        if dt <= 0 or dt * max(abs(np.linalg.eigvals(self.matrix))) > 2.5:
            raise ValueError("unstable RK4 step")
        from nova.core import require_native

        force = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(drive, dtype=float), (len(self.state),))
        result = require_native().cascade_rk4(
            self.state.ravel().tolist(),
            self.matrix.ravel().tolist(),
            force.tolist(),
            self.state.shape[1],
            dt,
        )
        self.state = np.asarray(result).reshape(self.state.shape)
        return self.state[:, 0].copy()


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/training.py
========================================================================================
"""Offline world training with explicit PyTorch or NumPy/local-equilibrium backends."""

from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from nova.environments import point_transitions
from nova.checkpoint import save_state, load_state


def train_world(output, steps=900, seed=0, *, backend="torch", train_encoder=False):
    if steps < 1:
        raise ValueError("steps must be positive")
    if backend == "torch":
        return _train_torch(output, steps, seed)
    from nova.jepa.equilibrium import EquilibriumWorldModel
    from nova.brain.latent import SpikingWorldModel

    if backend not in ("equilibrium", "adex"):
        raise ValueError("unknown training backend")
    if backend == "adex" and train_encoder:
        raise ValueError(
            "AdEx encoder uses explicit STDP rather than rate-network EqProp"
        )
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x, a, y, _ = point_transitions(seed=seed)
    model_class = SpikingWorldModel if backend == "adex" else EquilibriumWorldModel
    model = model_class(12, 8, 2, seed=seed)
    model.fit_normalizer(x)
    successful = 0
    last = {}
    for _ in range(steps):
        idx = rng.integers(len(x), size=32)
        last = model.learn_transition(
            x[idx], a[idx], y[idx], lr=0.01, train_encoder=train_encoder
        )
        successful += int(last["updated"])
    model.freeze_encoder()
    save_state(
        output, {"kind": "nova-world", "backend": backend, "state": model.state_dict()}
    )
    # Training summary only; scientific acceptance belongs to the explicit held-out runner.
    return {
        "backend": backend,
        "steps": steps,
        "updates": successful,
        "last_training_loss": last.get("loss"),
        "seed": seed,
        "checkpoint": str(output),
        "accepted": None,
        "evaluation": "not performed by training command",
    }


def _train_torch(output, steps, seed):
    import torch
    from nova.jepa import JEPA

    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    torch.set_num_threads(2)
    x, a, y, _ = map(torch.from_numpy, point_transitions(seed=seed))
    model = JEPA(12, 8, 2)
    opt = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=0.002)
    for _ in range(steps):
        idx = torch.randint(len(x), (128,))
        loss, metrics = model.loss(x[idx], a[idx], y[idx])
        opt.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()
        opt.step()
        model.target.update(model.context)
    # Preserve the established training command's report protocol for this baseline.
    tx, ta, ty, _ = map(torch.from_numpy, point_transitions(1024, seed + 1000))
    with torch.no_grad():
        target = model.target(ty)
        mse = (model.predictor(model.context(tx), ta) - target).square().mean().item()
        baseline = (target - model.target(y).mean(0)).square().mean().item()
        std = model.assert_noncollapsed(tx)
    path = Path(output)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open("wb") as stream:
        np.savez_compressed(
            stream, **{k: v.detach().numpy() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}
        )
    return {
        "backend": "torch",
        "latent_mse": mse,
        "mean_baseline_mse": baseline,
        "latent_std": std,
        "steps": steps,
        "seed": seed,
        "accepted": mse < baseline,
    }


def load_world(runtime, path):
    if runtime.tick_index or runtime.continual.U.shape[1]:
        raise ValueError(
            "load encoder before online learning; changing coordinates invalidates protection"
        )
    if runtime.config.world_backend == "torch":
        import torch

        with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as arrays:
            runtime.world.load_state_dict(
                {
                    k: torch.from_numpy(arrays[k].copy())
                    for k in runtime.world.state_dict()
                }
            )
        runtime.world.context.requires_grad_(False)
        runtime.world.target.requires_grad_(False)
    else:
        state = load_state(path)
        if (
            state.get("kind") != "nova-world"
            or state["backend"] != runtime.config.world_backend
        ):
            raise ValueError("world checkpoint/backend mismatch")
        from nova.jepa.equilibrium import EquilibriumWorldModel
        from nova.brain.latent import SpikingWorldModel

        cls = SpikingWorldModel if state["backend"] == "adex" else EquilibriumWorldModel
        model = cls.from_state_dict(state["state"])
        if (model.observation_dim, model.latent_dim, model.action_dim) != (
            runtime.config.observation_dim,
            runtime.config.latent_dim,
            runtime.config.action_dim,
        ):
            raise ValueError("world checkpoint dimensions do not match runtime")
        model.freeze_encoder()
        runtime.world = model


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/values/__init__.py
========================================================================================
"""Functional resource/affect state and hard output constraints outside learning."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
import numpy as np


SIMULATION_LABEL = "Simulação baseada em dados — não é a pessoa real."


@dataclass
class InternalState:
    energy: float = 1.0
    memory_load: float = 0.0
    curiosity: float = 0.0
    coherence: float = 1.0
    engagement: float = 0.0
    valence: float = 0.0
    arousal: float = 0.0
    control: float = 1.0
    previous_error: float = 1.0

    def update(
        self, error, compute_fraction, memory_load, value_delta=0.0, engagement=0.0
    ):
        self.energy = float(np.clip(self.energy + 0.01 - 0.02 * compute_fraction, 0, 1))
        self.memory_load = float(np.clip(memory_load, 0, 1))
        # Legacy one-step heuristic. Runtime overrides it with windowed LearningProgress.
        self.curiosity = float(np.clip(self.previous_error - error, 0, 1))
        self.previous_error = float(error)
        self.valence = float(np.tanh(value_delta - error))
        self.arousal = float(np.clip(error, 0, 1))
        self.control = float(np.clip(1 - error, 0, 1))
        self.engagement = float(np.clip(engagement, 0, 1))
        return self

    def sleep(self):
        self.energy = min(1.0, self.energy + 0.2)


class OutputPolicy:
    """A deterministic gate, not a trainable reward or a mutable network weight."""

    @staticmethod
    def render_claims(claims, *, identity_mode=False, consented=False):
        if identity_mode and not consented:
            raise PermissionError("identity consent required")
        lines = []
        for claim in claims:
            if claim.get("third_party_private"):
                raise PermissionError("third-party data is not releasable")
            if not claim.get("source"):
                raise ValueError("factual claim requires source")
            prefix = "Inferência: " if claim.get("inference") else ""
            lines.append(f"{prefix}{claim['text']} (fonte: {claim['source']})")
        answer = (
            "\n".join(lines)
            if lines
            else "Não sei; preciso de uma fonte para responder."
        )
        return f"{SIMULATION_LABEL}\n{answer}" if identity_mode else answer


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/values/progress.py
========================================================================================
"""Confidence-adjusted learning progress; raw surprise is not intrinsic reward."""

from collections import deque
import numpy as np


class LearningProgress:
    """Compare disjoint old/recent error windows, penalizing sampling uncertainty.

    A positive-part one-step error difference has positive expectation under IID
    noise. Windowing and a confidence margin reduce that bias, but do not prove
    zero reward for every stationary process. Inputs should be held-out errors.
    """

    def __init__(self, window=32, confidence=2.0, reward_cap=1.0):
        if window < 2 or confidence < 0 or reward_cap < 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid progress estimator")
        self.window, self.confidence, self.reward_cap = window, confidence, reward_cap
        self.errors = deque(maxlen=2 * window)
        self.reward = 0.0
        self.standard_error = 0.0

    def update(self, error):
        if not np.isfinite(error) or error < 0:
            raise ValueError("prediction error must be finite and nonnegative")
        self.errors.append(float(error))
        self.reward = 0.0
        if len(self.errors) == 2 * self.window:
            old, recent = (
                np.asarray(self.errors)[: self.window],
                np.asarray(self.errors)[self.window :],
            )
            self.standard_error = float(
                np.sqrt(
                    old.var(ddof=1) / self.window + recent.var(ddof=1) / self.window
                )
            )
            self.reward = float(
                np.clip(
                    old.mean() - recent.mean() - self.confidence * self.standard_error,
                    0,
                    self.reward_cap,
                )
            )
        return self.reward

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "window": self.window,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "reward_cap": self.reward_cap,
            "errors": list(self.errors),
            "reward": self.reward,
            "standard_error": self.standard_error,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state):
        obj = cls(state["window"], state["confidence"], state["reward_cap"])
        obj.errors.extend(state["errors"])
        obj.reward = state["reward"]
        obj.standard_error = state["standard_error"]
        return obj

========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/visuals/renderer.py
========================================================================================
"""Bounded volume rendering; appearance is illustrative, not a light solver."""

from __future__ import annotations
import base64
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import zlib
import numpy as np


def write_png(path, rgb):
    pixels = np.asarray(rgb, dtype=np.uint8)
    if pixels.ndim != 3 or pixels.shape[2] != 3:
        raise ValueError("RGB image required")
    h, w, _ = pixels.shape

    def chunk(kind, data):
        return (
            struct.pack(">I", len(data))
            + kind
            + data
            + struct.pack(">I", zlib.crc32(kind + data) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
        )

    raw = b"".join(b"\0" + row.tobytes() for row in pixels)
    data = (
        b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"
        + chunk(b"IHDR", struct.pack(">IIBBBBB", w, h, 8, 2, 0, 0, 0))
        + chunk(b"IDAT", zlib.compress(raw))
        + chunk(b"IEND", b"")
    )
    target = Path(path)
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target.write_bytes(data)


class FieldRenderer:
    @staticmethod
    def density(ecosystem):
        world = ecosystem.world
        mode = world.config.mode
        if mode == "conway":
            field = world.life.astype(float)
        elif mode == "wave":
            field = np.clip(np.abs(world.wave), 0, 1)
        elif mode == "nca":
            field = np.clip(world.channels[0], 0, 1)
        elif mode == "particles":
            field = np.zeros(world.shape)
            coords = np.floor(world.particle_positions).astype(int) % np.asarray(
                world.shape
            )
            np.add.at(field, tuple(coords.T), 1.0)
            field = np.clip(field, 0, 1)
        else:
            field = np.clip(world.v * 3, 0, 1)
        field = field.copy()
        # Developed occupancy follows the organism's point body; no mesh or
        # resolved soft-body mechanics is implied by the rendered appearance.
        for organism in ecosystem.organisms:
            body = organism.body["density"]
            indices = np.argwhere(body > 0.1)
            if len(indices):
                offsets = (indices / body.shape[0] - 0.5) * 4
                positions = np.floor(
                    organism.position + offsets[:, : world.ndim]
                ).astype(int) % np.asarray(world.shape)
                np.maximum.at(
                    field,
                    tuple(positions.T),
                    np.minimum(1.0, body[tuple(indices.T)] + 0.25),
                )
        if field.ndim == 2:
            field = np.repeat(field[None], 4, axis=0)
        return np.ascontiguousarray(field, dtype=float)

    @classmethod
    def frame(cls, ecosystem):
        density = cls.density(ecosystem)
        image = np.rint(density * 255).astype(np.uint8)
        topology = (
            ecosystem.organisms[0].genome.develop_brain()
            if ecosystem.organisms
            else None
        )
        return {
            "shape": list(image.shape),
            "volume": base64.b64encode(image.tobytes()).decode(),
            "metrics": ecosystem.metrics(),
            "mode": ecosystem.world.config.mode,
            "architecture": None
            if topology is None
            else {
                "coordinates": topology["coordinates"].tolist(),
                "types": topology["types"].tolist(),
                "families": topology["families"].tolist(),
                "edges": [
                    [int(i), int(j), float(topology["weights"][i, j])]
                    for i, j in zip(*np.nonzero(topology["weights"]))
                ],
            },
        }

    @classmethod
    def render(cls, ecosystem, output, *, kind="volume", resolution=384):
        if not 32 <= resolution <= 1024:
            raise ValueError("image resolution out of bounds")
        density = cls.density(ecosystem)
        if kind == "slice":
            scalar = density[len(density) // 2]
            rgb = np.stack((scalar, np.sqrt(scalar) * 0.7, 1 - scalar), -1)
        elif kind == "volume":
            h, w = density.shape[1:]
            transmission = np.ones((h, w))
            rgb = np.zeros((h, w, 3))
            gradient = np.gradient(density)
            normal = np.sqrt(sum(g * g for g in gradient)) + 1e-8
            for z in range(len(density)):
                d = density[z]
                alpha = 1 - np.exp(-d * 6 / len(density))
                light = 0.25 + 0.75 * np.abs(gradient[0][z]) / normal[z]
                color = (
                    np.stack((0.15 + 0.8 * d, 0.35 + 0.6 * d, 0.8 - 0.35 * d), -1)
                    * light[..., None]
                )
                rgb += transmission[..., None] * alpha[..., None] * color
                transmission *= 1 - alpha
            rgb += transmission[..., None] * np.array([0.015, 0.025, 0.06])
        else:
            raise ValueError("unknown renderer")
        y = np.linspace(0, rgb.shape[0] - 1, resolution).astype(int)
        x = np.linspace(0, rgb.shape[1] - 1, resolution).astype(int)
        write_png(
            output,
            np.rint(np.clip(rgb[y[:, None], x[None, :]], 0, 1) * 255).astype(np.uint8),
        )
        return {"image": str(output), "renderer": kind, "physical_radiometry": False}

    @classmethod
    def export_html(cls, ecosystem, path):
        template = Path(__file__).with_name("viewer.html").read_text()
        frame = json.dumps(cls.frame(ecosystem), allow_nan=False).replace(
            "<", "\\u003c"
        )
        html = template.replace("/*INITIAL_FRAME*/null", frame).replace(
            "/*LIVE_CONFIG*/null", "null"
        )
        target = Path(path)
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        target.write_text(html)
        return {"viewer": str(target), "offline": True}


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/visuals/server.py
========================================================================================
"""Loopback-only interactive viewer. Simulation mutations require a session token."""

from __future__ import annotations
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path
import secrets
import threading
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, strict_json
from .renderer import FieldRenderer


def serve(ecosystem, *, port=8765):
    from nova.bioma.presets import create_ecosystem

    token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    lock = threading.Lock()
    state = {"ecosystem": ecosystem}

    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        def log_message(self, *args):
            pass

        def reply(self, status, data, kind="application/json"):
            self.send_response(status)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", kind)
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            self.send_header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(data)

        def allowed(self):
            return self.headers.get("Host") in (
                f"127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}",
                f"localhost:{self.server.server_port}",
            )

        def do_GET(self):
            if not self.allowed():
                return self.reply(403, b"{}")
            if self.path != "/":
                return self.reply(404, b"{}")
            with lock:
                frame = FieldRenderer.frame(state["ecosystem"])
            html = (
                Path(__file__)
                .with_name("viewer.html")
                .read_text()
                .replace("/*INITIAL_FRAME*/null", canonical_json(frame).decode())
                .replace(
                    "/*LIVE_CONFIG*/null", canonical_json({"token": token}).decode()
                )
            )
            self.reply(200, html.encode(), "text/html; charset=utf-8")

        def do_POST(self):
            if not self.allowed() or not secrets.compare_digest(
                self.headers.get("X-Nova-Session", ""), token
            ):
                return self.reply(403, b"{}")
            try:
                length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))
                if not 0 < length <= 4096:
                    raise ValueError("request body size out of range")
                request = strict_json(self.rfile.read(length), 4096)
                with lock:
                    if self.path == "/step":
                        ticks = request.get("ticks", 1)
                        if not isinstance(ticks, int) or not 1 <= ticks <= 8:
                            raise ValueError("tick limit exceeded")
                        state["ecosystem"].step(ticks)
                    elif self.path == "/reset":
                        old = state["ecosystem"]
                        state["ecosystem"] = create_ecosystem(
                            request["preset"],
                            seed=old.world.config.seed,
                            size=old.world.shape[0],
                        )
                    else:
                        return self.reply(404, b"{}")
                    frame = FieldRenderer.frame(state["ecosystem"])
                self.reply(200, canonical_json(frame))
            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as error:
                self.reply(400, canonical_json({"error": str(error)}))

    class Server(ThreadingHTTPServer):
        daemon_threads = True

        def get_request(self):
            connection, address = super().get_request()
            connection.settimeout(10)
            return connection, address

    server = Server(("127.0.0.1", port), Handler)
    print(
        f"NOVA Biocosm: http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port} (Ctrl-C para encerrar)",
        flush=True,
    )
    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass
    finally:
        server.server_close()

# Estado da entrega

**Atualização 0.2:** o [estado da extensão adaptativa](adaptive/STATUS.md) e o [projeto técnico](../docs/adaptive-energy.md) descrevem memória adaptativa, inferência por equilíbrio/AdEx e planejamento por energia. Não foram executados novos testes ou benchmarks nesta revisão. As medições abaixo são históricas da primeira entrega e não validam as extensões.

Código experimental para as nove fases, com relatórios e resultados de cinco sementes por experimento. Implementação não equivale à aprovação científica integral da especificação.

| Fase | Resultado observado | Aceitação |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Retenção média ~1,4e-13; violações e saturação demonstradas | Critério linear atendido nas condições medidas |
| 2 | MSE latente 0,198 vs média 0,517; std 0,734 | Atendido no ambiente linear com distratores; demais domínios pendentes |
| 3 | Proteção exata satura e preserva memória, mas perde plasticidade | Não atende competitividade com GPM em Split-MNIST; dados reduzidos |
| 4 | Recuperação de padrões e planejamento funcionais | Ablações não estabelecem ganho geral de cada módulo |
| 5 | Pesos compactados em 2 bits; latência CPU desfavorável | Resultado negativo de velocidade; GPU, energia e bitnet.cpp não medidos |
| 6 | EqProp alinhado ao gradiente; sincronização e difusão verificadas | Benefício em tarefa cognitiva não demonstrado; opt-in |
| 7 | AdEx, cascata e encoders operacionais; espectro medido | Validação biológica não realizada; pico de microcircuito ~24,4 Hz |
| 8 | Busca melhora com orçamento; ECE de ~0,216 para ~0,024 em dados sintéticos | Validação limitada aos domínios de bancada |
| 9 | Fontes, rótulos, rejeição sem fonte e apagamento verificados | Perfis sintéticos; fidelidade humana/linguística não avaliada |

Antes da instrução posterior de dispensar novos testes, a suíte integrada registrou **40 aprovados e 1 ignorado por ausência de CUDA**, e os testes Rust registraram **2 aprovados**. Não foram iniciados novos testes após essa instrução. Os logs anteriores foram mantidos. Alterações finais de documentação, empacotamento, corpo 3D e acesso exclusivo para apagamento após revogação não receberam uma nova rodada de testes, conforme solicitado.

O JEPA treinado para o demonstrador produziu MSE 0,179 vs média 0,523, com std 0,738 (`trained-world.json`). O exemplo executado e o painel offline ficam em `demo-result.json`, `demo.jsonl` e `dashboard.html`. Checkpoints e bibliotecas compiladas permanecem em caminhos ignorados pelo Git.

Pendências externas: acesso a `www.cs.toronto.edu` para CIFAR-100; GPU/NVML e instalação de bitnet.cpp para custo; dados biológicos com referências/tolerâncias; modelos locais opcionais para OCR/ASR/LLM; dados pessoais apenas mediante consentimento válido. Esses itens não são substituídos por números simulados.


## Revisão Biocosm 0.3

Novos componentes e limites em [biocosm/STATUS.md](biocosm/STATUS.md). Implementação experimental, sem testes/benchmarks/simulações novos executados. Resultados históricos das fases não validam os módulos desta revisão.

# Extensão adaptativa — estado de implementação

Implementados os três eixos solicitados: capacidade por decaimento/liberação e expansão limitada; inferência latente por EqProp não linear ou circuito AdEx efetivo; planejamento por energia com feixe temporal.

Também adicionados: versões de características e âncoras, política de readout sem autograd, recompensa de progresso com margem de incerteza, objetivos/limites de estado configuráveis, limites de histórico/grafo, checkpoint v2 atômico com integridade, retomada do solver, configurações YAML e carregamento tardio de PyTorch.

**Validação desta revisão: não executada**, conforme a preferência anterior do usuário. Foram escritos testes e os experimentos 10–12 para reprodução posterior. A revisão de código, formatação e análise estática não equivalem a executar os modelos ou demonstrar ganhos.

Os resultados das fases 1–9 pertencem à entrega anterior. Nenhuma melhora de acurácia, retenção, energia, escala ou tempo real é afirmada para esta extensão sem medições novas.

Limites: recursos finitos; liberação permite esquecer; versões de entrada são parte da garantia; rede de taxa simétrica e AdEx são hipóteses distintas; o planejador pode parar em mínimos locais; viabilidade é avaliada no modelo; parada nula não é um certificado de segurança física.

# Estado da revisão 0.3 — Biocosm

Código experimental implementado para campos 2D/3D, organismos CPPN/GRN com grafos neurais mistos, busca evolutiva, renderização WebGL2/CPU, descoberta polinomial, verificadores com escopo, datasets com proveniência, HoloSeed e troca P2P explícita.

**Nenhum teste, benchmark ou nova simulação foi executado**, conforme orientação do usuário. As verificações desta revisão se restringem a análise estática de nomes/sintaxe pelo Ruff, formatação e integridade textual do diff. Os resultados históricos v0.1 não validam esses componentes.

- Trilha inteira especificada: implementação disponível, portabilidade ainda não medida.
- Reprodução float64: comparação explícita de hash; igualdade entre plataformas não presumida.
- Ciência: hipóteses empíricas e identidades dos modelos separadas; nenhuma descoberta física nova reivindicada.
- Render: implementação 3D disponível; execução em navegador/FPS não medidos.
- Rede: TCP/TLS e WebSockets opcionais com pares fixados; nenhuma rede planetária lançada ou ensaio distribuído realizado.
- Morfogênese: gramática finita, mecânica pontual, biofísica simplificada; nenhuma alegação de vida/conscência ou milhões de células eficientes.

Consulte [o desenho, comandos e limites](../../docs/biocosm.md).

ARQUIVO: scripts/bundle_source.py
========================================================================================
"""Create a deterministic, human-readable source bundle with per-file SHA-256."""

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DIRECTORIES = (
    "nova",
    "nova-core/src",
    "experiments",
    "tests",
    "scripts",
    "configs",
    ".github",
)
FILES = (
    "pyproject.toml",
    "requirements-cpu.txt",
    "nova-core/Cargo.toml",
    "nova-core/pyproject.toml",
    "nova-core/Cargo.lock",
    ".gitignore",
    "README.md",
    "DEVIATIONS.md",
    "docs/identity.md",
    "docs/adaptive-energy.md",
    "docs/biocosm.md",
    "reports/biocosm/STATUS.md",
    "reports/biocosm/status.json",
    "reports/adaptive/STATUS.md",
    "reports/adaptive/status.json",
    "reports/STATUS.md",
)
EXTENSIONS = {".py", ".rs", ".sh", ".yaml", ".yml", ".toml", ".json", ".html"}


def main():
    paths = {ROOT / path for path in FILES}
    for directory in DIRECTORIES:
        paths.update(
            p
            for p in (ROOT / directory).rglob("*")
            if p.is_file() and p.suffix in EXTENSIONS and "__pycache__" not in p.parts
        )
    ordered = sorted(paths, key=lambda p: p.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix())
    chunks = [
        "NOVA — CÓDIGO-FONTE CONSOLIDADO\n\nPacote textual; não é um executável. Binários nativos são gerados por Maturin.\nNão inclui dados, chaves, ambientes virtuais, dependências baixadas ou checkpoints.\n\nÍNDICE\n"
    ]
    for path in ordered:
        content = path.read_bytes()
        name = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
        chunks.append(
            f"{name} | {len(content)} bytes | sha256={hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()}\n"
        )
    for path in ordered:
        name = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
        content = path.read_text()
        chunks.append(f"\n{'=' * 88}\nARQUIVO: {name}\n{'=' * 88}\n{content}\n")
    output = ROOT / "artifacts/NOVA_codigo_completo.txt"
    output.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    output.write_text("".join(chunks), encoding="utf-8")
    print(
        f"{output.relative_to(ROOT)}: {len(ordered)} arquivos, {output.stat().st_size} bytes"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

ARQUIVO: scripts/setup.sh

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
python -m venv --system-site-packages .venv
.venv/bin/python -m pip install --disable-pip-version-check -r requirements-cpu.txt
.venv/bin/python -m pip install --disable-pip-version-check --no-deps -e .
# Rust is optional for Python work. Install through rustup with verified TLS.
if command -v cargo >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  .venv/bin/maturin build --manifest-path nova-core/Cargo.toml --release --locked -o .cache/wheels
  .venv/bin/python -m pip install --force-reinstall .cache/wheels/*.whl
elif [ -x .tools/cargo/bin/cargo ]; then
  export CARGO_HOME="$PWD/.tools/cargo"
  export RUSTUP_HOME="$PWD/.tools/rustup"
  export PATH="$CARGO_HOME/bin:$PATH"
  .venv/bin/maturin build --manifest-path nova-core/Cargo.toml --release --locked -o .cache/wheels
  .venv/bin/python -m pip install --force-reinstall .cache/wheels/*.whl
else
  printf '%s\n' 'Rust unavailable: Python installed; install Rust >=1.85 and rerun to build native kernels.'
fi

ARQUIVO: tests/biocosm/test_science_swarm.py

"""Evidence boundaries, transport admission and compatibility checks."""

import asyncio
import numpy as np
import pytest
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json
from nova.scientific.polynomial import Polynomial
from nova.scientific.verification import PolynomialVerifier
from nova.scientific.symbolic_regression import SymbolicRegressor
from nova.swarm.artifacts import ArtifactStore, SigningIdentity, ScientificLedger
from nova.swarm.p2p_node import Peer, PeerNode
from nova.swarm.subspace_exchange import package_subspace, merge_subspaces
from nova.swarm.sheaf_consensus import SheafGossip


def test_exact_continuous_oscillator_invariant_and_counterexample():
    p = lambda text: Polynomial.parse(text, ["x", "v"])
    verifier = PolynomialVerifier()
    assert verifier.invariant(p("x*x+v*v"), [p("v"), p("-x")]).status == "proved"
    assert verifier.invariant(p("x*x"), [p("v"), p("-x")]).status == "refuted"


def test_polynomial_parser_never_accepts_calls():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        Polynomial.parse('__import__("os")', ["x"])


def test_sparse_fit_has_heldout_evidence_without_proof_label():
    x = np.linspace(-1, 1, 80)[:, None]
    candidate = SymbolicRegressor(degree=2, max_terms=3).fit(
        x, 2 * x[:, 0] + x[:, 0] ** 2, ["x"]
    )
    assert candidate.metrics["heldout_mse"] < 1e-10
    assert candidate.to_dict()["status"] == "empirical_candidate"


def test_signed_blob_tamper_and_ledger_head(tmp_path):
    identity = SigningIdentity.load_or_create(tmp_path / "key")
    packet = identity.sign({"a": 1})
    packet["payload"]["a"] = 2
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        SigningIdentity.verify(packet)
    ledger = ScientificLedger(tmp_path / "ledger.sqlite", identity)
    try:
        head = ledger.append("discovery", {"status": "candidate"})
        assert ledger.verify(head)["entries"] == 1
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            ledger.verify("0" * 64)
    finally:
        ledger.close()


def test_subspace_union_requires_same_encoder_and_respects_capacity():
    local = np.eye(4)[:, :1]
    packet = package_subspace(np.eye(4)[:, 1:2], "shared")
    assert merge_subspaces(local, packet, "shared", 1)["status"] == "capacity_rejected"
    assert merge_subspaces(local, packet, "shared", 2)["rank"] == 2
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        merge_subspaces(local, packet, "other", 4)


def test_sheaf_consistency_is_not_proof():
    gossip = SheafGossip(1, "model", initial=[0.0])
    assert not gossip.accept(
        "peer", {"model_id": "other", "value": [1.0], "sequence": 0}
    )
    packet = {"model_id": "model", "value": [1.0], "sequence": 0}
    assert gossip.accept("peer", packet)
    assert not gossip.accept("peer", packet)
    assert gossip.step()["value"][0] > 0


def test_pinned_rpc_rejects_replayed_signed_request(tmp_path):
    a = SigningIdentity.load_or_create(tmp_path / "a")
    b = SigningIdentity.load_or_create(tmp_path / "b")
    client = PeerNode(
        a, ArtifactStore(tmp_path / "ac"), [Peer(b.peer_id, "tcp://127.0.0.1:9001")]
    )
    server = PeerNode(
        b, ArtifactStore(tmp_path / "bc"), [Peer(a.peer_id, "tcp://127.0.0.1:9002")]
    )
    request = canonical_json(client.message("inventory", {}))
    reply = asyncio.run(server.handle(request))
    assert reply
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        asyncio.run(server.handle(request))


def test_plaintext_remote_peers_are_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        Peer("a" * 64, "tcp://example.com:9000")

ARQUIVO: tests/biocosm/test_substrates.py

"""Future validation cases; authored but not executed for the v0.3 delivery."""

import numpy as np
import pytest
from nova.bioma.determinism import SplitMix64, digest_state
from nova.bioma.physics_engine import PhysicsConfig, PhysicsEngine
from nova.bioma.ecosystem import Ecosystem, EcosystemConfig
from nova.bioma.genetics import MorphogeneticCPPN
from nova.swarm.holoseed import HoloSeed


def test_specified_splitmix_vector():
    assert SplitMix64(0).next_u64() == 0xE220A8397B1DCDAF


def test_periodic_laplacian_has_zero_integral():
    world = PhysicsEngine(PhysicsConfig(shape=(8, 8, 8)))
    assert abs(world.laplacian(world.v).sum()) < 1e-12


def test_chemical_stoichiometry_simultaneous_update():
    world = PhysicsEngine(
        PhysicsConfig(shape=(8, 8), feed=0, kill=0, diffusion_u=0, diffusion_v=0)
    )
    before = (world.u + world.v).copy()
    world.step()
    np.testing.assert_allclose(world.u + world.v, before, atol=1e-15)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["conway", "fixed_gray_scott"])
def test_integer_replay_binds_exact_clock_and_state(mode):
    eco = Ecosystem(
        PhysicsConfig(mode=mode, shape=(8, 8)), EcosystemConfig(initial_population=0)
    )
    eco.step(3)
    packet = HoloSeed.pack(eco, tier="strict_integer")
    _, receipt = HoloSeed.reproduce(packet)
    assert receipt["status"] == "matched"
    packet["payload"]["expected_state_sha256"] = "0" * 64
    assert HoloSeed.reproduce(packet)[1]["status"] == "mismatch"


def test_hash_covers_nested_learning_state():
    state = {"organism": {"weights": np.eye(3), "adaptation": np.zeros(3)}}
    original = digest_state(state)
    state["organism"]["adaptation"][0] = 1
    assert digest_state(state) != original


def test_checkpoint_restores_full_ecosystem_hash():
    eco = Ecosystem(PhysicsConfig(shape=(8, 8)), EcosystemConfig(initial_population=1))
    eco.step(2)
    restored = Ecosystem.from_state_dict(eco.state_dict())
    assert restored.state_hash() == eco.state_hash()
    eco.step()
    restored.step()
    assert restored.state_hash() == eco.state_hash()


def test_cppn_development_is_repeatable_and_dale_compliant():
    genome = MorphogeneticCPPN(42)
    a = genome.develop_brain()
    b = genome.develop_brain()
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(a["weights"], b["weights"])
    assert np.all(a["weights"][a["types"] == 0] >= 0)
    assert np.all(a["weights"][a["types"] != 0] <= 0)
    assert np.count_nonzero(a["families"] == 2) <= genome.config.max_n2


def test_pair_queries_distinguish_equal_midpoints():
    genome = MorphogeneticCPPN(42)
    assert not np.array_equal(
        genome.query_pair([1, 0, 0], [-1, 0, 0]),
        genome.query_pair([0, 1, 0], [0, -1, 0]),
    )


def test_custom_genesis_replays_its_actual_genome():
    genome = MorphogeneticCPPN(19).mutate(29, 0.5)
    eco = Ecosystem(
        PhysicsConfig(shape=(8, 8)),
        EcosystemConfig(initial_population=1),
        genesis_genomes=[genome.state_dict()],
    )
    eco.step(2)
    restored, receipt = HoloSeed.reproduce(HoloSeed.pack(eco))
    assert receipt["status"] == "matched"
    assert restored.organisms[0].genome.hash() == genome.hash()

ARQUIVO: tests/test_adaptive_checkpoint.py
========================================================================================
"""Unrun deterministic restoration and archive-integrity specifications."""

import numpy as np
from nova.runtime import CognitiveRuntime, RuntimeConfig
from nova.checkpoint import save_state, load_state


def test_numpy_runtime_checkpoint(tmp_path):
    config = RuntimeConfig(
        world_backend="equilibrium",
        adaptive_memory=True,
        sleep_every=2,
        world_online_learning=True,
    )
    a = CognitiveRuntime(config)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(2)
    for _ in range(4):
        a.tick(rng.normal(size=12))
    a.save(tmp_path / "state.npz")
    b = CognitiveRuntime.load(tmp_path / "state.npz")
    observation = rng.normal(size=12)
    x, y = a.tick(observation), b.tick(observation)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(x["action"], y["action"])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(a.continual.dW, b.continual.dW)


def test_codec_roundtrip_without_pickle(tmp_path):
    state = {"array": np.arange(6).reshape(2, 3), "nested": [None, True, {"x": 1.0}]}
    save_state(tmp_path / "data.npz", state)
    restored = load_state(tmp_path / "data.npz")
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(state["array"], restored["array"])
    assert state["nested"] == restored["nested"]


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: tests/test_adaptive_subspace.py
========================================================================================
"""Regression specifications, authored but not executed in this revision."""

import numpy as np
from nova.continual import AdaptiveSubspaceLinear, AdaptiveConfig


def test_growth_keeps_old_version_coordinates_and_anchor():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
    m = AdaptiveSubspaceLinear(
        6, 2, config=AdaptiveConfig(policy="hybrid", max_features=12, growth_step=3)
    )
    x = rng.normal(size=(20, 6))
    y = rng.normal(size=(20, 2))
    for _ in range(20):
        m.step(m.encode(x, 0), y, 0.01)
    m.consolidate(m.encode(x, 0), anchor=True)
    reference = m(m.encode(x, 0))
    assert m.recruit() == 3
    assert np.count_nonzero(m.encode(x, 0)[:, 6:]) == 0
    assert np.count_nonzero(m.encode(x, 1)[:, 6:]) > 0
    for _ in range(50):
        m.step(m.encode(rng.normal(size=(20, 6)), 1), y, 0.01)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(m(m.encode(x, 0)), reference, atol=1e-10)
    assert m.release(20) == 0


def test_relaxation_frees_space_without_infinite_capacity_claim():
    m = AdaptiveSubspaceLinear(
        4, 1, config=AdaptiveConfig(policy="relax", max_features=4)
    )
    m.consolidate(np.eye(4))
    assert m.free_dims == 0
    assert m.release(2) == 2 and m.free_dims == 2
    assert m.recruit() == 0
    restored = AdaptiveSubspaceLinear.from_state_dict(m.state_dict())
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(m.U, restored.U)


def test_strict_policy_does_not_release_and_growth_is_bounded():
    strict = AdaptiveSubspaceLinear(
        3, 1, config=AdaptiveConfig(policy="strict", max_features=6)
    )
    strict.consolidate(np.eye(3))
    strict.manage_capacity()
    assert strict.free_dims == 0
    grow = AdaptiveSubspaceLinear(
        3, 1, config=AdaptiveConfig(policy="expand", max_features=5, growth_step=8)
    )
    assert grow.recruit() == 2 and grow.recruit() == 0

ARQUIVO: tests/test_cli.py

import json
from nova.cli import main


def test_cli_demo_and_dashboard(tmp_path, capsys):
    metrics = tmp_path / "metrics.jsonl"
    checkpoint = tmp_path / "demo.npz"
    html = tmp_path / "panel.html"
    main(
        [
            "demo",
            "--ticks",
            "4",
            "--checkpoint",
            str(checkpoint),
            "--metrics",
            str(metrics),
        ]
    )
    assert json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out)["ticks"] == 4
    main(["dashboard", "--metrics", str(metrics), "--output", str(html)])
    assert "latency_ms" in html.read_text()
    assert checkpoint.is_file()


def test_demo_resume_includes_body_state(tmp_path, capsys):
    import numpy as np
    from nova.runtime import CognitiveRuntime

    full = tmp_path / "full.npz"
    part = tmp_path / "part.npz"
    metrics = tmp_path / "metrics.jsonl"
    main(["demo", "--ticks", "8", "--checkpoint", str(full), "--metrics", str(metrics)])
    main(["demo", "--ticks", "4", "--checkpoint", str(part), "--metrics", str(metrics)])
    main(
        [
            "demo",
            "--ticks",
            "4",
            "--resume",
            str(part),
            "--checkpoint",
            str(part),
            "--metrics",
            str(metrics),
        ]
    )
    a, b = CognitiveRuntime.load(full), CognitiveRuntime.load(part)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(a.z, b.z)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(a.continual.dW, b.continual.dW)
    assert a.external_state == b.external_state
